Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ hei (nk.
Akɛ sui hu tsuuu nii jogbaŋŋ.
Amralo lɛ ewa kɛto gbɛjianɔ srɔtoi ni baawa okwaayeli yɛ Ghana bɔ ni afee ni niyenii babaoo aba maŋ lɛ mli.
Tsɛrɛmɔ wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ayɔse kɔnsonanti ni baa nɛɛ eko yɛ mli.
Faa lɛ mlikwɔlɛ shɛɛɛ mitai nyɔŋmai enumɔ shikulɛ masumɔ ni mawu kilomitai saŋ yɛ faa ni mli kwɔ ni ehiɛ hu tse mli.
Ebaawa kɛbaa kusum yi.
Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ hei srɔtoi yɛ wiemɔkuli mli
Yara shishi ji he ko ni mɛi bakpeɔ yɛ, ni etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni amɛyɔɔ, ni no yafeɔ yelikɛbuamɔ nɔ yɛ be ni amɛhiaa amɛhe titri lɛ.
Te abaafee tɛŋŋ akɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai mli nilee atao adafitswaa pɔtɛɛ ko yɛ sane ko mli
Kɛtsɔ onilee ni oyɔɔ yɛ jeŋnilee saji ahe lɛ, sɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lala kɛha nitsumɔ ni obaasumɔ akɛ otsu wɔ sɛɛ lɛ.
Akɛ niŋmaa ŋaabii wulu ni jeɔ shishi.
Sane lɛ mlipɛimɔ (kɛyashi mii 5)
Okɛ naashwɔmɔ kɛ suɔmɔ Abaa amɛyi tamɔ wuɔ kɛ ebii.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ OTSI 6 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ahara otii yɛ niŋmaai amli Kaimɔ Afi1 Otsi 3
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔ jebɔ lɛ nyɛɔ eyeɔ ebuaa yɛ shishinumɔ muu kɛ gbee namɔ ni enaa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ kanelɔi anɔ.
Loo pii ni Ɔmanhene lɛ shaa lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ amɛbaana loo pii afi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ.
Ebaanyɛ efee tsɔne/lɔre oshra, shigbeemɔ ni naa wa kn.kn.
Kwɛmɔ sane lɛ mli jogbaŋŋ koni ohala wiemɔ otii lɛ.
Yɛ maji komɛi amli lɛ anaa yarawoo lalai nɛɛ akɛ faafoo kusum kɛha mɔ ni egbo lɛ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Nikasemɔ He Nitsumɔ
Akɛbaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ nibii ayi ni eka gbɛ.
Tɔmɔi lɛ faa (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) yɛ slaidi lɛ nɔ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ
blawa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi) NIŊMAA HE MLAI
Afeɔ enɛ yɛ Kubri shia lɛ mli.
Mɛni osusuɔ akɛ eji yitso ni yɔɔ kuku ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ mli lɛ?
Eji gbɛ nɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ jieɔ henumɔ ko kpo kɛtsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ mli mfoniri nɔ loo nifeemɔi kɛ nibii krokomɛi tamɔ nikwɛmɔ nɔ.
Wiemɔ, shihilɛ, kusumii srɔtoi, maŋkuramɔ, kɛ gbɔmɛi ayibɔ ni yaa hiɛ lɛ ena lalai lɛ anɔ hewalɛ, ni enɛɛmɛi fɛɛ kɛ amɛhe efutu.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni osusuɔ akɛ Ghana shihilɛ aji aha bo.
Taomɔ nibii enumɔ ni kɛ gbele baa yɛ akutso lɛ mli ni ataoɔ nibii otii ni kɛ nɛkɛ gbele nɛɛ baa kɛ nɔnyɛɛ ni gbele lɛ yɔɔ yɛ akutso lɛ nɔ
Kwɛmɔ Kpokuafoaiŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ he vidio yɛ wiemɔi krokomɛi amli (Dangme, Ashante, Mfantse, Nzema, kn.kn)
Lɛ ji shi tsɛ yɛ ekpokpa lɛ nɔ
Mla ni kulaa najiaŋgbɛi ni akɛfeɔ wiemɔmuu tsakeɔ yɛ Ghana wiemɔi lɛ amli.
Ale Ghana jogbaŋŋ yɛ ekusum nifeemɔ lɛ ahewɔ.
Kanemɔ ni onya lalawiemɔ kuku nɛɛ he ni ohara: Takwa jɛkɛ!
Wiemɔ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ ahe (hiŋmɛi loo waobii tsimɔ, wiemɔkulibii loo sanekukuji anaa, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) Afi 2 Otsi
Nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ ŋaagbɛ he kaa
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Sanenaataomɔ Nikasemɔ: yɛ ŋaagbɛ nɛɛ mli lɛ, akɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli saji toɔ nikasemɔ lɛ he ni no haa kaselɔi nuɔ nibii ashishi faŋŋ fe nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni awieɔ nibii ahe tamɔ bɔ ni amɛji lɛ.
Agba shikwɛɛ afiyeli srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ, akwɛ henɔi lɛ.
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ mli.
Tipɛŋfoi Awiemɔkulibii Nɛkɛ wiemɔ henɔ nɛɛ lɛ, gbɛjianɔtoo ko bɛ he ni mɔ fɛɛ mɔ nyɛɔ ekɛtsuɔ nii ni anaa yɛ daadaa sanegbaai amli.
Bɔni afee ni owie Ga jogbaŋŋ lɛ, bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ okase daa gbi.
Mɛi pɔtɛɛ ni yeɔ: Akan ku gbajaa yeɔ Odwira, shi Fetu Afahyɛ lɛ Oguamɛi ni yɔɔ Cape Coast lɛ pɛ yeɔ.
Afeɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔ nɛɛ yɛ lilɛi lɛ hiɛ, teŋ loo esɛɛ gbɛ.
Ŋaagbɛi tamɔ mɔ kome nikasemɔ, ku nitsumɔ, hii kɛ yei amlijaramɔ loo amɛfutumɔ kɛ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi muu fɛɛ nifeemɔ fɛɛ hɔ sanenaataomɔ nikasemɔ nɛɛ shishi.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ kɛji akɛ kaselɔi tsɔ mlijaa nɛɛ mli, amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna lɛ baanya lalawiemɔ he yɛ Blɔfo wiemɔ mli kɛ Blɔfo jeŋnilee saji ahe.
Gbalamɔ yarawoo lalai amli (enitsumɔ kɛ ehe sɛɛnamɔi)
Kɛ Ga kpokuafoi ahetsuu nifeemɔ lɛ ato kusum krokomɛi anɔ lɛ he.
Ajara fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ awo gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli kɛ blawa shikamɔ mli.
\Afeɔ kusum yɛ Fosu gbatsu lɛ naa, ni naagbee lɛ akɛ lɛji daa yɛ Fosu mukpo lɛ mli kɛji Ɔmanhen ye ŋkpai eta.
Saji haramɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ amligbalamɔ (tamɔ, sanegbaa, mɔnaa wiemɔ lala)
Gbalamɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ asusuɔ kɔnsonantii atsɔɔ lɛ amli .
Nugbɔ miinɛ moŋ nɛ shi okwaafoi lɛ miitsu nii lolo
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Akwɛ nitsumɔ lalai amli wiemɔ (ahara otii, akwɛ jebɔ, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, wiemɔ kɛ ŋaa, kn.kn)
Sanefa Oti: Eji sanefa ni hiɛ feelɔ kɛ helɔ ni jieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu kpo.
Presentation Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ mɔ lɛ nuuu abifao kpojiemɔ nifeemɔ lɛ shishi (abifao kpojiemɔ mligbalamɔ) Wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii yɛ abifao kpojiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ bɛ shifimɔ ko.
Rɔba tsɛnsi lɛ yɛ Adu ŋɔɔ.
Mitee hiɛ mitai fioo lɛ, naa bu voo yɛ nu lɛ mli ekoŋŋ.
Tsɔɔlɔ (tsɔɔ + lɔ)
Bei pii lɛ, vaoli titri ni tsɔɔ nɛkɛ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli shi bei komɛi lɛ, naabu kɔnsonantii komɛi hu nyɛɔ etsɔɔ gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ.
Dikimɔ sanemuu oti lɛ kokloo ni ofo sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ ashishi.
Ehaa sanegbalɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko naa be kɛtsɔɔ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Esani okɔ kɛjɛ nibii ni atsɔɔ kɛjɛ Afi 1 kɛyashi 3.
Edamɔɔ ŋaa fɛɛ ŋaa ni abaawo lɛ nɔ kɛtsakeɔ enifeemɔi kɛ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Yarafeemɔ ji naagbee kusum loo nifeemɔ ni mɛi ni yɔɔ wala mli feɔ kɛwaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ kɛkpalaa lɛ kɛjɛ hiɛkalɔi ateŋ kɛyaa gbohiiajeŋ yɛ toiŋjɔlɛ mli.
N’atsɛ lɛ aha mi ei;
Wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ashishinumɔ lɛ baaha onyɛ onya owiemɔ lɛ shikamɔ kɛ ejebɔ lɛ he.
Asafoiatsɛ Lumɔ nɛɛ hɔ Shipi shishi ni ekɛ lɛ tsuɔ nii
Anyɛɔ ajeɔ shishi kɛ sanebimɔ ni he ehiaaa hetoo.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Reproduction / Sane kaimɔ
Awulaa kɛ nidiŋ kɛ nitsuru, wekui bua amɛhe naa kɛfoɔ mɛi ni etsɔmɔ amɛsɛɛ lɛ.
Yarawoo lala Eji nyanyɔji ashishikpemɔ kɛ awerɛhoo lala ni akɛfoɔ mɛi ni egboi.’ Eji lala ni alaa lɛ blɛoo kɛtsɔɔ dɔlɛ kɛ awerɛhoo, ni alaa yɛ yara kɛ gbele sɔɔmɔ feemɔ nɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi ji;
Mɛi ni kuɔ nɛkɛ mla nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, agbalaa amɛtoi.
Hiɛhiɛ shishinumɔ: Eji klɛŋklɛŋ shishinumɔ ni anaa.
Kaselɔ akane Afoats sane kuku ko, ko ni amɛnya he kɛtsɔ shwɛlɔi lɛ kɛ he nɔ lɛ ba yɛ lɛ nɔ.
Gbaa saneŋmaa Saneŋmaa henɔ ko ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni afeɔ nɔ ko ahaa;
Kaselɔi kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa baatsu nii kɛwie ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe beiaŋ ni amɛkɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) ŋaagbɛ lɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ amɛsanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.
Gbalamɔ adesa otui lɛ amli (n.k.
Eji nifeemɔ ni haa anaa naatselɛ.
Yɛ gbonyo lɛ kuramɔ mli lɛ, anyɛɔ aŋaa amɛmli nu.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: LALAWIEMƆ HENYAMƆ Kanemɔ nibii ni aŋmala yɛ lalawiemɔ he nyɛsɛɛ otsi lɛ ni okɛkai ohe.
Wɔbaa ya skul kɛkase nikasemɔ kɛ kɔmputa nitsumɔ.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ I: MƐƐ YƐ SANEGBAA MLI Eji beiaŋ ni sanegbalɔi lɛ tsakeɔ mɔ ni wiemɔ lɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli, ni haa gbɛjianɔtoo kɛ shishinumɔ ko baa sanegbaa lɛ he.
Nilee yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ ahe: Esa akɛ saneshishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ he akpɔ yɛ wiemɔi enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ amli jogbaŋŋ bɔ ni afee ni ekɛ rɛjista ni ja atsu nii kɛji etsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi.
Akutu tso kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Kaimɔ akɛ kɛji lalawiemɔ lɛ miiwie ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ saji ahe tete kɛjɛ beiaŋ ni aŋma lɛ lɛ, blema nifeemɔ lɛ ahe hiaa ejaakɛ ekudɔɔ ŋmalɔ lɛ.
Tsakemɔi ni eba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ mli Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ atsake yarafeemɔ hiɛ yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi lɛ babaoo amli, atsake kusum lɛ hiɛ ni afutu lɛ kɛ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ nibii ni ebaye nɔ lɛ.
Akɛ nibii nɛɛ woɔ gbonyo adeka lɛ mli.
Gbɛi kpakpa nɔ ni akɛ agwaseiaŋ tsuɔ nii lɛ ɛie hesalɛ, nyɛmɔ kɛ hewalɛ ko kpo.
Mɛni jwɛŋmɔ mli mfoniri asha?
Kɛji loomɔ lɛ, awoɔ nu (baa) kɛshwaa gbonyo lɛ he dani ajuɔ ehe.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Jiemɔ shishinumɔ kpo yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ he yɛ niŋmaa mli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Akpaa gbonyo lɛ krɔbɔ kɛ ayilɔ.
Wiemɔmuu ii.wiemɔmuu nta iii.Wiemɔmuu Haŋtsiŋ Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Yɛ afiyeli lɛ mli lɛ, ahoɔ shikwɛɛ niyenii tamɔ kpokpoi, ni mɛi feɔ ekome amɛkɛ tsui jurɔ daa shi yɛ nikpamɔ babaoo lɛ hewɔ.
Eji gbɛjianɔtoo ni hii kɛ yei fɛɛ tsɔmɔɔ mli kɛtsɔɔ he ni amɛshɛ yɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli, kɛtsakeɔ amɛ kɛboteɔ onukpayeli mli.
Atsɛɔ mi Nii Kwei ni mitsɔɔ Ga kɛ mɛi pii le Nkran.
Nikasemɔ oti: Kwɛmɔ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo maŋtsɛmɛi (maŋtsɛwalɔi, maŋtsɛ wolɔi, knkn) YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo Gbɛnaahiɛlɔi anɔkwɔlɔmɔ yɛ maŋtsɛyeli gbɛjianɔtoi amli Eji tso ni tsɔɔ gbɛnaahiɛlɔi anɔkwɔlɔmɔ.
Nilee ni ana yɛ lalai kɛ lalai asɔɔ lɛ baawa kaselɔi kɛna wiemɔkulibii he ni amɛna hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ anyiɛ kaselɔi ahiɛ kɛgba wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ he sane.
Ga mashi ji he kpo kɛha kusum kɛ blema nibii pii.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ gbɛiwiemɔi lɛ wowoi amɛgbɛhei lɛ.
Kaselɔi yɛ kuui amli aha sanemuji, amɛpɛi sanemuji lɛ amli ni amɛtsɔɔ nibii ni feɔ sanemuu lɛ.
Mlijaa 9: Lalai OTSI 19 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi: Awie nitsumɔ lalai ahe OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: NITSUMƆ LALAI
Gamɛi lɛ kɛ amɛfiyeli lɛ shwɛɛɛ kwraa.
Lalawiemɔ he sɛɛnamɔ Lalawiemɔ mligbalamɔ Lalawiemɔ ji niŋmaa ko ni akɛ wiemɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ ŋaa gbɛ nɔ kɛsɔɔ kɛjieɔ susumɔ, henumɔ loo yiŋtoo pɔtɛɛ ko kpo.
Gbi ko ni amɛtee amɛdaadaa juu ni amɛba lɛ.
Shwɛlɔ oti lɛ ji mɔ ni anuɔ ehe jogbaŋŋ yɛ adesa lɛ mli.
Ga wiemɔ lɛ jebɔ
Gbalamɔ mligbalalɔ mli otsɔɔ ni oha henɔi lɛ.
Gbalamɔ feemɔwiemɔ kɛ mligbalalɔ mli.
Afeɔ yɛ tsɛmɛi awe.
OTI MLIJAA NIKASEMƆ GBƐKPAMƆ KAA ŊƐLƐI L1 L2 L3 SANEGBAA • Gbalamɔ susumɔi kɛ otii ni ahara kɛjɛ sanegbai mli.
Amɛnitsɔɔmɔ lɛ ho be ni ato lɛ he kwraa ni jeee kuuŋbii lɛ fɛɛ ni wie.
Ashiɛɛɛ nikee lɛ.
Gbalamɔ Ga kɔnsonanti kpojee lɛ mli (e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee) Afi 2 Otsi 2: Gbalamɔ gbee nitsumɔ amli (e.g., sanemuu amli, wiemɔkulibii amli kɛ wiemɔi amli) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: FƆNƆLƆJI GBƐJIANƆTOO (BLAWA SHIKAMƆ GBƐJIANƆTOOI) Blawa Shikamɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi Blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ kɛ tsakemɔ baa blawa shikamɔ lɛ mli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ajajeɔ gbonyo lɛ ni ajuɔ ehe.
Dekanamɔ be he hiaa jogbaŋŋ ejaakɛ ewaa wɔ koni wɔkatsu nii fe nine.
Eŋɔɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome su asaŋ edamɔɔɔ shi tiimɔ nɔ.
Eeenyɛ efee kwakwe ni anaa yɛ kpatashi loo tsɔne ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ kɔmpiuta mli.
Kaselɔi baakase fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi, amɛbaakase blawa henɔi kɛ blawa jebɔ yɛ Ga mli.
Nikasemɔ he sanegbaa: Klasi muu fɛɛ
Tsɔɔmɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ahe sɛɛnamɔi.
Bɔimɔ sane lɛ kanemɔ hiɛhiɛ, okɛ ohiŋmɛi kɛ owao anyiɛ sane lɛ nɔ oyayaayai..
Kaselɔi lɛ miikase nii waa kɛha amɛkaa lɛ.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /b/ nyɛɔ ejeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi.
Ni oyaajie sane naa otsɔɔ
Taomɔ he ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ yɛ: Ani sane lɛ tsɛ he ko gbɛi?
Ekomɛi ji nikpamɔ afiyelii kɛ blemabii afiyelii, ni ayeɔ yɛ afi lɛ mli fɛɛ, kɛtsɔɔ kusum agbojee srɔtoi.
Nkwɛmɔnɔ, Olokwe he yɛ fɛo.
Susumɔ jebɔi ni kɔɔ gbaa saneŋmaa he ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kɛpia ohetoo lɛ.
Abaanyɛ agbala mli hu akɛ eji mlai ni akɛtsuɔ ni kɛha wiemɔkulibii atsɛrɛmɔ afee mlɛo.
Mɛi ni yeɔ: Oguaamɛi ni yɔɔ Cape Coast lɛ.
Bi bɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ ha: Ani adafitswaa nɛɛ tsɔɔ gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔ afee loo yiŋtoo ko kpo?
Mɛni maŋ nɔ ojɛ?
Vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ: Yɛ vaolii asusumɔkɛtsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ, otii komɛi yɛ ni akwɛɔ;
(e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee)
5 Ŋaagbɛ ni akɛŋma (Te eba lɛ tɛŋŋ?)
Wiemɔi lɛ yeee emuu.
Klasi muu fɛɛ: Awie gbɛi komɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa he.
Kanemɔ sane koni nyɛsusu he.
Naagbee gbɛhe Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ lɛ jieɔ ehe kpo yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ naagbee.
Nyɛtia gbele kɛ ehe saji lɛ amli.
Mlijaa 3 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ahe.
Klasi lɛ agba sanemuu henɔi lɛ ahe sane (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii)
Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he)
Naa bɔ ni ayeɔ afi lɛ.: Hesaamɔi Wulɔmɔi ni ji wowɛi lɛ anaadaalɔi lɛ baa shi.
Efee faŋŋ akɛ amɛsaa amɛhe.
Yarafeemɔ ji kusum nifeemɔ ni afeɔ kɛfuɔ/ toɔ mɔ ni egbo lɛ.
Mlijaa 10: LalawiemɔMlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 22 Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Lalawiemɔ henyamɔ Akwɛ nɔ ni eho Afi 1 Otsi 6: Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
Be mli ni awo lɛ tsuŋ lɛ, esɔleɔ ni ebiɔ nilee kɛjɛ bɔlɔ lɛ (Ɔboadze) kɛ hiimeji lɛ adɛŋ, ni ebiɔ hewalɛ yɛ mumɔŋ kɛ heloo gbɛfaŋ fɛɛ koni enyɛ eje kpo yɛ hewalɛ mli ni ejwɛŋmɔ mli hu aka kɛha nifeemɔi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ bɔ ni afee ni afi lɛ aye emuu.
Bɔ ni eyɔɔ lɛ nɛ:
Ebaanyɛ efee gbɛiwiemɔ, sutsɔɔlɔ loo sanekuku ni wieɔ feelɔ loo helɔ lɛ he.
Nikasemɔ oti: Akwɛ otii ni yɔɔ ŋkpaiyeli mli (Jaomɔ, shɛɛwiemɔi kɛ naamuu) YITSO: Ŋkpaiyeli Ŋkpaiyeli Eji nifeemɔ ko ni akɛ daa, akpɛtɛshi, weŋ, ŋmadaa, nu, tɛŋdaa loo nɔ ko ni hiɛ nu su, ni akɛshwieɔ shi ahaa mumɔŋ bii (sisai, jemawɔŋ, wɔji loo mumɔŋ bii) koni amɛtsu nɔ ko pɔtɛɛ ko amɛha.
Eyeɔ ebuaa shishinumɔ kɛ henumɔ: Ehaa maji maji nuɔ amɛhe shishi, ni ewoɔ ekomefeemɔ mli hewalɛ.
Akwasidae Afiyeli: Ashantemɛi kɛkaiɔ tsɛmɛi lɛ daa otsii ekpaa kɛtsɔ shikwɛɛ miyii, joo, niyenii ashwamɔ kɛ daji afɔsemɔ mli.
Trafiki mla nɔyeli: Kɛji aakudɔ tsɔji loo aafo gbɛ mli lɛ, esa akɛ aye mlai lɛ anɔ koni akɛna oshra loo agboi
Nɛkɛ beiaŋ lɛ, oblayoo fioo, ni ji omahene sɛi he yei lɛ ateŋ mɔ kome kpaa maŋ lɛ gbɛjegbɛi lɛ anɔ ni ekɛ niyenii srɔto ‘Eto” bahaa ewu lɛ.
Amɛbaatsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔi krokomɛi kɛ amɛkusum nɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Kɛkɔ nɔ ni okase yɛ lalawiemɔ he tsuutsu lɛ, tsɔɔmɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu ni yɛ lalawiemɔ nɛɛ he.
Feemɔ sanemuu ganee nɛɛ sanemuu haŋtsii
Sɔɔmɔ lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ kukuji etɛ, oti lɛ ji ‘tsuishitoo’
Najiaŋwolɔ lɛ ji mɔ ni woɔ shwɛlɔ oti lɛ naneteŋ ni ehaa lɛ naagbai srɔtoi.
Eji sanemuu henɔ ni haa nɔmimaa kɛ piamɔ wiemɔi kɛkɔ nɔ ni sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ.
Eyɛ yiŋtoo pɔtɛɛ ko Agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulinii hiɛ oti pɔtɛɛ ko, edamɔɔɔ ankroankro susumɔi, henumɔi loo mlijiemɔ nɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni susumɔ oti ji, koni otao susumɔ otii etɛ yɛ sane ko ni aha he.
Biɛ lɛ, atade lɛ ji helɔ ni naaa nifeemɔ lɛ he sɛɛ tuuntu lɛ.
Gbee: Eji henɔumɔ ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛŋmaa lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Hiimeji (Nanamom) ni agba amɛgbɛ lɛ, aheɔ ayeɔ yɛ mumɔŋ gbɛfaŋ akɛ amɛbafata Odwira afiyeli lɛ he.
Akɛ woo haa jemawɔji kɛ tsɛmɛi asisai lɛ.
Klɛŋklɛŋ lɛ, amɛbaakase wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi.
Lɛ ji tsɛ yɛ Ga maji lɛ nɔ
Ehaa mɛi kɛ mɛi shara jogbaŋŋ.
Eji onitsumɔ hu akɛ kɛ atsu bo lɛ oya.
Ŋmaa susumɔi lɛ kukuji.
Akɛ kusum nibii tsuɔ nii
Nikasemɔ he kaa
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ atsu nii yɛ kuu mli kɛgbala sanemuu kɛ ehe nibii lɛ amli ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii.
Oti : No ji shɛɛwiemɔ loo oti ni lalawiemɔ lɛ hiɛ lɛ.
Kɛ feemɔwiemɔ ni ona lɛ afee sanemuu.
Amɛkɛ amɛhe kɛ tui kɛ juu dadei ni yɔɔ amɛdeŋ lɛ ha polisifoi lɛ.
Bei komɛi hu lɛ, sanemuu oti walɔ lɛ nyɛɔ ejeɔ kuku lɛ shishi, Kɛ ehooo kwraa lɛ, sanemuu ni feɔ sanemuu oti walɔi yɛ kuku mli lɛ afee sanemuji enyɔ kɛyashi ejwɛ yɛ kuku kome mli.
Eko fɛɛ eko lɛ kɛ liamɔ ko yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ afata he.
Abaahara kaa sanebim ɔi lɛ yɛ henɔi ni baa n ɛɛ ashishi.
Ni no ji feemɔwiemɔ wiemɔkuku lɛ yitso.
Yitso: Vaoli gbɛhe Vaolii Agbɛhe Vaoli gbɛhe ji he pɔtɛɛ ni vaoli lɛ ka yɛ wiemɔ ko mli.
Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ akwɛ enɛ nɔ kɛgbala yarawoo lala nifeemɔ lɛ mli etsɔɔ.
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Nitsumɔ Nyɛtsɔa wiemɔkulibii komɛi ni nyɛsusuɔ akɛ akɛbaatsu nii yɛ hei nɛɛ amli;
Akɛgbeleɔ sane ko ni he miihia jogbaŋŋ hiɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii koni amɛkɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, mii ni amɛnaa kɛ hetoo kɛha amɛ nikasemɔ nɔyaa.
Abaaha amɛtsɔɔ wiemɔi, sanekukuji, sanefai, sanemuji kɛ kukujiaŋ aniŋmai ashishi kɛjɛ Blɔfo wiemɔ lɛ mli kɛba Ghana shikwɛɛ wiemɔi srɔtoi ni akaseɔ lɛ amli.
Tsakemɔi yɛ gbɔmɔtsoŋ Gbɔmɔtsoŋ tsakemɔi yɛ gbekɛbii hii amli
Gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he kɔɔ nibii ni anaa kɛ hiŋmɛi, anuɔ he ŋma, ataa he, aboɔ toi kɛ nibii ni asaaa naa.

Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
Maŋ lumɛi kɛ maŋbii fɛɛ wulaa amɛhe kɛ shikwɛɛ hesaamɔ nibii.
Gbalamɔ gbɛiwiemɔi srɔtoi ejwɛ amli ni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii nyɔŋma.
Agba gbee nitsumɔi komɛi ahe sane.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔbɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ ha yɛ sanemuu mli.
Doomɔ niŋmaa lɛ nɔ yɛ bo diɛŋtɛ wiemɔ mli.
(Mɛni/Namɔ he afi ayeɔ lɛ?
Akɛni ahaa mɔ ko ahɛ yɛ Fosu mukpo lɛ mli bei nɛɛ amli hewɔ lɛ, Ɔmanhen lɛ ji mɔ klɛŋklɛŋ ni fɔɔ yaa shii etɛ kɛ gbeleɔ mukpo lɛ naa kɛhaa maŋ lɛ.
Nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa amuɔ naagbai anaa: Klasi muu fɛɛ
Bɔ ni akaneɔ nii hiɛhiɛ Kɛji ona sane ko da ni oyɔse emlijaramɔi ni ootao okane lɛ.
Odwira gbi Jufɔ Hemɔkɛyeli lɛ ji akɛ Odwira ji mumɔŋ nikeenii ni hiimeji lɛ kɛhaa maŋtsɛyeli weku lɛ.
Maŋtsɛ lɛ wie hewale wiemɔ kɛtsɔɔ maŋbii lɛ fɛɛ.
Te vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii nɛɛ ateŋ nɔ ni nyɛɔ ejeɔ wiemɔkuli ko shishi?

Week 13 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Ŋmaa sanegbaa ko.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ tsaa otii ni wɔtsɔ hiɛ kɛkase yɛ afii enyɔ lɛ mli lɛ anɔ.
Sanemuu ni biɔ sane: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni biɔ sane.
Kadilɔi/ sutsɔɔlɔi nyiɛɔ gbɛiwiemɔi ni awieɔ amɛhe lɛ sɛɛ.
Sɔɔmɔ sɔnɛti (lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ liamɔ kpaanyɔ) yɛ oti ni osumɔɔ ni kɔɔ owala mli sane loo Ghana kusum he.
Be ni wuɔ gbɛɛ shii etɛ lɛ nakai leebi lɛ, mɛi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ Alata akrowa lɛ naa ekpe shi yɛ maŋtsɛwe lɛ.
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaaha sanebimɔi etɛ yɛ jeŋ saji henɔi lɛ ashishi (adesa, shwɛmɔ kɛ lalawiemɔ) koni kaselɔi akɔ emli ekome.
Wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi: Enɛmɛi ji heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu nii tamɔ: no ni, tamɔ no, oookɛɛ gbɔmɔ kɛ awohe, kɛ krokomɛi tamɔ gbɛigbɛɛlɔ, kɔsonanti shitɛlɛlɔi.
Kɛɛmɔ sanemuu: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni kɛɔ sane loo oti ko.
Ŋaagbɛii nɛɛ yeɔ ebuaa niiahesusumɔ vii, hetootamɔ kɛha saji anaamɔmɔ kɛ naatselɛ.
Too gbɛjianɔ kɛha ehe wiemɔ.

abɔɔ Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome lɛ mli lɛ, /b/ eje wiemɔkuli lɛ shishi.
Kɛji aawie susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi ahe yɛ sane ko he lɛ:
Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehe saji ahe titri lɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai srɔtoi ni baaye ebua kɛji aatsɔɔ sane ko shishi kɛmiiba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli.

Wiemɔkulibii ni ama
Sanemuu jieɔ susumɔ ni eye emuu kpo
(nɔ ni kɛ tsakemɔ baa wiemɔkulibii kɛ sanemuu teŋ).
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo he hiaa yɛ Ga wiemɔ mli.
Kɛji oonyiɛ kaselɔi ahiɛ kaŋmala sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ, maa nɔ mii akɛ ehe hia akɛ tsakpaa aba sanemuu oti lɛ kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ ateŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ naagba kome ni yɔɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ kusum nifeemɔi lɛ ahe yɛ okutso lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni abaanyɛ amɔ naa.
Okuapehene lɛ taa maŋjaranɔ ni emɛɔ Baamuhene kɛ ewebii lɛ koni ekɛ Odwira jɔɔmɔ lɛ ajɛ hiimeji lɛ aŋɔɔ kɛba koni anyɛ aye Odwira lɛ.
Gbalamɔ Ga ŋkpaiyeli jebɔ lɛ mli (gbɛgbelemɔ, wiemɔ, naamuu)
Niŋmalɔ lɛ kɛ kukuji ni eshwɛ lɛ piaa susumɔ oti lɛ.
Ehaa mɛi yeɔ awerɛho kutuu, ni etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni ka wekumɛi ateŋ nakai beiaŋ.
Ŋaagbɛ: Wiemɔ ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ tsɔ kɛjie eyiŋtoo lɛ kpo.
Shitiimɔ henɔ: Eji shiitiimɔi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli, titri lɛ yɛ liamɔ lɛ naagbee gbɛ.

Te vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii nɛɛ ateŋ nɔ ni nyɛɔ egbeɔ wiemɔkuli ko naa?
Nanemɛi enyɛ ni miigba bɔɔlu he sane: bɔɔlu, tswa, ntaŋ.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi aniishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ nikasemɔ lɛ mli.

Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, abaaha sanebimɔi etɛ yɛ jeŋ saji henɔi lɛ ashishi (adesa, shwɛmɔ kɛ lalawiemɔ) koni kaselɔi akɔ emli ekome.
Subaŋi ni shwɛlɔi lɛ jieɔ lɛ kpo lɛ haa ahaa oti ni awieɔ he lɛ, subaŋi lɛ tsɔɔ tsakpaa ni ka amɛteŋ.
Ajeɔ shishi kɛ hiɛgbelemɔ.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ yitsei komɛi Yɛ okutso lɛ mli lɛ, ani oyɔse akɛ sanegbaa kpakpa fɛɛ lɛ, toiboo kɛ mɛɛ yaa nɔ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Gbɔmɔtso lɛ kɛ jwɛŋmɔ naa hegbɛ kɛjɔɔ amɛhe kɛnaa hewalɛ hee ejaakɛ akpaaa gbɛ akɛ wɔbaatsu nitsumɔ ko kwraa yɛ dekanamɔ be lɛ mli.
Gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ ha nibii ajara tee ŋwɛi fe nine ni esa niyenii ni misumɔɔ lɛ hu he.
Lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ liamɔ nyɔŋma kɛ ejwɛ.
Wiemɔnta (wiemɔ + nta), gbɛiwiemɔ (gbɛi + wiemɔ), lamlu (la + mlu)
Enɛ baaha kaselɔi anaa atse yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Nɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ baa lɛ: mi, lɛ, e, bo, wɔ, nyɛ, amɛ, .

Tsɔɔmɔ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ kusumi ni afeɔ ahaa mɛi nɛɛ yɛ okutso lɛ mli: maŋtsɛmɛi, wɔŋtsɛmɛi, yeihɔlɔi kɛ gbekɛbii.
Agboshimɔ: Aboɔ agoo kɛshiɔ agbo kɛbiɔ gbɛ yɛ gbi ni afeɔ nii lɛ mli hewalɛ koni tsɛmɛi kɛ mumɔi lɛ ahɛle shi yɛ nifeemɔ ni baa lɛ he ni amɛye amɛbua.
Famɔ Sanemuu: Eji sanemuu henɔ ko ni akɛfaa mɔ.
Kɛtsɔ enɛ nɔ lɛ, Ɔmanhen kɛ enukpai lɛ yaa sɔlemɔfeemɔ nɛɛ ni amɛkɛ hegbɛ lɛ tswaa gbi ni abaaye afi lɛ yɛ afi hee ni abaabote lɛ mli lɛ.
Mlijaa 9 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie lalai ahe.
Gbeyeishemɔ ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ fɛɛ laajei
Oti: SANEGBAA Oti Mlijaa: Fɔnɔlɔji (Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi) Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi: Akɛ nilee ni ana kɛjɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli lɛ atsu nii yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Tsɛ Ataa Gbɛnaa hiɛlɔi lɛ anitsumɔ kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi: Weku Tɔkpaa hiɛnyiɛlɔ:
Nyɛkwɛa gbaa sane ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ koni nyɛŋmala otii ni ehiɛ lɛ fɛɛ: Atsɔse bo ni oda yɛ Ghana Dalɛ yɛ Ghana yɛ 1990 bei lɛ amli, eji haomɔ waa nakai bei lɛ amli kɛha mi.
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ okusum lɛ mli atsɔɔ shwɛmɔ lala henɔi ni ayɔɔ.
Yɛ nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ maŋbii lɛ waa kɛ maŋ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ nibii tamɔ nubu jee, sukuu aloo jara maa, kɛ nibii krokomɛi ni he baahia akutsoŋbii lɛ.
Mɛi tsɔɔ nɔ kɛjieɔ amɛhe kpo, shika ni amɛyɔɔ kɛ gbɛnaa ni amɛhiɛ.
Amɛnaagbee gbɛɛmɔi lɛ hu nyɛɔ efeɔ srɔto.
Mligbalalɔ Mligbalalɔ tsɔɔ bɔ ni nifeemɔ ko tee nɔ ha.
No hewɔ lɛ, /a/ nyɛɔ egbeɔ wiemɔkuli ko naa.
Jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔi kɛha wɔsɛɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ, wiemɔ lɛ tsɔɔmɔ loo wiemɔ lɛ he mlawoo
Wiemɔ yarafeemɔ kɛ ehenɔi ni yɔɔ kusum lɛ mli lɛ ahe.
Ehaa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ naa leɔ akɛ nakai mɔ floŋŋ lɛ eshɛ nuu loo yoo ni baanyɛ ebote gbalashihilɛ mli.
Gbɛi ni ashimɔ yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ mli ni abaanyɛ atsu he nii wɔsɛɛ lɛ yopomɔ OTSI 10 (18 marks) Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Nikasemɔ oti: Nyɛsusua Afoata he otii lɛ ahe.
Nɛkɛ teemɔŋ nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, agbaa gbɛ lɛ kɛjɛɔ Akropɔŋ maŋ lɛ teŋ ni ji Okuapeman maŋ wulu lɛ kɛyaa lumɛi agɔɔ ni atsɛɔ lɛ Amanprobi lɛ.
Kuui lɛ agbala fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amɛ ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko he.
Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ni ji etɛ lɛ mli lɛ, /a/ eba yɛ wiemɔkuli lɛ naagbee.
Sharamɔ ni ka maji maji ateŋ: Ehe baa sɛɛnamɔ kɛha maŋnɔkwɛlɔi kɛ maji maji ateŋ sharamɔ kpakpa.
Esa ni oniŋmaa /wiemɔ lɛ afee ekome: Esaaa ni otsakeɔ wiemɔkulibii otii kɛ ŋaa ni okɛtsuɔ nii lɛ.
Maŋtsɛ wulu kɛ eshishi bii Yɛ Gamɛi lɛ amli lɛ maŋtsɛ ni da fe fɛɛ ji Maŋtsɛ Wulu (King).
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Misumɔɔ omɔ jogbaŋŋ shi minyɛmi yoo lɛ sumɔɔ banku kɛ shitɔ.

Kaa Ni Afeɔ Yɛ Nikasemɔ Lɛ Teŋ Lɛ Jebɔ Mlijaa A: Sanebimɔi ni aharaa yɛ hetooi ateŋ = Sanebimɔi 20 Mlijaa B: Nikanemɔ – Sanebimɔ 1 Mlijaa C: Niŋmaa he sanebimɔ – 1 Question Bɔ ni afeɔ mlijaa A kɛ B he sanebimɔi lɛ Otsi Oti he ni asusuɔ lɛ Sanebimɔ henɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛi Fɛɛ 1 2 3 4 Sanebimɔi ni sa yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ: Eji shishinumɔ ni tɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii loo wiemɔkukuji ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ hiɛhiɛ shishinumɔ lɛ nɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji sane, susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi.
Ekɛ nyam bahaa mɔ floŋŋ lɛ kɛ eweku fɛɛ
(Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ aha sanekuku ko ni tɔmɔi yɛ kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi ahe nitsumɔ lɛ mli, sane lɛ mliwalɛ ahi kaselɔi aŋɛlɛ nɔ.
Halamɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nii ni aha lɛ mli etɛ yɛ kui enyɔ lɛ fɛɛ mli koni okɛfee wiemɔmuu ntai.
Blema saji: Odwira blema saji lɛ damɔ Akan maŋtsɛyelii lɛ anɔ, shi Fetu Afahyɛ lɛ Oguaamɛi ablema saji kɛ kusum pɛ anɔ edamɔ.
Kuu nitsumɔ: Kuuŋbii lɛ akɔ oti pɔtɛɛ ko ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii henɔi srɔtoi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛka amɛyiŋ yɛ he.
Ani okɛ amɛ kpaa gbee loo mɛni oyɔɔ kɛɛmɔ?
Lalai asɔɔ kɛ emɔmɔ yɛ yitsoŋ lɛ haa wɔnaa hesalɛ yɛ nikaimɔ, agbo naa wiemɔ kɛ hiɛkaa yɛ wiemɔ mli mli.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ nikasemɔ mli kɛwaa kaselɔi ashishinumɔ ni nifeemɔ aya hiɛ.
/n/ Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ:
Yɛ Ŋɛlɛ 3 lɛ, abiɔ saji ni haa asusuɔ niibii ahe vii kɛtsɔɔ niŋmaa kukuji loo kakadaji anɔ kɛkaa kaselɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mlikwɔlɛ kɛ shishinumɔ ni amɛyɔɔ sanegbaa gbɛjianɔtoo he kɛ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔi ni esani amɛkɛtsu nii yɛ maŋ kɛ jeŋjeŋ saji amli lɛ ahe.

Enɛ ha ni akɛ abifabii komɛi woɔ jemawɔji yɛ akutsei amli lɛ.

OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU HEN ƆI
Saneshishitsɔɔlɔi/tsɔɔlɔi atsɔɔ shishi/atsɔɔ lɛ bɔ ni eyɔɔ yɛ lɛ amɛkusum naa)
Anaa naabufemɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli;
Kɛji okɛ eko gbe yi lɛ gbalamɔ mli otsɔɔ kaselɔi lɛ.
Bɔ ni asɔɔ lɛ:
Hɔmɔwɔ afahyɛ lɛ ji afahyɛ kɛse kɛha Ga mashi bii fɛɛ.
Kɔlɔmɔ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii enumɔ kɛjɛ sutsɔɔlɔi lɛ mli kɛfee sanemuu nta.
Tamɔ, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nitsumɔhei komɛi ji;
Liamɔi : Eji wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛ yɛ kpaa kome nɔ ni feɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Be komɛi lɛ, tsakemɔ fioo ko baa wiemɔkulibii hee ni asɔ lɛ mli.
Gbalamɔ mligbalalɔi henɔi lɛ amli koni oha eko fee eko nɔkwɛmɔnɔi enumɔ.
Nɔ ni gbele kɛbaa mɔ nɔ: Eyɛ mli moŋ akɛ gbele foɔ adesa tɔlɛ sɛɛ, shi gbele diɛŋtsɛ nyɛɔ ekɛ nibii fɔji baa adesa nɔ yɛ gbɛi pii anɔ.
Nifeemɔ mli nikasemɔ: Foo ni ola nitsumɔ lala.
Ekɛ emaŋtsɛmɛi bibii lɛ kɛ maŋ onukpai lɛ taa shi yɛ Tabri gbatsu lɛ naa he ni akɛ tsina afimɔ enaji lɛ eŋmɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji jemawɔji agbɛi.
Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, agbeɔ tsina kɛjuraa Oguaamaŋ.
Wiemɔkulibii hee kɛ niŋmaa mlai Akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ja tsu nii.
Namɔ gbee anuɔ yɛ sane lɛ mli lɛ?
Mliijaa 7 Mlikwɛmɔ Otsii nyɔŋma kɛ enumɔ kɛ nyɔŋma kɛ ekpaa lɛ, wɔwie shikwɛɛ afiyelii, amɛhenɔi kɛ sɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa:
Kwɛmɔ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ nɛɛ nɔ Aflimata Aflimata bibioo ko A Ni hɔmɔ miiye lɛ B Esɔmɔ shi, esɔmɔ shi C Gɔbi gɔbi gɔbi, D Ni jwɛ, jwɛ, jwɛ ni etu.
Awoɔ shikwɛɛ atadei Afiyeli lɛ Kɛji aje afi lɛ shishi lɛ, maŋ lumɛi, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ maŋbii lɛ toɔ srɛnɛɛ.
Kɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii enumɔ agbala wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli (mii 5)
Ŋmaa vaolii etɛ kɛ hooo kwraa.
Mɛi pii basara Ga maŋ lɛ be ni ayeɔ afahyɛ lɛ.

Sekpe, Maŋ ko ta maŋ ko nɔ, Tsale wɔte, Kwashiorkor Wiemɔkulii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ahe sɛɛnamɔi Wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ahe hia jogbaŋŋ beiaŋ ni wiemɔi tsakeɔ lɛ.

Kɛfatahe lɛ, atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtaoɔ shika kɛhaa Oguaamaŋ.

Te awoɔ maŋtsɛ tɛŋŋ yɛ kpokpa lɛ nɔ?
Kwɛmɔ specification kɛ structure of the paper ni yɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ naagbee lɛ nɔ kɛha yelikɛbuamɔ.
Ekwɛ shwɛmɔ lalai hu, ewieɔ ehenɔi, jebɔ, sɛɛnamɔi kɛ bɔ ni feɔ ni anyaa lalai nɛɛ ahe.
Maŋnyɛ Maji lɛ fɛɛ woɔ maŋnyɛ kɛyeɔ buaa maŋkuramɔ lɛ, titri lɛ, yei agbɛjianɔtoo.
Sukuuyaa kɛ Shigbɛlɛmɔ: Ehaa nilee kɛ sukuuyaa he nibii baa sɛɛnamɔ yɛ jeŋ.
Kwɛ mli fɛɛ loo doo yitso lɛ nɔ Mlikwɛmɔ ekoŋŋ: Akwɛɔ mli ekoŋŋ koni akɛyɔse nɔ ni akane momo loo akane lɛ vii lɛ jogbaŋŋ Akwɛɔ mli jogbaŋŋ akɛ ani nɔ ko yɛ he ni sa akɛ atsake lo.
Jwɛŋmɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ahe.
Te obaakwɛ sanegbaa nɛɛ nɔ oha tɛŋŋ?
Kaa bɔ ni atsɛɔ wiemɔkulibii lɛ okwɛ ni oha ohiɛ ama fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ nɔ.

KAA NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ shishi yeɔ ebuaa niiashishinumɔ kɛ niiahesusumɔ vii yɛ kaselɔi agbɛfaŋ.

Ekomefeemɔ: Afiyeli nifeemɔi lɛ haa mɛi kuɔ amɛsɛɛ kɛyaa amɛmaŋ, ni no tsɔɔ mɛi ni amɛji.
Haramɔ sane ko yɛ otii tamɔ subaŋ kpakpa, STEM, GESI, srawa hewalɛ he saji, falefalefeemɔ, akutso kɛ jeŋmaji saji, hewalɛkpakpamɔ he ni okane.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kaselɔi kɛ amɛhe woɔ nikasemɔ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli kɛ yelikɛbuamɔ nibii srɔtoi atsu nii koni ewa nitsɔɔmɔ kɛ nikasemɔ fɛɛ.
Akɛ daa tɔ kome nyiɛɔ gbonyo lɛ hiɛ ni ayafuɔ lɛ.
Ku nitsumɔ: Nyɛkɛ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ yɛ Ghana maji srɔtoi lɛ anɔ lɛ atotoa amɛhe ni nyɛtsɔa srɔto kɛ nibii ni haa kusumi lɛ kɛ amɛhe yeɔ hegbɔ.
Ajoɔ Adammba kɛtsɛɔ hiimeji lɛ amumɔi lɛ koni wɔŋtsɛmɛi lɛ anyɛ agba.
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ otii etɛ komɛi ni ona yɛ sane ko ni aha he.
Lɛ ji kojolɔnukpa yɛ maji ni yɔɔ eshishi lɛ anɔ
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Kaselɔi baakase bɔ ni amɛbaana wiemɔ ni amɛkɛtsu nii.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛtɛɛtɛ yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Kɛji wɔdamɔ nibii nɛɛ anɔ lɛ, kanelɔi baanyɛ ana shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ ni amɛnya lalawiemɔ lɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ, otii kɛ ŋaagbɛ ni akɛŋma nii lɛ he.
Bi be ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ: Ani adafitswaa lɛ tsɔɔ be pɔtɛɛ ko?
Kɛji Ɔmanhen sha afɔle lɛ eta lɛ, Ayeɔ afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ Hɔɔ klɛŋklɛŋ ni yɔɔ Gbo nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ lɛ.
Oko kpɔ shitɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ afiyeli ni okutsoŋbii lɛ yeɔ ni otsɔɔ nibii etɛ hewɔ ni amɛbaahiɛ afiyeli lɛ mli.
Abaanyɛ akɛtsu nii yɛ ŋaa niŋmaa ni ji ‘creative writing’ mli shi kɛji akɛmii tsu nii trilii yɛ agwaseiaŋ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli lɛ sane baaba.
Akadi tsakemɔi ni eba yɛ mɔ floŋŋ lɛ mli.
Ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo: Ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo lɛ ji oti ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ.
OTI: JEŊNILEE YƐ GHANA SHIKWƐƐ WIEMƆ LƐ MLI Oti Mlijaa: Ŋaaŋ Saji Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalai ahe sui lɛ asɔɔ lalai Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Atsɔɔ nilee kɛ shishinumɔ ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalai ahe HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ obɔade lalai tritri lɛ nitsumɔ kɛ shwɛmɔi lalai ahe.
Mɛi komɛi kɛ amɛnaji foji nyiɛɔ.
Ajara hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli etɛ, tsɛ fɛɛ koni otsɔɔ nɔ ni yaa nɔ yɛ fɛɛ eko mli.
Mɛi lɛ jɛ Akrokpong- Akuapim, Aburi, Larteh kɛ Mamfe kn.kn yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ.

Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbɛi
Mɛɛ toisɛɛtsɛmɔ wiemɔ tsɔɔ be kɛ he ni aŋma nii lɛ yɛ.
gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ, hei ni gbɛɛmɔi jeɔ kpo yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ Yitso: Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli kɛ kɔnsonanti Voali Eji wiemɔ gbɛɛmɔi ni hoɔ yɔɔ kɛtsɔɔ daaŋ ni nɔ ko etsiii enaa.
Wiemɔi lɛ mli ka shi ni ehe hia kɛha sanegbaa lɛ.
Yɛ Ga wiemɔ nikasemɔ tsu lɛ mli (mɔ kome baafee tsɔɔlɔ, mɔ kome baafee nikaselɔ hee ni ejɛ Ablotsiri eba etsɛko, mɔ kome hu baatsɔɔ nɔ ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ wieɔ shishi eha kaselɔ hee lɛ) Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ He Ŋai Gbaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehenɔi lɛ ahe sane (N.k., wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ, Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ, kn.kn)
Agba shwɛmɔ lalai henɔi lɛ ahe sane
nTsɔɔmɔ gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛii ashishi.
Nikasemɔ he nitsumɔ
Jaramɔ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ owo ehenɔi lɛ amli ni bo diɛŋtsɛ oha nɔkwɛmɔnɔ yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ atsa nɔ ni awieɔ lɛ nɔ ni eko fɛɛ eko hu awa ni shɛɛwiemɔ muu ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ aŋmɛ jara nɔ.
Kɛ sane ni otsɔɔ shishi lɛ aba klasi ni nyɛ fɛɛ nyɛgbaa he sane.
Mɛni ji otii krɛdɛɛ ni yɔɔ mli.
Kɛji agbe afi lɛ naa lɛ, Hɔgbaa lɛ asafo kui (sɔlemɔi) fɛɛ feɔ ekome ni amɛjaa yɛ Victoria Park kɛdaa Nyɔŋmɔ shi akɛ Eye ebua Oguaa ni afiyeli lɛ efee shweshweeshwe.
Blemabii lɛ Afiyelii
Faafoo – Kɛji asaa gbonyo he lɛ, wekumɛi foɔ lɛ faa – Ahaa shika nigbonyo lɛ kɛfo faa kɛya gbohiajeŋ.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi aniashishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ shikwɛɛ afiyeli yɛ Ghana nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Otsi 8: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee NITSƆƆMƆ ŊAAGBƐ NƆDOOMƆ Aakpa akɛ tsɔɔkɔi kɛ nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa kaselɔi muɔ naagbai anaa, kuuŋ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ atsu nii kɛtsɔɔ yitso nɛɛ.
Edamɔɔ shi akɛ mligbalalɔ wiemɔkuku lɛ yitso.
Bei pii lɛ, anaa sanemuu oti walɔi nɛɛ yɛ kuku lɛ teŋ.
Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli lɛ haa gbekɛbii mɔɔ mlai ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ akutso mli lɛ tamɔ tsuishitoo, nijaa, niiashishinumɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Nifeemɔ ni yɔɔ feemɔwiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ ninaaa helɔ/nɔnalɔ henɔ kroko lɛ tuuntu.
Ŋmaa sane yɛ niyenii ni osumɔɔ waa he.
NB: Srɔtoi yɛ maŋtsɛmɛi ahalamɔ, amɛwomɔ kɛ amɛkpojiemɔ mli yɛ maji srɔtoi anɔ.
Nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ.
Nyɛsusua nɔ ni ji saneŋmaa
Nɛkɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔ nɛɛ kwɛɔ kusum, wiemɔ kɛ jeŋshihilɛ saji ni miiya nɔ nakai ŋmɛlɛtswaa lɛ mli ni akɛtsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi bɔ ni eyɔɔ ni anaa shishitsɔɔmɔi ni ja.
Mɛɛ gbɛi anɔ shwɛlɔi ahe hia yɛ niŋmaa mli?
Week 5 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii(s): Nyɛkɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ he gbɛjianɔtoi asusua nikanemɔ nɛɛ he(nkn., hiŋmɛi loo wao kɛ wiemɔi anɔ nyiɛmɔ kɛtao wiemɔkulibii loo wiemɔkukuji k.n) Yitso: Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ mligbalamɔ Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ ji nikanemɔ ni awoɔ lɛ foi kɛtaoɔ oti loo adafitswaa ko pɔtɛɛ shi jeee ni ookane wolo tso muu lɛ fɛɛ.
Amɛbaakase yarafeemɔ, yarafeemɔ henɔi, yarafeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔ kɛ tsakemɔi ni eba ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ mli.
Wiemɔkulibii aharamɔ: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni aharaa kɛsɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Hewalɛ shibaa: Gbele mli naagbai nyɛɔ ehaa hela mɔɔ mɛi titri lɛ mɛi ni egbɔjɔ momo loo mɛi ni hela yɛ amɛhe momo
Ŋmaa ko ni otsɔɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ anyaa Afoata sane lɛ mli.
Ekaneɔ ŋshɔ kei kpawo ni etsiɔ nuu lɛ ewoɔ ŋshɔ lɛ mli kɛkɛ lɛ nuu lɛ eje kpo.
Pɛimɔ sanemuji nɛɛ amli ni otsɔɔ ejebɔ
Tii saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ amli (wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, shishinumɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ, sanegbaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ)
Jeee gbonyo lɛ hiɛnyamwoo akwɛɔ dɔŋŋ shi moŋ koni maŋbii ale weku lɛ gbɛnaa yɛ maŋ lɛ mli.
Okulafeemɔ: Kɛji yoo ko wu gbo lɛ, atoɔ be ni afeɔ okula haa yoo lɛ.
Etsɔɔɔ nɔ kɛgbaa esusumɔi ahe sane shi etsɔɔ nɔ kɛnuɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ lɛ shishi.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ bɔ ni akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ kɛwieɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋmaji saji ahe ni awie naagbai ahe jogbaŋŋ.
Afeɔ yɛ gbii srɔtoi komɛi anɔ
Mlijaa nɛɛ waa kaselɔi ni amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna lɛ atsu nii kɛnu fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ shishi.
Oti: Koni akai Oguaamɛi ablema sane, kusum kɛ ekomefeemɔ.
Niŋmaa faŋŋ kɛ tsakpaa yɛ niŋmaa mli.
Nɛgbɛ ayeɔ afi lɛ yɛ?

Mlijaa 5: SanemuuMlijaa 5: Sanemuu Nikasemɔ He Kaa
Mɛni ji sane oti kɛ sane oti walɔi?
Gbalamɔ alabaaloo jebɔ lɛ mli.
Nya lalawiemɔ ko he.
Oshra gbele: Enɛ lɛ, oshra ni kɛ nɛkɛ gbele nɛɛ baa.
Tsɔɔmɔ ojwɛŋmɔ koni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oheɔ owoɔ mli loo eheee owooo mki.
Ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baa ni ekɛ tsakpaa baa maŋbii lɛ ateŋ.
Suɔlɔi krokomɛi kɛ nibii srɔtoi baaha gbonyo lɛ ni amɛkɛ lɛ yeɔ shɛɛ.
NB: Mfoniri nɛɛ miitsɔɔ mɔ ko ni miila yarawoo lala.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ shikwɛɛ/blema saneyeli kɛ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ saneyeli teŋ.
Te alaa yarafeemɔ lalai tɛŋŋ yɛ okutso lɛ mli?
Yɛ fɛɛ mli lɛ, esa akɛ bi nukpa lɛ kɛ nu shwieɔ enɔ klɛŋklɛŋ dani bibii lɛ anyiɛ sɛɛ.
Esa ni ale akɛ gbele shishitsɔɔmɔ yɛ srɔtoi yɛ kusumi srɔtoi lɛ amli, mla naa kɛ gbɔmɔtsoŋ kwɛmɔ mli saji.
Kaselɔi baanu kusum he nibii ashishi ni amɛbaanya kusumi krokomɛi ahe hu ni ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baaba.
Kwɛmɔ gbee lɛ (subaŋ/henumɔ) – bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ obaaha nɔkwɛmɔnii!
Kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi: Eji kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome ni akɛje wiemɔkulibii ashishi ni tsara amɛhe nɔ yɛ liamɔ mli.
Week 6 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Nyɛsusua naajiemɔi yɛ naabu he be mli ni ookwɛ toiboo kpɛŋŋ, kwɛmɔ nɔ ni onu kɛ nɔ ni obaakɛɛ.
Shia lɛ mli ejɔ.
Nibii fɛɛ ni ahe hiaa lɛ yɛ mli.
Gbaa saneŋmaa hiɔ sui ni jieɔ henumɔ ko kpo yɛ nibii pɔtɛɛ he ni awieɔ lɛ he.
Jemawɔji agbɛi fata gbɛi henɔi srɔtoi lɛ ahe yɛ Ghana fɛɛ.
Wekumɛi ni kpeɔ kɛgbaa kotsa lɛ.
Namɛi ji maŋtsɛmɛi bibii?
Kaa ŋaagbɛ kɛ ŋaagbɛi ni waa kɛkadiɔ kaselɔi ashishinumɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ kasemɔ he.
Kɛji yinɔbii bibii ni baa lɛ kɛ amɛhe wo afiyeli kɛ kusum nifeemɔi amli lɛ, amɛbaakase blema saji kɛ kusum he nibii ni baawa kɛbaa shikwɛɛ nibii nɛɛ ayi kɛha wɔsɛɛ yinɔbii krokomɛi ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ.
Kanemɔ Lalawiemɔ l ɛ: Kanemɔ lalawiemɔ ni ahara lɛ shii abɔ ni onu emli wiemɔ lɛ, gbee (henumɔ ni akɛŋma lalawiemɔ loo wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu lɛ) shishi kɛ wiemɔ lɛ.
Shwɛlɔi: Amɛ ji mɛi ni niŋmalɔ lɛ taa sane woɔ amɛnaa kɛjieɔ eyiŋtoo lɛ kpo.
Ati nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii nikasemɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ
Tsɔɔmɔ nilee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ shishitsɔɔmɔ ni jɛ mɔ ko naa mli
Ahaa la saa, saanɔbo, sune, pioto, mamai kɛ shika fɛɛ.
/ɔ/-baashi-sɛɛ-kokloo Kɔnsonanti Eji gbɛɛmɔi, ni yɛ amɛsɔɔ mli lɛ, lilɛi lɛ he ko tsiɔ kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ naa dani efɛɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ, ni akɛfataa vaoli he kɛfeɔ blawa.
Nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ kroko ni aakpa gbɛ akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii ji akɛ amɛbaama nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ lɛ nɔ mi.

Bibioo Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) – (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Nifeemɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ kɛ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jeɔ ehe kpo yɛ liŋwiskiti gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ, tamɔ mɔfɔlɔji, fɔnɔlɔji loo sintasi.
Gbalamɔ Ga obɔade afiyelii lɛ amli ni otsɔɔ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi.
Klasi lɛ agba he sane.
Be ni kaselɔi kɛkaneɔ nii hiɛhiɛ
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi akadi kaselɔi ni yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ sanegbaa naagbaai lɛ ni amɛtsɔ ŋaagbɛ nɔ kɛjaje susumɔ gbohii lɛ.
Afiyeli hesɛɛnamɔi: Afiyeli he yɛ sɛɛnamɔi babaoo.
Tɔmɔi yɛ niŋmaa mlai lɛ mli lɛ yɛ mlɛo.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ bɔ ni akɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii ŋaagbɛi lɛ tsuɔ nii kɛdoɔ loo foɔ niŋmai kakadaji anɔ kuku.
Nibii komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛsɔɔ kɔnsonantii komɛ ji kɔɔyɔɔ naatsii (plossives), ashrahe (fricatives), ashrahe kpoo (affricate), gugɔjiaŋ (nasal), kn.
Jwɛŋmɔ Eji susumɔ loo jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ yɛ nɔ ko he.
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii kɛ fi sɛɛ
Ŋkpaiyeli Kusumfeemɔ lɛ sɛɛ
Okuapehene lɛ heɔ shɛɛwiemɔi fɛɛ ni jɛ maŋ onukpai kɛ ewebii ni yɔɔ maŋsɛɛ kɛ emaŋ lɛ fɛɛ adɛŋ.
Afisifeemɔ: Akɛ afisi tsaa wiemɔkulibii nɔ kɛnaa wiemɔkulibii hee.
Bei pii lɛ akɛ gbɛhegbɛi ni yoɔ helɔ/nɔnalɔ henɔ nɛɛ.
Yiŋtoo lɛ gbalaa mɛi ajwɛŋmɔ kɛbaa nɔ ni yaa nɔ lɛ nɔ ni ewoɔ oblantai lɛ aŋaa.
sɛɛgbɛ tsɔɔlɔ (lɛ) fɔɔ gbɛiwiemɔi asɛɛ nyiɛmɔ shi jeee be fɛɛ be Najiaŋgbɛi Najiaŋgbɛi ji wiemɔkuli ni nyɛɔ damɔɔ shi haa gbɛiwiemɔ sanekuku.
Ga sɛɛsɛɛ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ naaŋmɔlɔ nɔ kɛtsɔɔ srɔto yɛ nɔ ni sa ni ofee kɛ nɔ ni yaa nɔ lɛ teŋ.
Yɛ enɛ mli lɛ yitso ni tere wiemɔkuku lɛ ji tso
Ajieɔ afi lɛ diɛŋtsɛ shishi klɛŋklɛŋ Hɔɔ ni yɔɔ Gbo nyɔɔŋ lɛ mli lɛ nɔ.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ sanebimɔ ni yɛ faŋŋ.
YITSO: Yarawoo lalai (enitsumɔ kɛ sɛɛnamɔi).
Amɛ le wiemɔkulibii lɛ jogbaŋŋ Mɛi ni kɛ nɛkɛ tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii lɛ tsuɔ nii lɛ nuɔ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ashishi jogbaŋŋ.
Gbala nibii etɛ komɛi ahewɔ ni akɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli tsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa nɛ mli.
Kaa Oti: Dok Ŋɛlɛ 4 – Tsuŋ Nitsumɔ Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu henɔi srɔtoi ni ni ŋmalɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii lɛ (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii).
Twsiamɔ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛŋmaa sane yɛ mlijaramɔi amli.
Kwɛmɔ mlai lɛ koni oŋmala tɔmɔi fɛɛ ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli..
OTSI 13 Susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sanemuu mli mligbalamɔ : Susumɔ oti ji oti ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 Asɛsimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Wiemɔkulibii haram ɔ: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛtsuɔ ni kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ kɛ gbee lɛ kpo.
Mɛɛ be mli yɛ afi lɛ mli ayeɔ nakai afi lɛ?
Wiemɔkulibii lɛ ewieee oti lɛ he.
Afee abifao kpojiemɔ kusum lɛ atsɔɔ.
Kaselɔi ni bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ jogbaŋŋ lɛ kɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli atsu nii.
Yelikɛbuamɔ kpitiokpitio ni tsɔɔlɔ kɛhaa kaselɔi beiaŋ ni nitsumɔ lɛ miiya nɔ lɛ yeɔ bua kaselɔi lɛ kɛnaa shidaamɔ kpakpa yɛ nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
He ni ŋmalɔ lɛ jɛ lɛ naa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni ekɛtsuɔ nii kɛ nibii krokomɛi hu.
Kɛji kɔnsonanti ko je wiemɔ ko shishi lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ shishijee.
Okwaafoiatsɛ Atoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ kɛji okwaafoi ji maŋbii lɛ.
Mɛni feelɔ ni yɔɔ (a) lɛ mli lɛ he lɛ?
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Sane kaimɔ
Owiemɔmuji lɛ ahi yitsei nɛɛ shishi.
Oti he ni awieɔ: Enɛ ji nɔ nɔ ni adesa lɛ damɔɔ asaŋ no nɔŋŋ akɛkudɔɔ adesa lɛ nɔyaa.
nakai, nɛkɛ, lɛ nɛ.
Yifalɛ (yɛ Ghana wiemɔi lɛ ekomɛi amli lɛ): E.g.
Nɔ hewɔ ni amɛɔ yɛ sanegbaa mli: Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli haa sanegbalɔi lɛ naa be ni sa kɛkudɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ.
Nɛkɛ futumɔ nɛɛ tsɔɔ he ni gbonyo lɛ jɛ kɛ jamɔ mli ni Ghanabii pii yɔɔ.
Abaanyɛ adamɔ afoata loo anɔkwale saji agbɛjianɔtoo nɔ.
Yɛ henɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, anaa wiemɔkulibii he kwraa loo akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni yɛ momo ni akɛtsuuu ni lɛ ha wiemɔkulibii ko.
Odwira Afiyeli: Nikpamɔ afiyeli ni Akropong-Akwapimɛi yeɔ yɛ Boka Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ kɛdaa wowɛi lɛ ashi kɛha nikpamɔ babaoo.
Nilee yɛ wiemɔ ni abaatsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛya kɛ wiemɔ ni atsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi yɛ lɛ, kusum he nilee, wiemɔ mli mlai / akpɔi kɛ natselɛ kn.kn) Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 3: Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
NIKASEMƆ OTI: WIEMƆKULIBII HEE ASƆƆ Kanemɔ otsi 7 nikasemɔ lɛ mli Nikasemɔ he Kaai
Mɛni ji nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa?
Lalawiemɔ ni jieɔ henumɔ kpo Lalwiemɔ nitsumɔi krokomɛi
Akutseiaŋbii akuu fɛɛ akutso jɛ maŋ lɛ nɔ heko srɔto.
NB: Tsɔɔlɔi baanyɛ amɛkɛ gbɛii nɛɛ ekomɛi afata he pii.
Kuui lɛ baabatsɔɔ klasi lɛ.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: LALAWIEMƆ HENYAMƆ Ati lalawiemɔ ji mli: Kwɛmɔ otsi 21 kɛ 22 kɛna mligbalamɔ ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ he kɛ nibii ni adamɔɔ nɔ kɛnyaa lalawiemɔ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni esa akɛ atsɔɔ sane shishi ni eja lɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ lɛ
Ni kɛji egbe wiemɔkuli ko hu naa lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ naagbee.
Kanemɔ lalawiemɔ kɛjɛ lalawiemɔ wolo lɛ mli.
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔi kɛ wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi tsuɔ nii, ekomɛi ji: Tamɔ no, No ni, abuomama kɛ mfonirishamɔ.
Ehaa ewebii naa he afabaŋfoo
Daamɔ mlai ni nyɛkase lɛ nɔ kɛhala tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ fɛɛ.
Nitsumɔ lalai haa gbeyei mɔmɔɔ mɛi fɔji ni baato amɛyiŋ akɛ amɛbaapila nitsulɔi lɛ.
Ŋmalamɔ kɔnsonantii ni yɔɔ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ OTSI 12 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee atsu nii kɛhara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko ni abaadoo nɔ mli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 4: Akɛ nilee ni oyɔɔ yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he lɛ akane nii ni akɛtao hetoo aha jeŋshihilɛ mli naagbai Afi 2 Otsi 4: Awie nikanemɔ gbajaa gbeji lɛ ahe (nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ , nikanemɔ vii, kn.) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: NIKANEMƆ HIƐHIƐ KƐ NIKANEMƆ VII Nikanemɔ Hiɛhiɛ: Yɛ enɛ shishi, akwɛɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli oyaoyai kɛnaa nɔ pɔtɛɛ he ni niŋmaa lɛ wieɔ, otii gbajaa kɛ niŋmaa lɛ jebɔ.
Gɔɔŋyaa Atereɔ gbonyo lɛ (eniji yajeɔ hiɛ ni eyitso yajeɔ sɛɛ).
Liamɔi lɛ nyɛɔ efeɔ sanemuu, enyɛɔ efeɔ sanekuku loo wiemɔkulibii fioo ko pɛ hu.
Kaselɔi lɛ baaha sanemuji ekomeiekomei ni amɛjara mli.
Kɛji aaye afi lɛ, agbaa mɛi awalashihilɛ mli saji kɛtsɔɔ lalai kɛ wiemɔi amli ni akɛkaŋfoɔ mɛi ni egboi lɛ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi kɛ kaa he gbɛjianɔtoo kpakpa atsu nii kɛye ebua nikasemɔ lɛ.
Shwɛlɔi otii ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli lɛ haa anaa bɔ ni sane lɛ naatoo yɔɔ.
Sanemuu oti walɔ lɛ haa kanelɔi lɛ naa shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ ni etsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni nɔ fɛɛ nɔ he ni awieɔ he lɛ yɛ mli lɛ.
Wɔkɔ afii enyɔ ni wɔtsɔɔ nɔ ni feɔ amɛ srɔtoi kɛ nɔ ni haa amɛyeɔ hegbɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛji aatsɔɔ sanemuu oti yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
faa gbɛi (Nsaki, Tano, Densu, kn.)
Yɛ lalai lɛ alamɔ mli lɛ, mɔ ko woɔ lala lɛ ni fabii lɛ hereɔ nɔ.
Ayeɔ afi yɛ manyawalɛ nyɔɔŋ lɛ nɔ daa afi.
(N.k., wiemɔkulibii asaneshishitsɔɔmɔ, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ, Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ, kn.kn)
Nɔ hewɔ ni ŋaagbɛi lɛ ja kɛha shigbɛlɛmɔ sanebimɔ lɛ
Pɛimɔ tsakpaa ni yɔɔ omaŋ kɛ maŋ kroko ni kɔɔ kpokuafoaiŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ kusum tsumɔ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ye omɔ
Ehaa akɛ he woɔ mli jogbaŋŋ.
Atsɔɔ shishinumɔ yɛ Afoata he.
Esa ni ole yiŋtoo ni hɔɔ sane/niŋmaa l ɛ sɛɛ: Esa ni onu sane lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ dani obɔi shishitsɔɔmɔ’
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ GA NIŊMAA MLAI
Mlijaa 1 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ ewie wiemɔkulibii ni sa ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa mli, mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni akɛwieɔ naagba otii ahe.
Abaaha saneŋmaa he sanebim ɔi ejwɛ ni esa ak ɛ kaselɔi aŋma sanŋmaa ni hi ɛ wiemɔkulibii aaafee 300.
Kɛ je na lɛ, ashwɛɔ shwɛmɔi pii.
Kanemɔ Afoata sane ko.
Okɛ mfoniri ni aha bo ata adesa ko.
Yɛ nɛkɛ gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, Gyaasehene – niji maŋtsɛ ni fata maŋtsɛmɛi bibii lɛ enumɔ lɛ ahe yɛ Akuapem kɛ lumɛi krokomɛi lɛ tsɛɔ Banmuhene (Lumɛi afuuhe kɛ hiimeji lɛ anaadalɔ) lɛ kpee.
Kaselɔi ekomeiekomei aha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ sanemuu henɔ lɛ anɔ (ganee, nta kɛ haŋtsii) Kaselɔi atsɔɔ sanemuu henɔi ni akɛtsu nii yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
Ani wielɔi lɛ kɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsu nii yɛ amɛsanegbaa lɛ mli fɛɛ?
Tɔmɔi bibii kɛ wuji fɛɛ jeɔ kpo faŋŋ.
Kusumɔ nifeemɔ: Lalawiemɔ ko kusum lɛ feɔ hemɔkɛyeli nibii, wiemɔ lɛ shishijee, akutsoŋ nifeemɔi loo kuu ko mli ni asɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ yɛ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oŋma no akɛ sanemuu oti lɛ.
Kpa mfonirii kɛ wiemɔi haŋtsii lɛ ahe mama: Kwɛmɔ bɔ ni akɛ mfonirishamɔ (henumɔ nibii), no ni, tamɔ no.
Wiemɔ lalawiemɔ oti ahe.
Gbalamɔ blawa jebɔ lɛ mli yɛ Ga wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ mli.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ: Nyɛwiea lalawiemɔ ko he
Haramɔ nitssumɔ lalai henɔi enyɔ kɛjɛ okusum lɛ mli koni owie ejebɔ, eshikamɔ, (rhythm, mɛlɔdi) kɛ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi ni akɛtsu nii lɛ mli (no ni, tamɔ no, oookɛɛ gbɔmɔ, mfoniri, kɔnsonanti nɔtoonɔtoo, okadii kɛ nii kɛ nii).
Nyɔŋmɔjeŋ gbele: Gbele ni agboɔ kɛji ada loo hela ye mɔ ni etsɔ mli kɛ gbo.
Shwɛlɔi ahenɔi ji shwɛlɔ oti kɛ najiaŋwolɔ.
Awie nɔdoomɔ niŋmaa he
Hei ni asɔɔ yɛ:
Mlipɛimɔ kɛ Shishitsɔɔmɔ (mii 10)
Naabu shikamɔ: Enɛ tsɔɔ bɔ ni naabu shikamɔ lɛ yɔɔ haa, kɛji abɔɔ naabu lɛ tako loo kokloo aloo agbɛɔ naabu lɛ mli.
Ashaa egbɔmɔtso he ni ajaleɔ ehe (kɛjɛ enane shishi kɛyaa ŋwɛi).
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli Note
Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Kɛji eŋma otii etɛ ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ mli lɛ - mii 3
Otsi 20: Shwɛmɔ lalai NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
‘Hɛlmɛtiwoo: Esa akɛ abu hɛlmɛti kɛji aata moto loo baisiko nɔ.
Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 23 Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Otsi 10: Gbalamɔ sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji: nuu, googa ko, gbee agbo lɛ.
Gbɛi ni ashimɔ yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ mli ni abaanyɛ atsu he nii wɔsɛɛ lɛ yopomɔ
Yitso: Kɔnsonantii Agbɛhe Kɔnsonanti Gbɛhe Kɔnsonanti gbɛhe ji he pɔtɛɛ ni kɔnsonanti ko ka yɛ wiemɔkuli ko mli.
Enyɛɔ efeɔ awerɛho sane, kunimyeli, nifeemɔi ni yɔɔ fɛo, hiɛnɔkamɔ loo oshra k.n k.n.
Enɛ hako Ghana aye ehe kɛjɛ kukrudu shimɔ mli.
Gbalamɔ nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa otii kɛ jebɔi amli koni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii.
Ŋkpaiyeli mli tso
Tsɔɔmɔ klasi lɛ.
‘mitsɔɔ mijwɛŋmɔ, obaanyɛ owie kɛ okaditoo nibii komɛi tamɔ yitso hosomɔ tsɔɔ be mli ni eshɛ sanegbalɔ ko nɔ ni ebaawie.
Amɛkwɛ mlai ni yɔɔ he lɛ kɛtao tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ koni amɛkɛŋmɛ jaraŋ ni asusu he.
Gbɛigbɛɛlɔ Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛi
Lɛ ji klɛŋklɛŋ tsɔselɔ
Oketeke lɛ baa nɛɛ;
Ewa amɛ gbi ko shwane be mli ni akrowa lɛ ŋmɔji fɛɛ sha.
Ayeɔ aheɔ akɛ esaaa ni maŋnyo ni fataaa ku lɛ he lɛ naa amɛ yɛ nɛkɛ teemɔŋ nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli.
Agbala gbee mli, ni akwɛ ehenɔi lɛ amli (nɔ ni yɛ ŋwɛi, nɔ ni yɛ shishi, nɔ ni miiba shi, nɔ ni miiya ŋwɛi kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) kɛ amɛnitsumɔi
Nɛkɛ DoK ŋɛlɛi lɛ akpaa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi ajie yiŋkaa hesalɛ kɛ naagba naamɔmɔ hesalɛ kpo kɛ nu naagbai anaa.
Gbɛɛmɔ gbɛhe tsakemɔ tsɔɔ bɔ ni wielɔi tsakeɔ wiemɔ lɛ hiɛ bɔ ni afee ni etsɛmɔ afee mlɛo loo anyɛ anu shishi ni etsuɔ nitsumɔ kple yɛ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ nɔyaa kɛ wiemɔ lɛ tsakemɔ yɛ bei amli.
Kanemɔ sane ko koni nyɛsusu he.
Nikasemɔ otii: Nyɛtaoa mumɔi loo jemawɔji ni yɔɔ kpɔ lɛ mli agbɛi, hei ni amɛjɛ kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛhiɛ nakai gbɛii lɛ YITSO: Mumɔi, loo jemawɔji agbɛi Mumɔi/Jemawɔji Mumɔ ko, jemawɔŋ.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai : Kɛji saneshishitsɔɔlɔ ko kɛ nɛkɛ mlai nɛɛ tsu nii jogbaŋŋ lɛ, ebaanyɛ etsɔɔ sane fɛɛ sane shishi ni shishinumɔ ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli baafee faŋŋ no ji shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ baaye emuu.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, be ni akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔɔmɔ yitsei nɛɛ amli ata lɛ, amɛbaaanyɛ amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛna kɛjɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii ŋai lɛ amli lɛ adoo saji anɔ ni amɛnyɛ amɛtsɔɔ saji ashishi kɛba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ pɔtɛɛ ko mli hu.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi anishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ ahe.
Agbɛnɛ, jeee akɛ akutso fɛɛ akutso tsuuu nii yɛ nɔyeli kome shishi, ni no hu ha amɛna lalai ahe gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ nɔ hewalɛ waa.
Sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ waa kɛnaa sanemuu oti lɛ yɛ gbɛi nɛɛ anɔ:
(Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛgba yitso ko he kadaa, ni akɛmɛ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ ni amɛkɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ ni amɛjie nibii komɛi ni ejaaa gbɛ ni awie lɛ anaa).
Ehaaa mɔ kome too ahe sanegbaa lɛ kɛfɔ etsitsi.
Eji kadimɔ ni jieɔ he fɛo kpo, ehaa ofeɔ krɔŋkrɔŋ ni anaa ogbojee.
No sɛɛ lɛ aboɔ yaafo.
Ha gbɛiwiemɔi nyɔŋma kɛ najiaŋgbɛi enumɔ.
Henumɔ shihɛlemɔ: Bɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ teɔ henumɔ shi ni ekɔɔ kanelɔi ahe eko lɛ.
Saneshihitsɔɔmɔ he sɛɛnamɔi : Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ tsakpaa ko baa wiemɔi ateŋ, ehaa shishinumɔ baa jeŋ nilee he, ni ekwɛɔ ni shramɔ kɛ sanegbaa feɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Shwɛlɔ kroko hu ji Oyiŋ-etsɛɛɛ- tsɔmɔ, shwɛlɔ nɛɛ nyɛɛ edamɔ yiŋtoo kome nɔ.
Kaa Oti: DoK Ŋɛlɛ 3- Shia Nitsumɔ Daamɔ nilee ni ona yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe lɛ nɔ ni osɔ lalawiemɔ ni hiɛ kukuji etɛ yɛ otii nɛɛ ateŋ ekome nɔ
Gbalamɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli ni oha gbɛi etɛ komɛi anɔ ni ehe hiaa kɛha owiemɔ lɛ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ.
Yɛ kusum nifeemɔ nɛɛ mli lɛ, Okuapehene lɛ jieɔ ehe flo ni akɛ mama bihii komɛi fiɔ lɛ ni etswaa tu shii etɛ kɛtsɔɔ akɛ, akɛ kusum kɛ gbi lɛ eba naagbee.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔyɛɛ ni ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ yarafeemɔ nifeemɔi yɔɔ yɛ:
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛdamɔ nibii ni mɛi krokomɛi ewieɔ lɛ anɔ ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ yɛ he, kɛji amɛwieee kɛshiii lɛ, amɛbaafi wiemɔ lɛ sɛɛ kpɛŋŋ ni amɛbaawie he jogbaŋŋ.
Mɔ ko nyɛɛɛ akɔne eyiŋtoo yɛ sane ko he.
Akɛ kaselɔi baatsɔ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai amli hu.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hia kaselɔi jeee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli pɛ, shi ekɛ tsakpaa ko baa nikasemɔi tamɔ Blɔfo, kɛ maji krokomɛi anɔ wiemɔi lɛ hu.
Kwɛmɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli ni ohara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ mli lɛ.
Kwɛmɔ gbaa sane jebɔi lɛ koni otsu fɛɛ he nii.
Gbekɛ nuu legelege kɛ nuu kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Eka gbɛ aʋkɛnɔkwɛlɔ lɛ baanyɛ ekɛ eko agbe Ŋkpaiyeli gbɛjianɔtooɛ he.
Ekwɛɔ ni ayeɔ afi ni atsuɔ kusumi
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji najiaŋgbɛi koni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nii enumɔ.
Kɛ Ga yarafeemɔ kusum lɛ ato kusum krokomɛi yɛ Ghana ayarafeemɔ lɛ he.
Mɛi komɛi hu jɔɔ amɛhe yɛ amɛdekamɔ be mli.
Otii ni piaa sanemuji lɛ asɛɛ

Okɛ wiemɔ otii lɛ afee sanemuji ntai ni kɔɔ yitso ni akwɛɔ lɛ he.
Gbɛjianɔtoo ko kwraa pɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ he.
Yɛ shia mli lɛ gbekɛ kpakpa gbɛnaa nitsumɔi lɛ ekomɛi ji kɛji ote shi leebi lɛ osaa osaa ni oka nɔ lɛ dani
Daamɔ nilee yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii nɔ kɛkane niŋmaa ko ni oha sanebimɔi ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ ahetoo.
Mɛni ji osusumɔ yɛ nɛkɛ jwɛŋmɔ tsɔɔmɔ nɛɛ he.
Jebɔ : Eji lalawiemɔ lɛ shikamɔ.
Jaramɔ mligbalalɔi owo amɛhenɔi lɛ ashishi.

Akwɛ daa ni wa numɔ: Esa ni akwɛ ni akanu daa fee nine loo akpa numɔ kwraa.
Yigbelɔ: Yigbelɔ ji wiemɔkuli loo sanekuku ni kɛ gbeɔ shishinumɔ ni sanemuu mli lɛ yi.
Gbalamɔ mli ni otsɔɔ srɔto enyɔ ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii teŋ.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ akadi kaselɔi ni yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔ naagbai lɛ ni etsɔ ŋaagbɛ nɔ kɛku nakai naagba lɛ naa.
Ha afiyeli henɔi etɛ ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ kɛha eko fɛɛ eko.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ ja, emli ka shi ni ato naa jogbaŋŋ.
Kusum he nilee: Esa ni okwɛ wiemɔ mli ni otsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛyaa lɛ kusum kɛ amɛnifeemɔi koni otsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi nakai
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, ehaa kaselɔi nyɛɔ amɛyeɔ amɛhe nɔ: beiaŋ ni amɛmɛɔ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ tsɔɔ gbekɛbii lɛ tsuishitoo kɛ henɔyeli, ni ji nibii komɛi ni ahe hia jogbaŋŋ yɛ adesa walashihilɛ mli.
Afɔɔ yarafeemɔ Hɔɔ kɛ Soo.
Kaselɔi aŋmala wiemɔkulibii otii ni jejee kpo yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.
Gbɛi lɛ tsɔɔ akɛ tsakpaa ko ka mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ teŋ, ni anaa wɔŋ lɛ nifeemɔi komɛi yɛ mɔ ni akɛ gbɛi lɛ ewo lɛ lɛ he.
(Nk., shwɛlɔi, oti he ni awieɔ, naatoo, he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ, mɔ susumɔ/jwɛŋmɔ, henumɔ, k.n k.n) YITSO: Afoata mli ootui Afoata Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ akɛtsuɔ Nilee Saji ahe nii.
Esa akɛ atsɔse kaselɔi ni amɛŋmɛ amɛtsui shi ni amɛye amɛhe nɔ beiaŋ ni amɛmɛɔ ni eshɛ amɛnɔ lɛ.
Nɔ ni akɛyoɔ gbekɛ ko akɛ gbekɛ kpakpa ji egbɛnaa nitsumɔ.
Yarafeemɔ waa ni egbeɔ dɔlɛ ni yɔɔ mɛi amli be mli ni amɛsuɔlɔ lɛ egbo sɛɛ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ kɛji kaselɔi gbe mlijaa nɛɛ naa lɛ, amɛbaajie nilee yɛ nɛkɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ amli lɛ atsɛtsɛɛ wiemɔkulibii jogbaŋŋ ni amɛkane nii jogbaŋŋ hu.
Otsi 21: Ayɔse ni awie lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ ahe (lalawiemɔ lɛ mli wiemɔi, otii, kukuji, liamɔi, wiemɔheŋɔɔmɔwolɔi, shitiimɔi kn.kn)
Kaimɔ Tsutsu ko lɛ, ataa gbonyo lɛ shi yɛ tsu ko mli.
Nɔnyɛɛ ni enaa yɛ wiemɔ lɛ tsɔɔmɔ, ekasemɔ loo mlawoo he lɛ mligbalamɔ.
Julɔi etɛ lɛ nyɛ amɛjo foi yɛ be mli ni amɛkɛ polisifoi kɛ tu ta naa lɛ.
Yitso: Naajiemɔ Mligbalamɔi Enɛ ji gbɛjianɔtoo ni gbalaa nibii amli tsɔɔ.
Amɛduɔ ŋmaa (abele) ni no damɔ shi ha dioofeemɔ kɛ be ni akɛsɔleɔ kɛhaa maŋ lɛ.

OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU HENƆI
Okɛ kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi nɛɛ mli enumɔ afee: wiemɔmuji ni baa nɛɛ:
Mɛni wolo henɔ ohe lɛ?
Akɛkaiɔ bɔ ni fee ni atswa maŋ loo akutso ko ama shi, hiɛnyiɛlɔi kɛ kakalɔi ni damɔ shi kɛha hemɔkɛyelii ni amɛhiɛ mli lɛ.

ni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ wo henɔi lɛ ashishi.

Gbalamɔ Ga kɔnsonanti kpojee lɛ mli (e.g., wiemɔkuli shishijee, teŋ kɛ naagbee) Afi 2 Otsi 2: Gbalamɔ gbee nitsumɔ amli (e.g., sanemuu amli, wiemɔkulibii amli kɛ wiemɔi amli) OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: FƆNƆLƆJI GBƐJIANƆTOOI (GBƐƐMƆ NƆYELI GBƐJIANƆTOOI) Fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi mligbalamɔ: Fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ji ŋaagbɛi ni haa gbɛɛmɔi ni wielɔi kɛtsuɔ nii lɛ tsakeɔ bɔ ni afee ni wiemɔ afee mlɛo.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie shishinunmɔ kpo yɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ mli.
Sanemuu ni biɔ sane
Nikasemɔ ni eteenɔ yɛ okwaayeli nikasemɔ he hewɔ lɛ, naagbai babaoo ni yɔɔ bei ko lɛ naa ebashi kɛtsɔ nɛkɛ nikasemɔi nɛɛ amli.
Afoata tsɔɔ adesatamɔ nɔ kɛtsɔɔ mɔ lɛ su kɛ enilee yɛ yitso ko he.
Atsɔɔ kaselɔi lala henɔi srɔtoi lɛ: nitsumɔ lala kɛ shwɛmɔ lala.
Tamɔ liamɔi, kukuji kɛ liamɔi mligbalamɔ.
Gbekɛ yoo lɛ la Feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔi enyɔ loo etɛ akɛ yigbelɔ dani shishinuumɔ lɛ jeɔ kpo.Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii;
Week 11 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Okɛ kadilɔi/ sutsɔɔlɔi ahe mlai afee wiemɔmuji Yitso: Okɛ sutsɔɔlɔi/kadilɔi afee wiemɔmuu (Wiemɔmuji feemɔ kɛ kadilɔi /sutsɔɔlɔi) Sutsɔɔlɔi/Kadilɔi Sutsɔɔlɔi/kadilɔi ji wiemɔi loo nifeemɔi ni tsɔɔ mɔ ko loo nɔ ko su loo jieɔ kadimɔ ko kpo etsɔɔ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, be mli ni akaseɔ mlijaa nɛɛ he nii lɛ, kaselɔi baale amɛ kusum koni amɛ baayi.
Kɛji mɔ ni egbo lɛ shi eŋa yɛ ɛɛ lɛ, akɛ lɛ tsɔɔ okulafo kusum lɛ mli.
Srɔtoi yɛ niyenii ni ahaa amɛ lɛ mli
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ, wekumɛi kpeɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ GA NIŊMAA MLAI
Shitiimɔi : Eji ŋaa nɔ ni atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtsuɔ lalawiemɔ he nii, be pii lɛ atiɔ liamɔi komɛi amli ni eha lɛ lala su titri naagbee liamɔi lɛ.
Y ɛ shia mli l ɛ gbekɛ kpakpa gb ɛnaa nitsum ɔi lɛ ekomɛi ji kɛji ote shi leebi lɛ osaa osaa ni oka nɔ lɛ dani ojeɔ kpo, Kɛji oje kpo lɛ ŋa mɔ fɛɛ mɔ, onukpa jio, otipɛŋfonyo jio.
Okadi ni akɛyɔseɔ lɛ ji [j].
Hɔgbaa lɛ, Akuapem Krontihene lɛ bɔɔ gwa srɔto ko ni akɛmuɔ Odwira afiyeli lɛ naa.
Kɔsonanti ni haa gbeekpaa lɛ kpokpoɔ lɛ ekomɛi ji;
Ehe miihia hu akɛ ona hiamɔ nii ni sa lɛ, wolo ni sa lɛ, taomɔ ni ona akɛ adafitswaa lɛ yɛ nakai wolo lɛ mli koni ona baafa ni eyɔɔ lɛ.
Wiemɔ yarafeemɔ kusum ni afeɔ ahaa mɛi nɛɛ: maŋtsɛmɛi, wɔŋtsɛmɛi, yei hɔlɔi, gbekɛbii kɛ mɛi foji.
Amɛsusuɔ akɛ kaselɔi ni feɔ amɛshigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ yɛ woji kɛ niŋmaai amli lɛ shwereɔ fe ekrokomɛi lɛ ejaakɛ nikanemɔ hiaa yiŋkaa jogbaŋŋ.
Ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ tsɔ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ akɛ ehe esako tsɔ yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Yarafeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi: Yarafeemɔ he hiaa jogbaŋŋ yɛ maji amli.
Ato slaidii lɛ anaa jogbaŋŋ ni sa hiŋmɛiaŋ, niŋmaai lɛ faaa tsɔ yɛ slaidi lɛ nɔ.
Taomɔ otui komɛi yɛ mli.
Adebɔɔ mli oshrai: Adebɔɔ mli oshrai tamɔ la, nugbɔ ni naa wa, tsui ni ekumɔɔ egbeɔ mɛi anɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi hu kpataa mɛi awala hiɛ
Kɛji obaanya Afoata sane he lɛ, tsu nibii nɛɛ: GBƐJIANƆTOO NƆ LƐ SUSUMƆ KUDƆMƆ OTII 1 Mlinii (Nɔ ni) • Mɛni mlihalamɔ lɛ tsɔɔ?
Daaŋ agbafeemɔ: Eji gbɛjianɔtoo ko ni kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ ko naa lilɛi lɛ henɔwomɔ kɛninaa daaŋ agba lɛ kɛfataa he ni asɔɔ yɛ lɛ.
Ŋmalamɔ gbaa saneŋmaa mli otii lɛ.
Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ Apɛi wiemɔkulibii ni aha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛtsɔɔ blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo henɔ ni akɛtsu nii lɛ ni koni klasi lɛ agba he sane (tsɔɔlɔ aha nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi yɛ Ga mli)
Kɛ wiemɔkulibii hee ni osɔɔ lɛ atsɔɔ klasi lɛ ni awie he.
Saji lɛ aharamɔ (kɛyashi mii 5)
Gbɛi komɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa : Ayɛ gbɛi pii anɔ ni adesai baanyɛ amɛtsɔ nɔ kɛku gbele naa.
Nitsumɔ kɛ Jarayeli: Ehe hiaa jogbaŋŋ kɛha maŋsɛɛ nitsumɔ, jarayeli kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ ashɛɔ loo amajeɔ nibii.
Otsii kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi ni kɔɔ mlijaa nɛɛ he nɛ:
Ehaa lalawiemɔ lɛ ŋɔɔ ni etsakeɔ ejebɔ lɛ hu.
Kwɛmɔ kusumnaa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli koni osusu sɛɛnamɔi ni maŋbii kɛ shiabii naa yɛ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ ni ogbala wiemɔheŋɔɔwolɔi ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli tamɔ:
Tɔmɔi komɛi yɛ mli (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) yɛ slaidi lɛ mli.
Mɛi ni kɛ niyenii baa yɛ niyelihe lɛ kɛ gbɔi lɛ asanegbaa mli: niyenii,
Ehaa niŋmalɔi kɛ wielɔi kɛ gbee kɛ gbɛi srɔtoi anɔ tsuɔ nii yɛ niŋmaa kɛ wiemɔ mli.
Tɔmɔi lɛ faa tsɔ (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) Afutu mfonirii kɛ niŋmai lɛ ni akɛ sui kɛ nibii krokomɛi tsuuu nii jogbaŋŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni oto naa nakai lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ ni ji ŋkpaiyeli.
Gyaasehene lɛ faa Banmuhene lɛ koni eya koo lɛ mli, he ni gɔɔ lɛ yɔɔ lɛ, koni ekɛ hiimeji (Nananom) lɛ ayawie ni ekɛ Odwira lɛ ajɛ Nananom lɛ aŋɔɔ kɛbaha Okuapehene.
Sane mlipɛimɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ mlitsɔɔmɔ
Kpojee yɛ maŋjara nɔ
Yarafeemɔ ji gbɛ ni ahe awo mli akɛ akutsoŋbii lɛ atsɔɔ nɔ amɛkɛma amɛkɛ mɔ ni egbo lɛ tsakpaa lɛ nɔ mi ekoŋŋ.
Tsɔɔlɔ kɛ gbekɛbii asusu mlai ni kulaa kadilɔi/sutsɔɔlɔi kɛnitsumɔ hesanemuu feemɔ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ niŋmaa mli okadii ahe (omashi, alɔgɔ, boo okadi, omashi alɔgɔ)
Gbalamɔ kpokuafoi hetsuu nifemɔ lɛ amli (kusum ni afeɔ dani hetsuu lɛ jeɔ shishi, nbe mli ni nifeemɔ lɛ miiya nɔ kɛya nifeemɔ lɛ sɛɛ)
Nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa niŋmaa lɛ mli otii, ehaa anuɔ ŋmalɔ lɛ yiŋtoo lɛ shishi ni efiteee be hu.
No ji akɛ, enyiɛɔ sanemuu oti lɛ sɛɛ ni ebaa dani naamuu wiemɔi lɛ baa yɛ kuku lɛ mli.
No sɛɛ dani akɛɔ maŋbii kɛ mɔ ni egbo lɛ wekumɛi.
Nihoolɔi, gbonyo tsu mli saalɔi, lalatswalɔi, gbonyo adeka kpɛlɔi kɛ mɛi ni woɔ mɛi anaa ŋmɔlɔ naa shika waa yɛ yarafeemɔ mli.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Jwɛŋmɔ shikamɔ mli hewalɛ
Kwɛmɔ gbɛhe ni vaolii kɛ kɔnsonantii lɛ kamɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli.
Nɛkɛ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ haa tsɛrɛmɔ feɔ mlɛo ni edamɔɔ liŋwistiki nibii komɛi anɔ tamɔ ehaa gbɛɛmɔ lɛ sɔɔmɔ lɛ feɔ mlɛo yɛ fonɛtisi shihilɛ mli.
Nyɛtsua nii yɛ kuui amli
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ FƆNƆLƆJI 1.
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ shishi yeɔ ebuaa niiahishinumɔ kɛ niiahesusumɔ vii yɛ kaselɔi agbɛfaŋ.
Etsuɔ nii jogbaŋŋ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ mli ni ejieɔ hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ mli yɛ be kukuoo ni akɛha kuu mli (minitii 20).
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANESHISHITSƆƆMƆ HE MLAI Tii Saneshishitɔɔmɔ Mli Ekoŋŋ Kwɛmɔ otsi 13 kɛha saneshitsɔtsɔɔmɔ mligbalamɔ, ehenɔi kɛ ehesɛɛnamɔi lɛ.
Shia gbele: Enɛ lɛ mɔ kome loo mɛi enyɔ gbeɔ mɔ lɛ.
Yɛ jara lɛ nɔ (mɔ kome baanyɛ afee nihɔɔlɔ, mɔ kroko hu gbɔ ni eba shishramɔ kɛ mɔ kome hu ni miitsɔɔ gbɔ lɛ sane shishi kɛmiiba shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli)
Enɛ mɔɔ kanelɔ lɛ jwɛŋmɔ, Kɛkɛ lɛ okɛ oti lɛ efɔ jaraŋ, koni otsɔɔ nɔ pɔtɛɛ ni otaoɔ ofee.
Kɛji Banmuhene lɛ bashɛ pɛ, etsiɔ ebɛŋkɛɔ Okuapehene lɛ, ni akɛ mama haa amɛyiteŋ, hemɔkɛyeli lɛ ji akɛ Banmuhene kɛ Okuapehene pɛ naa nɔ ni hiimeji lɛ keɔ amɛ lɛ.
Week 2 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii
Kɛ nilee ni ona kɛjɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee.
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ enumɔmli lɛ - mii 4
Feemɔ sanemuji yɛ henɔi nɛɛ anɔ:
Gbalamɔ shwɛmɔ otui lɛ amli.
Akɛ saneshitsɔɔmɔ nikaselɔ lɛ, aha bo shɛɛ wiemɔ ni tsɔɔlɔnukpa lɛ baawie yɛ osukuu lɛ Naawiemɔ kɛ Nikee nifeemɔ lɛ shishi ni esani atsɔɔ shishi.
Ha toibolɔi nibii ni tee nɔ fɛɛ lɛ amligbalamɔi loo nibii ni otsɔmɔ mli ni maa sane lɛ nɔ mi.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ He Ŋai
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /a/ nyɛɔ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ sane ko ni jieɔ yitso lɛ kpo.
Ebaawo kaselɔi ahewalɛ koni amɛfee ekome kɛ nya kusumi nifeemɔi ahe.
Otsi 7: Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he)
Tsɛ Ataa: Tsɛ Ataa ji weku kɔklɔɔ lɛ yitso ni lɛ, eŋa kɛ ebii yɔɔ mli lɛ.
‘sukuyaa ji samfee ni gbeleɔ shweremɔ agbo lɛ’ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ni yɔɔ kuku lɛ mli lɛ.
Anyɛɔ akɛ yoo lɛ haa nuu lɛ nyɛmi uu ni ekwɛ bii lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ ahewɔ ni nitsulɔi ni yɔɔ okutso lɛ mli lɛ kɛ nitsumɔ lalai tsuɔ nii.
Saji lɛ anɔdoomɔ (mii 5)
Ebɔɔ mɔdɛŋ akɛ atsɔse yinɔbii ni baa lɛ koni amɛnu tɔkpaa lɛ shishi ni amɛbaa gboshi nibii lɛ ayi.
Ewebii lɛ naa lɛ akɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kpakpa NB: Tsɔɔlɔ aha kaselɔi ale akɛ nyɛmɛi hu amɛgbɛnaa nitsumɔ yɛ weku lɛ mli..
Enaa kpɔiaŋ tuamɔ kɛ hewalɛ
Tɛkinɔlɔji: Akɛ tɛknɔlɔji etsake nibii pii ahiɛ yɛ yarafeemɔ mli, ajieɔ yara lɛ yɛ t.v nɔ, afɔɔ nine atsɛɔ mɛi yɛ intanɛti lɛ nɔ ni ashiɛɔ yara lɛ tete po yɛ nɔ ni mɛi pii naa he anuɔ.
Mɛni ofeɔ yɛ odekanamɔ mli?
blawa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi) SANEŊMAA WIEMƆSHISIHTSƆƆMƆ KƐ
Mɛi kpanaa ni yɔɔ wɔkustei lɛ amli ni wɔnaa lɛ fɛɛ etsɔmɔ sukuu tsɔsemɔ lɛ mli da.
Kusumi nibii kɛ hesaamɔ
Blema ko lɛ, akɛ daa yaa gɔɔ mli.
Tswɛi tsaa yɛ shi kɛ ŋaashi
Helɔ/nɔnalɔ: Helɔ loo nɔnalɔ ji gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi ni naa nɔ lɛ loo nifeemɔ ni yɔɔ sanemuu lɛ mli lɛ yaa shiɔ lɛ lɛ.
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi Gbɛkpamɔi DOK ŋɛlɛ Kaa Ŋaagbɛ/ henɔ Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) 3 Tsuŋ nitsumɔ Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee 4 Pɔtufolio
Saamɔ okane ni okwɛ tɔmɔi ni yɔɔ ogbaa sane lɛ mli ni osaa fɛɛ.
Amɛkwɛɔ amɛweku kɔklɔɔ lɛ mli bii ahiamɔ nibii fɛɛ.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Gbele He Kusumii Onukpai kpeɔ kɛji mɔ lɛ gbee shi nɔŋŋ.
Haramɔ nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛ yɛ okusum lɛ mli ni ogbalaa nitsumɔ lalai henɔi lɛ amli.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ YarafeemɔMlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ
Baatsɔɔ yɛ klasi muu lɛ hiɛ.
Hogb ɛtsotso Afiyeli: Akɛkaiɔ Ewemɛi agbɛfaa wulu kɛjɛ Notsie ni ji ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ Togo lɛ.
Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni ayɔɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
Kojo ye omɔ kɛ flɔɔ.
Ekoŋŋ yɛ Odwira da Jufɔ lɛ, sɛi shiai kpawo lɛ naadaalɔi lɛ baajieɔ yɛlɛ hee ni afa lɛ kpo.
Faa lɛ teŋ gbɛ kwɔlɛ ha mitsitsi yashi ŋmɔtɔ kpɔ ko.
Nikasemɔ Nitsumɔ Saamɔ ohe ni oha agba kadaa ni ji awie aha loo awie ashi yitso lɛ: “Eji oblahii kɛ oblayei lɛ agbɛnaa akɛ amɛkɛ amɛhe baawo shikwɛɛ afiyelii lɛ amli bɔ ni afee ni amɛkusum lɛ akalaaje.” Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Ekoŋŋ lɛ, ebaawa kaselɔi ni amɛtsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ susumɔi ni ahe hia jogbaŋŋ kɛ nɔ ni he ehiaaa tsɔ yɛ sanegbaai amli.
Ŋaagbɛi ni akɛbaasa naatsii lɛ anaa IV.
Kaselɔ kɛ nilee kɛ shishinumɔ ni ana atsu ŋkpaiyeli kɛ yarawoo lala kusumi lɛ.
Feemɔ nɔyeli tso ni maŋtsɛmɛi kɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nyɛmaŋ lɛ mli.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Henɔi
Mikaiɔ nifeemɔi kɛ nibii fɛɛ ni Adadzewa kɛ mɛi krokomɛi ni fata he lɛ jie lɛ kpo lɛ.
Fantemɛi yɛ Eyipa/sikasa, Dɛtseyi/fundahɔ kɛ fea.
Enɛ baaha ni niyenii aba babaoo.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Mɛi enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ nitsumɔ:
Otsi 14: Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mlai lɛ ahe (n.k., nilee yɛ wiemɔi ni abaatsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi kɛya kɛ wiemɔi ni atsɔɔ sane lɛ shishi yɛ lɛ, kusum he nilee, wiemɔ mli akpɔi kn.kn) NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Mɛi ni ashiɔ yɛ sɛɛ lɛ naaa yelikɛbuamɔ ni mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ kɛhaa amɛ lɛ dɔŋŋ.
Nikasemɔ oti: Atao kusumnaa nɔyeli kuramɔi srɔtoi lɛ (Papa, wekuyitso, weku tɔkpaa yitso) YITSO: Kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ gbɛjianɔtoo (Shia) Kusumnaa Gbɛjianɔtoo Eji nɔyeli ko ni shikwɛɛbii kɛ amɛnilee sharaa, kɛkuraa, kɛtsɔɔ he kɛ mliwoo gbɛjianɔtoo nɔ.
Kuu fɛɛ kuu faa kɛyaa hei srɔtoi fe ekrokomɛi, ni no hewɔ lɛ gbɔmɛi kɛ shihilɛi srɔtoi na eko fɛɛ eko nɔ hewalɛ.
Gbekɛbii (gbekɛ + bii)
OTI: WIEMƆ KƐ NITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Sanenaajiemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi kɛ ehe mlai lɛ atsu nii kɛtsɔɔ niŋmai komɛi ashishi.
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu Mlijaa 5 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ wie sanemuu henɔi lɛ ahe.
Oti lɛ ji atsuu Oguaamaŋ lɛ he ni ashwie mumɔi fɔji fiaa.
Kloyosikplemi Afiyeli: Yilo Krɔbɔbii ji mɛi ni yeɔ kɛkaiɔ amɛgbɛfaa wulu kɛjɛ Kloyomi (Krɔbɔ gɔŋ lɛ) kɛbashɛ he ni amɛyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Kɛfata he lɛ, esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi lɛ adamɔ kaselɔi ahiamɔ nibii anɔ ni ekudɔ enitsɔɔmɔ lɛ tamɔ eto be srɔto eha amɛ, ekɛ yelikɛbuamɔ nibii atsu nii, etsake nitsumɔ lɛ hiɛ loo eha nanemɛi aye abua amɛhe kɛji efi.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni sanemuu henɔi nɛɛ waa kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ ni yɔɔ nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ kpo.
Oshrai anaakuu: Esa ni ato lajee he gbɛjianɔ, akɛ nibii ni woɔ la aka bɛ ŋkɛ nibii ni jeɔ la.
Ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ lɛ, nakai shihilɛ lɛ bɛ dɔŋŋ ejaakɛ nibii babaoo etsake shi moŋ oboade nifeemɔi yɛ, mɛi ni efeee.
Week 10 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii(s): Okɛ gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋ gbɛi ahe mlai afee wiemɔmuji Yitso: Gbɛiwiemɔ (najiaŋgbɛi) kɛ sanemuu feemɔ Gbɛiwiemɔ Gbɛiwiemɔ ji wiemɔ ko ni yeɔ gbɔmɔ, nɔ ko, susumɔ ko loo he ko najiaŋ..
Akɛ nilee ni ana yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he lɛ ayɔse susumɔi otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ni adoo nɔ lɛ mli.
Kpɔiaŋgbɔlemɔ: Esa akɛ wɔfɔ wɔkpɔiaŋ gbɔlemɔ, wɔ fɔ nyiɛmɔ, wɔ fɔ foijee loo kpɔiaŋgbɔlemɔi nifeemɔi srɔtoi feemɔ.
bɔ ni obaakane nii lɛ, kɛ shishi ni obaana yɛ oti lɛ he, ŋaagbɛ nɔ ni akɛ ŋma, bɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi kɛ ŋaagbɛi krokomɛi tsu nii.
Gbalamɔ Ga vaoli kpojee lɛ mli.
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ ateŋ ekpaa mli lɛ - mii 6
Mɛi enyɔenyɔ lɛ atsɔɔ nɔ ko kɛtsɔ nɔ ni amɛkase lɛ nɔ.
Daamɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wieɔ anɔ: Klasi muu fɛɛ nifeemɔ
Mlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa MliMlijaa 1: Wiemɔkulibii Ni Akɛtsuɔ Nii Yɛ Sanegbaa Mli kɛ sanegbaa kɛ wiemɔ mli aya hiɛ pam.
Weku yitsei: Mɛi nɛɛ ji mɛi ni wekumɛi abii ehala akɛ amɛweku hiɛnyiɛlɔ ni anaa amɛ akɛ weku yitsei.
Saamɔ kɛ kwɛmɔ ekoŋŋ.
Abifao lɛ miifo
Hii ni yɔɔ shia lɛ mli lɛ tereɔ gbonyo lɛ kɛyaa gɔɔ lɛ mli, wekumɛi nyiɛɔ sɛɛ ni ayatoɔ lɛ.
Aŋmɛ gbɛi srɔtoi kɛha nifeemɔ kɛ gbɛtsɔɔmɔ (aŋmɛ kaselɔi agbɛ kɛha sanebimɔi ahetoo kɛtsɔ wiemɔ, niŋmaa, nitɛŋmɔ nɔ).
Oblayei lɛ laa lalai srɔtoi ni ekomɛi kɔɔ gbalashihilɛ mli ŋɔɔmɔi kɛ joomɔi ahe.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Kaa Klasi muu nitsumɔ
Gbonyo Nɔ Womɔ Awoɔ gbonyo lɛ adeka mli.
Nyɛsɛɛ haa kotsa (taakotsa akwabii ejwɛ) bosaa kɛ abonuai
Kaselɔi baanyɛ ayɔse naagba ko hu ni amɛtao hetoo kɛ tsɔɔ shishi fitsofitso.
Mlijaa 9: LalaiMlijaa 9: Lalai Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
Atsɔɔ kaselɔi wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi fɛɛ ni ayɔɔ lɛ ni atsɔɔ amɛ emli nɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ Ga mli.
Kanemɔ sane ni baa nɛɛ ni okadi sutsɔɔlɔi ni yɔɔ mli.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANEMUU OTI K Ɛ SANEMUU OTI WALƆ
Anyɛɔ akɛtsuɔ nii hu kɛsaa blawa shikamɔ ni ejaaa gbɛ hu, titri ji wiemɔkulibii ni ama.
Otsii kɛ nɔkwɛmɔi ni mlijaa lɛ wie he:
Ku nitsumɔ: Awie gbele henɔi lɛ ahe yɛ kusum naa.
Mɔ sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔi ji sutsɔɔlɔi loo kadilɔi ni akɛsusuɔ mɔ ko.
Eyeɔ ebua maŋ lɛ nɔyaa hu kɛtsɔ nitsulɔi ni he esa tamɔ tsɔɔlɔi, datrɛfoi, maŋhebulɔi kɛ onukpai yɛ akutsei amli.
Shia sɛi nɔtamɔ loo yɛ tɛ diŋ nɔ
gbɛjianɔtoi lɛ fɛɛ,jwɛŋmɔi ni ohiɛ loo otii amli ni ogbalaa.
Akɛ nu la juɔ ehaa gbonyo lɛ shii etɛ.
Kɛ tsalɔ henɔi ni ayɔɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ afee sanemuji.
Mlijaa 2 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ baakwɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni akase yɛ otsii enyɔ ni eho lɛ mli.
Nifeemɔ: Blema, kusumɔ kɛ mɔ diɛŋtsɛ nifeemɔi anɔ ni adamɔ nɔ kɛŋma lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Nyɔŋmɔ ajɔɔ Ghana maŋ lɛ kɛ emli bii fɛɛ daa gbi.
No ji niŋmalɔ lɛ adafitswaa ni sanemuu lɛ hiɛ ni afɔɔ no kpo jiemɔ faŋŋ yɛ yitso lɛ mli loo anaa lɛ yɛ sane lɛ mli wiemɔi komɛi amli.
No tsɔɔ maŋ loo akrowa loo shihilɛhe kɛ be nɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ ba nɔ.
Sanetso mlijaramɔ Okɛ sanemuu oti lɛ aŋmɛ jaraŋ, Sanemuu oti walɔi, kɛ naamuu loo naamuu hewalɛwoo wiemɔs.
Ebaanyɛ efee agwaseiaŋ nɔ, tipɛŋfoi anɔ, lalawiemɔ henɔ, loo sanegbaa nɔ.
Ŋɛlɛ 4 Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ:
Kɛji etsi mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi kpawo ni yɔɔ ŋɛi lɛ fɛɛ mli lɛ - mii 7
Nyɛtɛŋa mfoniri ko ni shaa otii lɛ fɛɛ ni awie lɛ mfoniri.
Tsɛŋ kɛ tsweitsaa yɛ hiɛ
Jaramɔ gbɛiwiemɔi nɛɛ amli owo kɔsuaŋgbɛi, kwamaŋgbɛi, gbɛisisa/kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi kɛ gbɛi ni anyɛɔ ataa he
Fetu Afahyɛ Fetu Afahyɛ ji afi ni Oguaamɛi kɛ amɛmaŋtsɛmɛi lɛ yeɔ yɛ Teŋ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ.
Afiyeli beiaŋ ji be ni mɛi kɛkaiɔ mumɔi ni he ba sɛɛnamɔ kɛha akutso kɛ maŋ lɛ.
Mii ni yɔɔ he ji nyɔŋmai enumɔ (50).
Nitsɔɔmɔ (Gbɛjianɔtoo) Ato slaidii lɛ anaa jogbaŋŋ, eyɛ fɛo ni akɛ, ato niŋmaa lɛ naa jogbaŋŋ, mfonirii ni niŋmaai lɛ hu faa tsɔ.
Yɛ gbɛ nɛɛ nɔ lɛ kanelɔ lɛ jwɛŋmɔ baanyiɛ sane lɛ sɛɛ Akɛ klɛŋklɛŋ mɔ najiaŋgbɛi”mi” ni fɔɔ ŋmaa koni ŋmalɔ lɛ loo kanelɔ lɛ ana ehe loo toiboloi ana lɛ akɛ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ehe sane ni.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ OTSI 14 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ mli mlai lɛ ahe (Nk.
Afoata sane lɛ henyamɔ lɛ nɔdoomɔ: Mlinii (Nɔ ni) Mɔsusumɔ/Jwɛŋmɔ (Namɔ) Oti (Mɛni hewɔ) He ni nɔ lɛ ba yɛ (Nɛgbɛ kɛ Mɛɛ be)
Ghanamaŋ lɛ mli yɛ fɛo hewɔ lɛ gbɔ babaoo basaraa wɔ daa afi.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Ŋaagbe He Sanebimɔi Kuuŋ nitsumɔ
Atsɔɔ nɔ akɛtswaa nitsumɔ lɛ ahe adafi.
Gbɛ srɔto nɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ, titri, wiemɔkulibii hee lɛ tsuɔ nii yɛ tsake baa bɔ ni gbɔmɔ flo ko kɛ nakai wiemɔkulibii lɛ nɔŋŋ tsuɔ nii yyɛ sanegbaa mli.
Kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛdamɔ nibii ni mɛi krokomɛi ewieɔ lɛ anɔ ni amɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ yɛ he, kɛji amɛwieee kɛshiii lɛ, amɛbaafi wiemɔ lɛ sɛɛ kpɛŋŋ ni amɛbaawie he jogbaŋŋ.
Okɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai akane niŋmaa ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii 400 koni okɛ nilee ni ona lɛ adoo susumɔ gbajaa/ otii lɛ anɔ.
Atsɛrɛɔ kotsai lɛ amli ni afolɔɔ abonuai lɛ amli ni akɛyaa hejuuhe.
Jwɛŋmɔ mli hewalɛnamɔ yelikɛbuamɔ: Esa akɛ abi yelikɛbuamɔ kɛjɛ jwɛŋmɔ mli hewalɛnamɔ nitsulɔi loo yelikɛbuamɔ kui adɛŋ.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ ni mli kwɔ
Gbɛkpamɔi krɛdɛɛ ni he hiaa shigbɛlɔmɔ lɛ.
Hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ haa okaneɔ nii babaoo ni onaa suɔmɔ ohaa nikanemɔ.
Haramɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ Ga mli ni otsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli Sɛɛnamɔ Hɔmɔwɔ afi ni Gamɛi yeɔ lɛ tsɔɔ nikpamɔ wulu be ni amɛŋɔɔ shidaa kple amɛhaa wɔwɛi lɛ nikpamɔ babaoo lɛ hewɔŋ.
Susumɔ ŋkpaiyeli mli otii enyɔ otsɔɔ.
OTI: JEŊ NILEE Oti Mlijaa: WOJIAŊ SAJI Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe lɛ anya lalawiemɔi ahe Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ lalawiemɔi ahe kpo atsɔɔ HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAALƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ wieɔ wojiaŋ asaji he.
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ: Agba afiyeli kusum he sane
Gbɛɛmɔ Mlihɔlɔmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ ji fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo ni haa akɛ gbɛɛmɔ hɔlɔɔ wiemɔkuli mli bɔ ni afee ni anyɛ atsɛ loo kɛsaa eblawa shikamɔ lɛ.
OTI: KUSUM NIFEEMƆI KƐ MAŊKURAMƆ 99 Oti Mlijaa: Kusum Nifeemɔi 99 OTSI 15 101 Yitso Oti Ni Akwɛɔ: Afiyeli 101 OTSI 16 106
Akɛhaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ yaawɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Gbɛɛmɔ Laajemɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ ji fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtoo ni haa gbɛɛmɔ ko laajeɔ yɛ wiemɔkulibii amli yɛ he pɔtɛɛ yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Ahaa nuu onukpa ni toɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ.
Sanemuu haŋtsii ni wieɔ sukuuyaa he sɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Vaolii komɛi jeɔ wiemɔ shishi, ekomɛi kaa teŋ ni ekomɛi gbeɔ wiemɔkuli naa.
Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Kɛ niŋmaa okadi ‘ A, B, D…’ kɛtsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ shitiimɔ henɔi lɛ amli.
Sanemuu ni akɛfaa Sanemuu: Sanemuu ji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ kɛyaa ni akɛtsa ni shishinumɔ yɛ he ni akɛjieɔ oti ko he loo jieɔ shɛɛwiemɔ ko kpo.
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 7 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalamɔ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ amli (ama wiemɔi, nɔtoonɔtoo, asɔ, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he) Kaimɔ Afi 2 Otsi 5: Akwɛ afisi nitsumɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
Tsɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lalao, tayaa lalai, shwɛmɔ lalai kɛ abifaowɔlemɔ lala 1 1 WOJIAŊ SAJI 1.
Gbɛkɛbii lɛ mii shwe bɔɔl yɛ agona lɛ nɔ gbɛkɛ nɛɛ.
Oti: Jeŋnilee ni kɔɔ Ghana wiemɔ he kasemɔ Oti mlijaa: Akrɔkuaŋ saji/ŋaaŋ saji Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ:
Abuɔ nɛkɛ gbele henɔ nɛɛ akɛ oshra kɛji jeee mɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ fo ewala nɔ loo hela ni gbe mɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ komɛi ni akwɛɔ kɛji aatsɔɔ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa ko mli.
Odwira Afiyeli Lɛ
Nɔnalɔi niyɔɔ sanemuu mli (nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ loo helɔ) (direct or indirect): Akwei ha lɛ pɛn (nɔnalɔ diɛtsɛ loo helɔ).
Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Sanebimɔi
Sanemuu nta ni wieɔ afiyeli he
Jaramɔ kɔnsonantii ni baa nɛɛ (b/, /d/, /g/, /t/) amli owo kui ni nyiɛ sɛɛ lɛ amli:
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ OTSI 18 Excellent (7-8 marks) Very Good (5-6 marks) Satisfactory (3-4 marks) Needs improvement (1-2 marks) Mlinii Awieɔ otii lɛ ahe.
Liamɔ mlitsɛrɛmɔ: Eji hei yɛ lalawiemɔ mli ni liamɔ hee tsaa nɔ yɛ he ni liamɔ ni eho lɛ gbeɔ naa yɛ lɛ.
Nyɛsusua otii ni yɔɔ gbaa saneŋmaa mli.
Okɛ hetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoi ni akɛkpeɔ mɛi floŋŋ kɛbaa amɛnukpatsɔmɔ mli yɛ akutsei srɔtoi amli yɛ Ghana lɛ atotoi he.
Enyɛɔ egbalaa wiemɔkulibii krokomɛi ashishinumɔ yɛ sanemuu lɛ mli.
Kɛji abaagbele gbɛ ni ji gbi ni akɛ dioofeemɔ kusum lɛ baaba naagbee lɛ afeɔ kusum ko ni atsɛɔ lɛ odadao nɔshwiemɔ.
Kukuji: Eji liamɔi ni ajara amɛmli kɛwo kuui amli, ni yɛ hiɛ shitiimɔ henɔ kome.
Yɛ osusumɔ mli lɛ, ani ehe hiaa akɛ aya nɔ akɛ maŋtsɛmɛi amaŋkuramɔ atsu nii NB: Tsɔɔlɔi akwɛ akɛ kaselɔ atsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛkɛɔ nɔ ni amɛkɛɔ lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ blawa jebɔ ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli
Maŋtsɛmɛi, onukpai kɛ Okuapemanbii yakpeɔ yɛ Mpeniase – (tso klɛŋklɛŋ ni adu be mli ni Akropɔŋ batsɔ Okuapeman maŋ wulu lɛ.
Naagbai komɛi ni baa niyenii saji ahe kɛji kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ mli tsake.
Afee enɛ koni kaselɔi ale amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛkusum ni amɛnya mɛi krokomɛi anɔ he hu.
Shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ (akɛ wiemɔkulibii fioo ko pɛ kɛkɛ shaa nibii babaoo ahe mfoniri)
Mlijaa 10: Lalawiemɔ OTSI 23 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Lalawiemɔ henyamɔ Kaimɔ Afi 1 Agba mɔnaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he sane be mli ni aakwɛ toiboo veveeve kn.kn.
Bo naawiemɔ ni aatswa loo aakane toi koni ojie naa kɛba owiemɔ mli.
Esa akɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ afee faŋŋ: Bɔɔ mɔdɛŋ ni sane / niŋmaa ni otsɔɔ shishi lɛ afee faŋŋ ni ekahiɛ shishinumɔi srɔtoi.
Mɔ ni mɛi nɛɛ kɔɔ akɛ enyiɛ amɛhiɛ lɛ batsɔɔ nɛkɛ tɔkpaa nɛɛ hiɛnyiɛlɔ ji yitso lɛ.
Biɛ lɛ, esa akɛ aŋmɛ kaselɔi agbɛ kɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔi yɛ gbɛ kpakpa nɔ yɛ nɔ ni amɛnanemɛi ahetoo loo susumɔi ahe.
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu henɔ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ amli
Ayeɔ kakaiɔ loo babaoo ashamɔ yɛ ŋshɔ lɛ mli, ni amɛfeɔ kusum kɛdaa wowɛi 77 ni yɔɔ Oguaamaŋ lɛ shi, ni amɛtsɔɔ no nɔ hu kɛsaa amɛmaŋ lɛ mli.
Akɛ daa tamɔ shinapu, weŋ, akpɛtɛshi, ŋmadaa, kɛ ekrokomɛi lɛ shwieɔ shikpɔŋ ni ajaoɔ kɛwoɔ mumɔi lɛ atoii anɔ ni afaa amɛ koni amɛfee nɔ ko pɔtɛɛ amɛha yɛ nɔ hewɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai lɛ he.
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ boo okadi lɛ muɔ sanemuu henɔ nɛɛ naa.
OTI: SANEGBAA Oti Mlijaa: Nikanemɔ Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ nilee ni ona kɛjɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mlitsɔɔmɔ lɛ ajie otii akpo yɛ niŋmaa mli.
Liamɔi: Eji wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛ yɛ kpaa kome nɔ ni feɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ.
Maŋ lumɛi lɛ shwaa kpokpoi, niyenii ni akɛyeɔ afi lɛ.
Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi Henɔi Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi: ji fɔnɔlɔji ŋaagbɛ ni baa kɛji gbɛɛmɔ ko kɔ gbɛɛmɔ ni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ su ni ekɛ tsakemɔ baa esɔɔmɔ kɛ efeemɔ mli.
Kɛji mɔ ko he kpɔ yɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii mli lɛ, eyeɔ ebuaa enikanemɔ jogbaŋŋ.
Haramɔ Wiemɔ Heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi lɛ Taomɔ wiemɔ heŋɔɔmɔwolɔi komɛi tamɔ:
Nɔ hewɔ ni okɛ ŋaagbɛ lɛ tsu nii lɛ.
Nikasemɔ Otii: Yarawoo lalai (Enitsumɔ kɛ sɛɛnamɔi).
Eji hesalɛ gbɛ ni akɛsɔɔ nibii dani afeɔ.
Otsi 13: Awie sanehishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ ahe.
Sanefa he shishinumɔ lɛ waa ni onaa hesalɛ yɛ niŋmaa kɛ wiemɔ mli ni.
Tsɔɔmɔ nitsumɔ lalao, tayaa lalai, shwɛmɔ lalai kɛ abifaowɔlemɔ lala 1 1
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ sanemuji henɔi lɛ amli: kɛɛmɔ, famɔ, kɛ sanebimɔ sanemuu ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Yiŋshwiemɔ Eji susumɔ ni ona yɛ nɔ ko he shi jeee ni ole akɛ no ji anɔkwale lɛ loo oyɛ he nilee.
Tsɔɔlɔ baanyɛ ekɛ betsɔɔlɔ hu akai kaselɔi lɛ kɛji mɔ ko ewie eta ni eshɛ mɔ kroko nɔ ni ebaawie.
Otsi 18: Abaatsɔɔ srɔtoi kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔi yeɔ hegbɔ yɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kui srɔtoi lɛ ateŋ.
Bei komɛi lɛ akɛ jieɔ mɔ ni egbo lɛ yi Yarawoo lalai ahesɛɛnamɔ
Dani abaaju gbonyo lɛ he lɛ, weku lɛ kɛ kotsa, papam, samla, paada kɛ hejuu nibii krokomɛi baa ni akɛjuɔ ahaa lɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ kɛ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ teŋ ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
NITSƆƆMƆ ŊAAGBƐ LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni aksɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ kɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi babaoo ni akɛtsɔɔ nii yɛ Ghana wiemɔi amli lɛ tsuɔ nii.
Yɛ kusumii komɛi amli lɛ wekuyitsei lɛ kpeɔ ni amɛwoɔ amɛteŋ mɔ kome ni lɛ etsɔɔ tɔkpaa lɛ yitso.
Kwɛmɔ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ mli nilee ni okɛtao sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ he kɛ nɔ ni ehiii yɛ nikanemɔ nɛɛ he.
Susumɔ nibii etɛ ni atsi ta lɛ he kukubɔ.
Ebaanyɛ efee mɔ ni jaje loo toibolɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: datrɛfonyo kɛ helatsa ko sanegbaa mli: tsofa,
Ekɛ nɔyaa bahaa emaŋ lɛ
Biɛ lɛ, ekɔɔ kakla ni ekɛgbeɔ tsina lɛ ehaa wɔji lɛ.
Nifitemɔ nɛɛ ha nijiaŋwujee ba ni mɛi bɛ hiɛnɔkamɔ dɔŋŋ fe hefimɔ.
Wiemɔkulibii hee lɛ ka shi Wiemɔ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ tipɛŋfoi aŋɛlɛ lɛ ka shi, tamɔ daadaa wiemɔkulibii hee tamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni afɔlɔ nɔ.
Ekɛ hegbɛi tamɔ ekomefeemɔ kɛ hiɛnyiɛmɔ baa.
Gbaa sane gbɛkpamɔ ji akɛ akɛ gbɛjianɔtoo kpakpa ni ato aha adesatamɔ atsu nii, tamɔ sane lɛ yitso, hekɛhɛlɛmɔ, Ga sɛɛsɛɛ, kn.
Mlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji GbɛjianɔtooiMlijaa 2: Fɔnɔlɔji Gbɛjianɔtooi kuu ni afutu kaselɔi lɛ kɛ klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nitsumɔ kɛ klasi lɛ nitsɔɔmɔ.
Odwira gbi Ju Yɛ Akan kusum naa lɛ, hemɔkɛyeli ko yɛ akɛ maŋtsɛmɛi gbooo, moŋ amɛyahiɔ akrowa ko.
Tswiamɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mligbalamɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ.
Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ yiŋtoo: Lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ yiŋtoo kɛ shɛɛwiemɔ lɛ.
Gbɛkpamɔ lɛ ji akɛ, yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ naagbee mli lɛ, amɛkɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli ŋaagbɛi lɛ baatsu nii kɛwie naagba otii wuji ahe, ana nilee ni sa kɛsɔ wiemɔkulibii ni shishinumɔ yɛ he, atsɔɔ srɔto ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii shishinumɔ teŋ ni amɛnyɛ amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa agba sane.
Bei pii lɛ, sanemuu oti lɛ ji wiemɔ ni jeɔ kuku lɛ shishi (shi jeee be fɛɛ be).
Helai: Helai tamɔ HIV/AIDS, sikli hela, atridii, musuŋkɔɔ, tsui hela kɛ ekrokomɛi lɛ haa mɛi wala ŋmɛɔ amɛ kɛji amɔɔɔ naa.
Mɛi / kui srɔtoi baa ni amɛbashiɔ kpe kɛyashiɔ enɔ jetsɛremɔ be ni sa akɛ ayafu gbonyo lɛ.
Kanemɔ sanemuu walɔi ni baa nɛɛ ni ojwɛŋ nɔ ni ji oti ni awieɔ he lɛ he.
Shika gbɛfaŋ naagbai: Gbele kɛ nyɔmɔ baa titri lɛ yarafeemɔ mli nyɔmɔi, helatsamɔ mli nyɔmɔi, mɛi anitsumɔ mli nyɔmɔwoo nyɛɔ eŋmɛɔ amɛ tete po.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔ lɛ abɔ mɔdɛŋ ni eha mɔ fɛɛ mɔ kɛ ehe awo nikasemɔ lɛ mli, ekɛ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii ni eha kaai ni sa beiaŋ ni nikasemɔ lɛ yaa nɔ lɛ bɔ ni afee ni eye ebua kaselɔi lɛ.
Shigbeemɔ naakuu: Esa ni ato gbɛjianɔ kɛku shigbeemɔ naa, tamɔ abaafee nibii ni abaanyɛ amɔmɔ mli kɛji aanyiɛ loo aakwɔ nii loo anyiɛɛɛ nibii ni shaneɔ anɔ.
Tsɔɔmɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ jemawɔji agbɛi etɛ he.
Sanemuu nyiɛɔ jebɔ pɔtɛɛ ko nɔ, tamɔ feelɔ-feemɔwiemɔ-helɔ (SVO)
Gbalamɔ onɔ lɛ mli.
Mikɛ hekpokpomɔ sha foi kɛje kpo kɛjɛ faa lɛ mli.
Eji wiemɔkuli ni tsɔɔ wɔ nɔ ni feelɔ lɛ feɔ loo nɔ ni ninaa feelɔ lɛ.

Mfonirishamɔ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni akɛ wiemɔ shaa nibii komɛi ahe mfoniri yɛ nikanelɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mli.

OTI: WIEM KƐ ENSITSUMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Wiemɔ Mlai Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Apɛi sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli.
Enɛ hewɔ lɛ, amɛhaa lɛ nibii fɛɛ ni ehe hiaa gbɛfalɔ.
Mɛɛ gbɛi anɔ he ni nɔ lɛ ba yɛ he hia yɛ niŋmaa mli?
Feemɔsane ko yɛ kadaŋgbamɔ he
Mɛi nɛɛ feɔ ekome kɛyeɔ naagbai anɔ, ni amɛpleɔ nibii ni baaha amɛ miishɛɛ kɛ ninamɔ he.
Enɛ ji kusum nifeemɔ ko ni tsɔɔ Akuapem ekomefeemɔ.
toiŋjɔlɛ, suɔmɔ, hiɛmiamɔ, gbele, awerɛhoo kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Akɛni eji yɛlɛyeli afi lɛ, ahaa hiimeji lɛ niyenii kɛtsɔɔ akɛ niyenii ebu.
Eho be ni ato lɛ he fioo.
Kɛji eba yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ teŋ.
Afiyeli ji Ghana kusum ni he yɔɔ sɛɛnamɔ jogbaŋŋ, ebuaa mɛi anaa ni ekɛ ekomefeemɔ baa.
Agbele rɛjista henɔi lɛ ahiɛ atsɔɔ kaselɔi ni atsɔɔ amɛ shishi.
Sanemuu ganee Bei pii lɛ, anaa tipɛŋfoi awiemɔkulibii yɛ sanemuu ganeei amli, tamɔ sanemuji ni yeko emuu kɛ wiemɔkulibii kukuji ni akɛfɔlɔɔ sanegbaa mli Akɛ mɔdiɛŋtsɛ gbee tsuɔ nii Nɛkɛ wiemɔ henɔ nɛɛ jieɔ wielɔ lɛ henumɔ kɛ esu kpo.
Gbɛisisa loo Kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi: Gbɛisisa loo Kɔɔyɔɔŋgbɛi ji gbɛiwiemɔ ni akɛtsɛɔ nibii ni anaaa kɛ hiɛŋmɛi loo anyɛɛɛ he ata kɛ niji tamɔ ekpakpafeemɔ, miishɛɛ, kɛ jalɛsaneyeli.
Agba lalawiemɔ otii lɛ ahe sane
Tɔmɔi komɛi tamɔ (tsɛrɛmɔ, niŋmaa mlai, niŋmaa mli okadii, niŋmaa lɛ anaatoo, k.n.k.n) bɛ slaidi lɛ mli.
Okuapehene lɛ tsɔɔ nibii ni etee nɔ yɛ maŋ lɛ mli afi ni eho lɛ mli ni etsɔɔ nibii ni abaafee afi hee lɛ mli ni kɛ nɔyaa kɛ shweremɔ baaba.
Ekɛ wiemɔkulibii hee ni ja tsuɔ nii Agwaseiaŋ wiemɔkulibii kɛ wiemɔkulibii kuku ni sa nɔ he ni awieɔ lɛ tsuɔ nii Sanemuji haŋtsii amli Bei pii lɛ, anaa agwasieaŋ wiemɔkulibii yɛ sanemuji haŋtsii amli.
Wiemɔi ni akɛtsu nii: Wiemɔkulibii ni lalawiemɔfolɔ lɛ kɛtsu nii, sintasi kɛ bɔ ni eŋma enii lɛ eha.
