Ŋmaa sane yɛ omɔdɛŋbɔɔ ko nI ohiɛ mɛɔ he kɛ gbɛ nɔ ni otsɔ kɛfee he.
Atsɔɔ susumɔi kɛ shishinumɔ ni ana yɛ ŋkpaiyeli kɛ yarawoo lala he.
Nɛkɛ gbɛgbamɔ nɛɛ tsɔɔ akɛ hiimeji (Nananom) lɛ baabafata amɛhe kɛye Odwira lɛ yɛ Okuapehene maŋtsɛwe lɛ.
Enɛ ha mikai nɔ ko ni ba nakai be lɛ mli.
Hetsuumɔ Dani abaabote gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ mli Gbaa nɔ ni sa feemɔ Gbaa nɔ ni sa dani aje nifeemɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ shishi.
Wiemɔkuli, sanefa, sanekuku, niŋmaa mli okadii, najiaŋgbɛi
Klasi muu fɛɛ sanegbaa: Agba gbele he sane.
Oshrai: oshrai tamɔ tsɔji oshrai, nu miimɔ, loo shigbeemɔ kɛjɛ tsu kakadaŋŋ yiteŋ kn, lɛ ebaanyɛ egbe mɛi.
Gbɛkɛ ŋmɛji kpawo mli lɛ, Okuapehene kɛ maŋtsɛmɛi bibii enumɔ lɛ – Gyaasehene, Benkumhene, Nifahene, Adontenhene kɛ Kurontihene yaa sɛi tsuji lɛ mli ekomeiekomei ni amɛyawajeɔ amɛkita kɛha Ofori Kuma Sɛi lɛ.
Tsu nii kpakpa koni onya hewalɛ kɛ nibii kpakpai.
Mɛni ji jemawɔŋ.
Naatoo: Eji nifeemɔi ni tee nɔ yɛ adesa lɛ mli lɛ naatoo.
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti walɔi ni nikanemɔ lɛ mli lɛ.
Kɔnsonantii komɛi ni atsiɔ kɔɔyɔɔ lɛ naa kɛsɔɔ.
Nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ yarafeemɔ he lɛ baaye ebua kaselɔi ni amɛbaale amɛkusum kɛ mɛi krokomɛi anɔ.
Ha sɛɛnamɔi etɛ ni kpokoafoiaŋ hetsuumɔ kɛ bɛjemɔ lɛ kɛbaa.
Kuui lɛ aka amɛyiŋ kɛgba gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔi tamɔ naabu kokloo feemɔ, daaŋagba feemɔ, gugɔjiaŋgbɛɛmɔ feemɔ kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ he sane.
NB: Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ baanyɛ etao mfonirii krokomɛi yɛ ŋkpaiyeli he efata mfonirii lɛ ahe kɛye abua kaselɔi koni amɛnu nikasemɔ lɛ shishi jogbaŋŋ.
Feelɔ: Sanemuu lɛ feelɔ ji gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi loo gbɛiwiemɔ kuku ni sanemuu lɛ wieɔ he lɛ.
Gbalamɔ sɛɛnamɔi ni oha lɛ mli etɛ otsɔɔ.
Gbaa saneŋmaa jebɔ Hiɛgbelemɔ: Enɛ gbalaa mɔ jwɛŋmɔ kɛbaa sane pɔtɛɛ he ni awieɔ lɛ.
Ga, Afuamaŋ, Nsakina) Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ nyɔji lɛ ahe (nk.

KAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ otsii 9, 10 kɛ 11.
Aawo tsɔɔlɔi lɛ ahewalɛ bɔ ni afee ni amɛkɛ nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋai ni haa
Nilee ni yɔɔ nitsumɔ kɛ shwɛmɔ lalai ahe lɛ baawa kaselɔi ni amɛhebaasa kɛji amɛmiifo amɛdiɛŋtsɛ amɛ lalai.
Mlijaa nɛɛ he hia kɛha bo jogbaŋŋ akɛ kaselɔ, jeee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ kasemɔ pɛ hewɔ shi moŋ ekɛ tsakpaa ko baa nikasemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ Blɔfo wiemɔ lɛ kɛ wiemɔi krokomɛi lɛ amli.
Tamɔ ɔkwɛmɔnɔ nɛɛ, sanemuu oti ni yɔɔ kuku ko mli yɛ saneŋmaa ko ni yitso ji “Sɛɛnamɔ yɛ sukuuyaa he” lɛ baanyɛ afee “Nitsumɔ ni awoɔ nyɔmɔ jogbaŋŋ ni anaa lɛ ji sɛɛnamɔi ni he hiaa fe fɛɛ ni yɔɔ sukuuyaa he lɛ ateŋ ekome.”
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii etɛ ni afeɔ kɛji agbe hetsuumɔ kusum oti lɛ diɛŋtsɛ naa.
hewɔ lɛ atsɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ nɔ kɛsaa kɔnsonanti ni nyiɛ amɛsɛɛ lɛ naa.
Tsɔɔlɔi baanyɛ amɛkɛ mfoniri ni fata he lɛ awo nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ obɔ NB: Tsɔɔlɔi atao mfonirii ni sa afata he kɛtsɔɔ nii lɛ.
Tamɔ, kɛji otee datrɛfonyo loo helatsamɔhe lɛ, wɔkɛ wiemɔkulibii komɛi kpeɔ ni wɔkɛtsuuu nii yɛ wɔdaadaa sanegbaai amli.

Bo toi jogbaŋŋ

Niahefɛlemɔ Feemɔ Practice Paper 2 ni yɔɔ Otsi 18.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ Kwɛmɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛhe ni /b/ ka yɛ wiemɔkulibii ni baa nɛɛ amli:
Kwɛmɔ wiemɔi lɛ tsɛrɛmɔ kɛ hidɔɔ saji ni yɔɔ mli lɛ.
Enɔ jetsɛremɔ leebi lɛ, amɛbuaa amɛhe naa ekoŋŋ koni amɛhe nikee.
Abi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ana nilee yɛ gbɛjianɔtoo, nibii, tsakemɔi kɛ kaa he ŋai ni yeɔ ebuaa kaselɔi lɛ.
Gbakamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni baa nɛɛ amli:
Ehaa anaa nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni sa akɛ ana kɛ nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni sa akɛ ana.
Kɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii atsu nii kɛhara otii ni yɔɔ nikanemɔi aml.
Nitsumɔ lala: Nitsumɔ lala ji lalai ni kɛ nitsumɔi hiɛ tsakpaa.
Tsɔɔmɔ kusumnaa nɔyeli shikamɔ yɛ shia mli.
Ashɛɔ eyi ni ajaleɔ ahe.
Aatao atsɔɔ akɛ mɔ lɛ egbo moŋ shi hiɛnɔkamɔ ji akɛ eyɛ.

Sɛitsɛ Lumɔ nɛɛ ji mɔ ni kuraa maŋtsɛ sɛi lɛ ni etsuɔ he kusum.
Tsɔɔlɔi akwɛ ni amɛyi gbɛ ni kɔɔ he ni amɛyɔɔ lɛ kusum lɛ nɔ.
Yɛ kui etɛtɛɛtɛ amli lɛ, nyɛkaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ yɛ nifeemɔi srɔtoi amli tamɔ (yɛ jara lɛ nɔ, helatsamɔhe loo sukuu) nyɛtsɔa wiemɔi loo sanekukuji komɛi ni nyɛfɔɔ nuu lɛ ashishi.
Tɔmɔi bibiibii komɛi yɛ niŋmaa mlai lɛ mli ni bei komɛi lɛ eji tɔmɔ oti.
Ewie lalawiemɔ jebɔ he.
Agbenaa shi milaaje mitu lɛ.
(Shishitsɔɔlɔi/ tsɔɔlɔi atsɔɔ gbele shishi tamɔ bɔ ni eyɔɔ yɛ amɛkusum lɛ mli lɛ) Nibii komɛi ni kɛ gbele baa
Kanemɔ nɔ ni baa nɛɛ ni otsɔɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi ni yɔɔ mli lɛ.
Kɛsaa he kɛjie shwɛmɔ kpo kɛtsɔɔ bɔ ni mɛi kɛ wiemɔi ni bulɛ yɛ he gbaa sane kɛtaoɔ akutso lɛ naagbai lɛ atsabaa.
Ni yoo ni bɛ wu kɛ bii lɛ, etsɛsɛɛ weku fuɔ lɛ adeka
Gbalamɔ nifeemɔi etɛ ni anaa yɛ mɔ hetsuumɔ kusumfeemɔ lɛ mli.
Abi ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ana nilee yɛ gbɛjianɔtoo, nibii, tsakemɔi kɛ kaa he ŋai ni yeɔ ebua kaselɔi lɛ.
Hela nɔkwɛmɔ: Esa ni mɛi ni yɔɔ helai komɛi tamɔ sikli hela loo tsui hela lɛ akwɛ amɛhe nɔ jogbaŋŋ ni amɛjwɛŋ nɔ amɛkɔlɔɔ amɛtsofai lɛ.
Awie shwɛmɔi hesɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe.
Wɔji komɛi agbɛi: Gua, Oyaadu, Gbɔbu, Sakumɔ, Naaye, Dantu, Kɔɔle, Afiee, Klɔte, Densu kɛ ekrokomɛi.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ jieɔ shishinumɔ yɛ abifao kpojiemɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli fitsofitso.
Etsuɔ sɛi lɛ kusumi
Tsɔɔmɔ Afoata mli otui lɛ.
Kɛji akɛ Ɔmanhen tsuŋwoo nifeemɔ lɛ ba naagbee lɛ, ebaa maŋjaranɔ ni eyaa sɛi tsu lɛ mli ayayeɔ ŋkpai ekɛbaa jɔɔmɔ kɛjɛɔ Oguaamaŋ wowɛi 77 lɛ adɛŋ, amɛhemɔkɛyeli ji akɛ, amɛ amɛkwɛɔ Oguaamaŋ lɛ nɔ.
Wiemɔ nɔ ni ataoɔ lɛ kpoo koni okɛ nibii ni baama nɔ mi afata he.
Anaa lɛ akɛ nɔnalɔ yɛ feemɔwiemɔ gbɛfaŋ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ ji: Oblanuu kakadaŋŋ lɛ ehe tsɔne hee.
Nitsɔɔmɔ mlinii lɛ ja ni ato naa jogbaŋŋ.
Mii 3 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnii Mii 2 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnii Mi 1 kɛha gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ ateŋ ekome kɛ ehe nɔkwɛmɔnɔ
Kanemɔ sanekuku ko koni oŋmala sutsɔɔlɔi kɛ kadilɔi ni yɔɔ mli.
Mlijaa nɛɛ haa wɔnuɔ he haa mɛi ni wɔnyɛɔ wɔtsɔɔ wɔjwɛŋmɔ yɛ blema nifeemɔ, kusum nifeemɔ kɛ akutsoŋ nifeemɔi amli.
Be mli ni mifee miyiŋ akɛ mabote faa lɛ mli lɛ, mikɛ mitu lɛ ma minaa kɛ sɔlemɔ, mishane kɛbote ŋmɔtɔ lɛ mli.
Jiemɔ osusumɔi lɛ akpo otsɔɔ klasi lɛ ni agba he sane.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔi Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Afiyeli shishitsɔɔmɔ: Afiyeli ji kusum nifeemɔi ni ku loo maŋ ko nɔ bii feɔ kɛgbaa blema sane loo mumɔŋ nifeemɔi kɛtsɔ wiemɔi, lalai kɛ jooshwɛmɔi anɔ.
Esa akɛ ona tsui ni oye ohe nɔ be mli ni omɛɔ lɛ.
(Kaselɔi lɛ eko fɛɛ eko atsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ)
Tsɔɔmɔ nibii ni asusuɔ he kɛji aanya Afoata sane he.
Yɛ gbɛi komɛi anɔ lɛ kɛji mɛi ni ebote gbala mli naaa bi afɔ lɛ, amɛyashwaa akɛ kɛji jemawɔŋ lɛ ha amɛ bi ni amɛfɔ lɛ, amɛkɛ abifao lɛ baawo jemawɔŋ lɛ.
Wiemɔnta : Wiemɔkulibii enyɔ loo etɛ ni akɛtsaa kɛfeɔ wiemɔkuli hee.
Akɛ nibii fɛɛ ni ahe hiaa lɛ woɔ mli.
Ehaa anaa akɛ oje gbekɛbii ateŋ kɛyabote onukpai amli
Wiemɔkulibii ni asɔ
Sanemuu ganee ni wieɔ Ghana niyenii he
Kɔ sane ni naa shɛɔ wiemɔkulibii ohai enumɔ ni kɔɔ yitsei nɛɛ ateaŋ ekome he.
Afii ejwɛ nitsumɔ ni European Union to shishi lɛ kwɛ gbɛ ni ni abaatsɔ ni shweremɔ aba okwaafoi anidumɔ kɛ nikpamɔ mli kɛtsɔ shikataomɔ gbɛjianɔtoo komɛi amli kɛha okwaafoi ni yɔɔ Sub-Saharan Afrika lɛ.
Daamɔ nibii ni akɛsusuɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ ni okɛsusu Ga vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ (N.K., lilɛi shikamɔ, naabufeemɔ, lilɛi henɔwomɔ)
Ŋmalamɔ wiemɔkulibii ni okɛbaatsu nii yɛ onitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Enɛ hewɔ lɛ, mɛi efaa gbɛ kɛjɛɛɛ shɔŋŋ kɛ amɛhe ebaawooo yarafeemɔ mli dɔŋŋ.
Yɛ henɔ nɛɛ shishi lɛ, atsɔɔ wiemɔkuli lɛ aŋkroaŋkro ashishi yɛ niŋmaa lɛ mli, akwɛɛɛ yiŋtoo, ŋaa loo wiemɔ ni atsɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ shishi lɛ kɛyaa lɛ kusum.
Kanemɔ niŋmaa ko ni emli wiemɔkulibii shɛɔ 300 loo nɔ ni fe nakai ni otsɔɔ shishi.
Ani niŋmalɔ lɛ aloo shwɛlɔi lɛ ateŋ mɔ ko?
Ekɛ hegbɛi tamɔ, nitsumɔ mli ekomefeemɔ, kuuŋ nitsumɔ kɛ hiɛnyiɛmɔ hesalɛ fɛɛ baa.
Kusum krokomɛi ahe nilee: Ehaa hegbɛ ni mɛi kɛtsɔɔ amɛjwɛŋmɔ, saji kɛ nilee ni kɔɔ mɛi krokomɛi akusum he.
Ŋaagbɛi komɛi ni yɔɔ mli ji ankroankro nikasemɔ, kuu enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ,
Gboshinii Eji kusum ni afeɔ dani ajara mɔ ni egbo lɛ jwetri loo nibii ni eshi yɛ esɛɛ.
Nɛgbɛ sukuubii lɛ yaa?
Bei pii lɛ, asɔɔ wiemɔkulibii hee nɛɛ kɛtsimɔ wiemɔkulibii afɔji komɛi ni bɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ Nɔkwɛmɔnii
Naamuu: Ewieɔ loo emaa sanetso ni yɔɔ sane kuku ni ji ekome lɛ nɔ mi yɛ nibii pɔtɛɛ hewɔ ni agbaa sane lɛ.
Nifeemɔ ni damɔ wiemɔ lɛ nɔ (mii 5)
Otsi 5: Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli
(nk., hiɛ, teŋ kɛ nagbee).
Kaa ni kɛ nikasemɔ gbɛjianɔtoo yaa Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔi DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa henɔ L1.1.4.4.1 Awie nitsumɔ lalai ahe 4 Sanegbaa L1.2.3.4.1 Atao ni anya ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ shwɛmɔ lalai ahe.
(Nibii komɛi ni sa akɛ okadi: kwɛmɔ bɔ ni kaselɔi lɛ kɛ amɛhe sharia yɛ heshibaa mli, bɔ ni amɛbiɔ saji ni kɛ hɔɔ amɛ atrɔma kɛ bɔ ni amɛfeɔ kɛji aheee amɛjwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ lɛ awooo mli, kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ amɛjwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔi ŋmɛɔ jara).
KAA NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛɔ otsii 7 kɛ 8.
Daamɔ nibii ni ja nɔ kɛtsɔɔ vaoli gbɛɛmɔi lɛ amli (tamɔ., naabufeemɔ, lilɛi henɔwomɔ kɛ egbɛhe)
Okɛ onukpai ni yɔɔ akutso lɛ mli ana sanegbaa loo mɛi ni baatsu kusum lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ hu.
Polisifoi lɛ yati amɛnɔ be mli ni amɛjaa nibii ni amɛyaju lɛ.
Tii Afiyeli nikasemɔ lɛ mli ekoŋŋ
Ebaawo kaselɔi ahewalɛ koni amɛtsa yaa kɛye afii lɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ.Nuu lɛ wie waa diɛŋtsɛ.
Kɛ nilee ni ona yɛ sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ mli lɛ ajie susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ nikanemɔi amli lɛ akpo.
Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔɔ shishitsɔɔlɔ lɛ diɛŋtsɛ ŋaa nɔ Nɛkɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔ nɛɛ gbɛi kroko ji “overly free loo loose translation” nɛkɛ ŋaa nɛɛ jeɔ niŋmaa lɛ jebɔ loo eshishinumɔ lɛ ŋlɛ nɔ kwraa.
Ejee yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli pɛ shi yɛ Blɔfo kɛ nikasemɔi krokomɛi tamɔ Arabic kɛ French.
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛii etɛ anɔ ni abaatsɔ kɛyɔse susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko mli.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Tsɛrɛmɔ sanemuji nii baa nɛɛ amli.
Mɛi ni eŋmala nii yɛ shigbɛlɛmɔ lɛ he ni okɛtsu nii lɛ atatsii jogbaŋŋ.
Akɛ nibii ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ.
Tsɛ afii enumɔ komɛi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana kɛ mɛi ni yeɔ.
Nuu lɛ kwɔ ni etaloo hu.
OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SANESHISHITSƆƆMƆ
Mɛni hiɛdɔɔ loo henumɔ yɔɔ sane lɛ mli?
Tsɔɔmɔ nɔ hewɔ ni wiemɔkulibii ni ohara lɛ he hiaa kɛha onitsɔɔmɔ lɛ.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ shishinumɔ kpo yɛ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi yɛ Ga mli.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti WalɔMlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ
Yɛ Ŋɛlɛ 3 lɛ, ahaa niiahesusumɔ vii he kaa kɛtsɔɔ niŋmaa kukuji loo kakadaji anɔ kɛkaa kaselɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mlikwɔlɛ kɛ shishinumɔ ni amɛyɔɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ henyamɔ he kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ otii lɛ baatsu nii kɛnya lalawiemɔ ko he.
Akɛ sui kɛ mfonirii etsu nii jogbaŋŋ.
Kaselɔi atsɔɔ wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ashishi yɛ kui enyɔ enyɔ mli.
Hɔmɔwɔ Afi lɛ ji afi ni Gamɛi yeɔ.
Abaawie lalawiemɔ henyamɔ hu he.
Kusumnaa nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ jeɔ shishi yɛ wekukɔklɔɔ mli.
Ehaa wɔnaa lɛ faŋŋ ni wɔnuɔ gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ shishi faŋŋ .
Akɛ kusum kɛ kristofoi ajamɔŋ hemɔkɛyeli efutu yarafeemɔ: Bianɛ lɛ, afutu yara kusum nifeemɔ kɛ kristofoi ajamɔŋ mli hemɔkɛyelii, eyafeɔ sɔlemɔ ahaa gbonyo lɛ yɛ sɔlemɔtsui amli, kpeshii he saji kn.kn.
Kukuji abɔ ni yɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Akuntaabuu tsɔɔlɔ kɛ ekaselɔi yɛ nikasemɔ tsu mli: ajiekɛjɛmli, abɔhe,
/a/, /e/, /ɛ/, /i/, /o/, /ɔ/ kɛ /u/, /b/, /d/, /f/, /g/, /h/, /j/ kɛ /k/
Nɔ ni akwɛɔ lɛ damɔ bɔ ni akɛ lalawiemɔ mli otii lɛ sɔɔ lalawiemɔ srɔtoi kɛ ehenɔilɛ, kɛ amɛhe nyamɔ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnii ji: agbo, miishɛɛ tɔ, eyɛŋ futaa.
Aakpa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi aha fɛɛ ahetoo.
Eji hegbɛ ni anaa akɛ je lɛ jaa loo gbaa nibii ni amɛfee amɛha amɛmaŋ lɛ.
Esa akɛ mɔ fɛɛ naa deka kɛji etsu nɔ akpaa gbɛ yɛ edɛŋ lɛ egbe naa.
Galamsey: ɛskaveta, nu, shika tsuru, tsɔnei, nui, ojarawatɛ, shikpɔŋŋ, kɛmika, kɛ nii kɛ nii
Abaagbele wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ ekomɛi ahiɛ atsɔɔ amɛ dani abaatsɔɔ amɛ nɔ ni yɔɔ Ga mli lɛ.
Kwɛmɔ gbɛnɔ ni niŋmalɔ lɛ kɛ niŋmaa mli okadii lɛ tsu nii yɛ, kɛ ehesalɛ.
Jaasetsɛ Ga maji pii woɔ ni mɛi komɛi hu woo jasetsɛ.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔ be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ yɛ sanemuu mli.
Kɔsuaŋgbɛi ji gbɛi ni akɛtsɛɔ mɔ, maŋ, akrowa, nitsumɔhe loo nikasemɔhe ko.
Kpatu gbele: Enɛ lɛ jeee dalɛ loo hela gbeɔ mɛi lɛ.
Ehaa ewebii lɛ anitaomɔ nibii
Atofotsɛ Awoɔ lumɔ nɛɛ ni etoɔ maŋtsɛ wulamɔ nibii fɛɛ, hei pii lɛ fotro hu.
Wiemɔ ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ tsakemɔi ni eba yarafeemɔi amli lɛ.
Afɔ helatsamɔhe lɛ sramɔ: Esa akɛ afɔ helatsamɔhe lɛ sramɔ kɛha gbɔmɔtso lɛ mlikwɛmɔ koni kɛji hela ko etee yɛ gbɔmɔtso lɛ mli lɛ, ana mra ni aku naa.
Ekwɛɔ nɔ ni nitsumɔ lalai ji, ehenɔi kɛ ehe sɛɛnamɔi.
Tsɔɔmɔ otii nɛɛ ashishi
Abaaha sanesɛɛkɔmɔ ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii 300-350 yɛ adesa nɔ ni sanebimɔi nyɔŋma yɛ eshishi.
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sanegbaa ko mli.
Wowɛi anibimɔ: Afiyeli ji be amli ni mɛi kɛ amɛnaagbai kɛ nibimɔi shwieɔ wowɛi lɛ ahiɛ koni amɛna amɛ mɔbɔ ni amɛye amɛbua amɛ.
Gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ haa wɔnuɔ tsakemɔ ni baa wiemɔi amli ni ejeɔ tsakemɔ ni akutso loo kuu ko kɛbaa gbɛɛmɔ lɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ, najiaŋgbɛi ni damɔ feelɔ gbɛhe lɛ akɛ enii ni efeɔ (feemɔwiemɔ) lɛ fataa ehe ni aŋmaa lɛ akɛ wiemɔkuli kome.
Nyɛsusua oblayei kɛ hii ahetsuumɔ gbɛjianɔtoi ni haa anaa mɔ ko akɛ onukpa he (mɔ floŋŋ onukpatsɔmɔ gbɛjianɔtoo: dani, beiaŋ, kɛ no sɛɛ).
Wiemɔkulibii nɛɛ ekomɛi ji, tsofai, atridii, nɛɛsifonyo, helasaa, sɛtoskopu, kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Kɛji amɛku amɛsɛɛ amɛba lɛ, amɛkɛ amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛhe, sɛii kɛ hewulamɔ nibii lɛ fɛɛ ni atsuu amɛhe lɛ baahaa Okuapehene.
Kuŋbii lɛ eko fɛɛ eko wiemɔ yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Nitsumɔhei wuji hu tsɔɔ no nɔ amɛkɛhɔɔ amɛ nibii kɛji amɛpia nifeemɔi lɛ ni amɛ hu amɛkɛ nibii hee ba jaraŋ.
Feelɔ yɛ sanemuu mli: nkn., Kofi tee sukuu .
Enɛ ji feelɔ yigbelɔ.
sanekukuji kɛ sanefafai)
Akropɔŋ fɛɛ feɔ dioo be mli ni blafoi woɔ sɛii kɛ Okuapeman hewulamɔ nibii kɛtsɔɔ Akropɔŋ gbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛyaa Ademi mu (blema faa) kɛha amɛhe tsuumɔ.
Baatsɔɔ klasi lɛ onitsumɔ lɛ.
Ehaa anaa tsakpaa ni ka mɔ ni hiɛ gbɛi lɛ kɛ jemawɔŋ lɛ teŋ.
Nibii ni akɛfuɔ gbonyo: Fantemɛi heɔ amɛyeɔ akɛ gbele lɛ gbɛfaa ni, ni mɔ ni egbo lɛ yaatsa eshihilɛ lɛ nɔ yɛ gbohiiajeŋ.
Aku nu futuɔ kaŋfa ni akɛmaa enaa kɛbɛjeɔ lɛ.
Hei ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ yɛ: Hei komɛi ni asɔɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ yɛ ji naabu shishi kɛ ŋwɛi, naabu kɛ nyanyɔŋ, nyanyɔŋ, agba bɔdɔɔ, agba keketee, lilɛi lɛ sɛɛ kn.
Enyɛɔ eyafeɔ nibii komɛi ni anaa ahe susumɔ kɛtsɔɔmɔ, gbɛɛmɔi, fu, nibii aŋɔɔmɔ kɛ joomɔ kɛ nibii asu.
Kaselɔi yɛ kuui amli aha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔi ni awie he lɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi ni amɛpɛi mli.
Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ Mlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti WalɔMlijaa 3: Sanemuu Oti Kɛ Sanemuu Oti Walɔ
Amralofoi ni yɔɔ no beiaŋ, National Democratic Congress lɛ bɔ ehe mɔdɛŋ wo mlai ni naa wa akɛ eeekpɔ maŋ lɛ kɛbadamɔ enaji anɔ lo.
Buamɔ oshishinumɔ lɛ naa: Daamɔ onilee yɛ otii ni yɔɔ nifeemɔi lɛ amli lɛ nɔ kɛbua nibii ni oyɔse yɛ lalawiemɔ mli kɛna shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ mli.
Odwira Afiyeli Lɛ Maŋtsɛmɛi kɛ mɛi ni jɛ Fanteakwa yɛ Boka kpokpa lɛ nɔ yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ.
Ŋmaa gbaa sane ni hiɛ mlijaramɔi etɛ yɛ yitso ko he.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ he sanebimɔi kɛ gbɛkpamɔi (kɛyashi mii 4)
Yɛ Shikagbɛfaŋ: Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ ji be ni akutsoŋbii lɛ yeɔ jara kɛbuaa shika naa kɛhaa amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛhe ejaakɛ mɛi babaoo ji mɛi ni baa akutso lɛ mli.
Otsi 9 kaa otsi lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ, otsi 10 kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 4 nɔ ni otsi 11 he kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ.
KAA HE NƆDOOMƆ Kaa ŋaagbɛ ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ ŋmɛɔ kaa ŋaagbɛi krokomɛi kɛha hesalɛnamɔ, shishinumɔ ni mli kwɔ kɛ yiŋkaa gbɛ.
/u/: Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ
Agbala henɔi bibii ni yɔɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ ashishi lɛ ni akɛ nɔkwɛmɔnii fi sɛɛ.
Doomɔ sane mlijaramɔi etɛ ni yɔɔ sane ko mli nɔ.
HIƐGBELEMƆ KƐ MLIJAA LƐ NƆDOOMƆ Mlijaa nɛɛ baawie gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he yɛ kusum nifeemɔi kɛ maŋkuramɔ yitso lɛ shishi.
Naagbai nɛɛ kudɔɔ mɛi asusumɔ ni enaa nɔnyɛɛ yɛ amralo lɛ mlai kɛ enifeemɔi amli ni ejieɔ tsakpaa ni ka maŋbii kɛ amralo lɛ teŋ lɛ kpo.
Kwɛmɔ shitiimɔ henɔ lɛ.
Kaselɔi afee nifeemɔ lɛ ekoŋŋ ni amɛkɛwie naagba ni tsɔɔlɔ lɛ ha lɛ he loo aha kaselɔi ni jwɛŋmɔ mli kwɔ lɛ ahiɛ sanegbaa lɛ hiɛ.
Shii abɔ ni akɛ jemawɔji agbɛi woɔ abifabii lɛ nakai nɔŋŋ anaa amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi kɛ yibaamɔi lɛ.
Nikasemɔ Otii: Afoata sane henyamɔ (yitso, oti he ni awieɔ, wiemɔ halamɔ, niŋmaa mli ŋaagbɛi, k.n.).
Otsi 8 kaa lɛ damɔɔ DoK ŋɛlɛ 4 nɔ ni akpaa gbɛ akɛ kaselɔi ajie yiŋkaa hesalɛ kɛ naagba naamɔmɔ hesalɛ kpo kɛ nu naagbai anaa.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ WOJIAŊ SAJI 1.
Otii ni fiɔ sɛɛ
Enɛ haa sanegbaa kɛ jwɛŋmɔtsɔɔmɔ yɛ sanegbalɔi lɛ ateŋ lɛ yaa nɔ yɔɔ ni eyeɔ emuu hu.
Amɛkpe kɛmiiye maŋtsɛ lɛ he odase.
Kwɛmɔ lalawiemɔ lɛ jebɔ: Nibii komɛi ni ahe hia yɛ lalawiemɔ lɛ jebɔ lɛ mli ji:
Hejuu yɛ ŋshɔ loo faa mli
Nomɛi ji vaoli gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ kɛ kɔnsonanti gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ.
Tsɛsɛɛ haa abonoai kɛ sawie.
Helɔ/nɔnalɔ tuuntu kɛ helɔ/
Ha henɔi nɛɛ eko fɛɛ eko he nɔkwɛmɔnii ni opɛi mli.
Kaselɔi asusu nɔ ni amɛna yɛ vidio nɛɛ mli lɛ ahe ni amɛkɛto amɛnɔ lɛ he koni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni yeɔ he gbɔ kɛ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ mli hu.
Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Eyipa loo sikasa Kɛji mɔ ko gbo lɛ, gbii nyɔŋmai ejwɛ sɛɛ dani afeɔ eyara.
Gbalamɔ henɔi lɛ amli koni oha nɔkwɛmɔ nibii.
Wiemɔ gbɛi anɔ ni kɛnyaa lalawiemɔ he.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshwiemɔ
Susumɔ feemɔwiemɔ he otsɔɔ.
Mlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ SaneshishitsɔɔmɔMlijaa 6: Nikanemɔ Kɛ Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ Gbɛkpamɔ DoK Ŋɛlɛ Kaa ŋaagbɛ Akɛ nikanemɔ hiɛhiɛ kɛ nikanemɔ vii he nilee lɛ ahara otii pɔtɛɛ ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko ni abaado nɔ mli 2 Practice paper Awie saneshishitsɔɔmɔ henɔi lɛ ahe (N.k., wiemɔkulibii, shishinumɔ saneshishitsɔɔ, kn.
Yɛ Ghana lɛ kpokpa wuji loo wiemɔ fɛɛ wiemɔ yɛ ejemawɔji ni eheɔ amɛɛ eyeɔ.
Nya adesa ko he (yitso lɛ, otii, wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsi nii, wiemɔheŋɔɔlɔwolɔi, k.n.k.n)
Ku nitsumɔ Yɛ kui ni hiɛ hewalɛi srɔtoi
Mɛni ji gbaa saneŋmaa?
Aje shishi shwane aafee ŋmɛlɛ akpaa gbɛkɛ ŋmɛji ekpaa pɛpɛɛpɛ.
Gbi ko ni amɛtee amɛdaadaa juu ni amɛba lɛ, Polisifoi lɛ yati amɛnɔ be mli ni amɛjaa nibii ni amɛyaju lɛ.
Nyɛwiea lalawiemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe
Mlai Mlai ni kulaa feemɔwiemɔi kɛ sanemuu feemɔ lɛ ekomɛi nɛ:
Kaata stoovi lɛ he.
Awo tsɔɔlɔ lɛ hewalɛ ni ekɛ nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi, yelikɛbuamɔ nibii kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛ srɔtoi atsu nii kɛye ebua kaselɔi.
Eyɛ mli akɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ bɛ kpɛŋŋ moŋ nɛ shi kɛji akwɛɛɛ ni ahi ni akɛ henɔ nɛɛ tsu nii fe nine lɛ, shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ eyafeŋ faŋŋ loo oti komɛi baalaje yɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Esa akɛ edamɔ otsi 1 kɛyashi otsi 5 nɔ.
Mɛni ji lalawiemɔ
Ayeɔ ŋkpai lɛ hu kɛbaa loo babaoo, ŋmɔshi nibii babaoo kɛ shweremɔ hu.
NITSƆƆMƆ KƐ NIKASEMƆ ŊAAGBƐI LƐ ANƆDOOMƆ Nitsɔɔmɔ he ŋaa ni akɛtsu nii yɛ mlijaa nɛɛ mli wieɔ ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi ni akɛtsɔɔ shikwɛɛ wiemɔi lɛ he.
Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛɛ
Amɛhe baakpɔ yɛ niŋmaa, wiemɔ, nikanemɔ kɛ yiŋkaa mli.
Gbalamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni okɛgbalaa susumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi amli lɛ.
Anɔkwayeli: Kaatsake shishinumɔ, jebɔ loo gbee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ/ niŋmaa momo lɛ mli kɛji ootsɔɔ shishi kɛmiiya wiemɔ kroko lɛ mli.
Yɛ niŋmaa / wiemɔ momo lɛ anɔkwa.
Bakatue Afiyeli: Akɛkadiɔ wuoyaa beiaŋ, Elminabii ni yɔɔ Teŋ Kpokpaa lɛ nɔ bii kɛ lɛlɛ kudɔmɔ kɛ shikwɛɛ jooshwɛmɔ tsɔɔ naa.
Afɔlɔnɔ: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfoɔ wiemɔkul nɔ kuku kɛnaa wiemɔkuli hee.
Yɛ kusumtsumɔ lɛ mli
Fɛɛ sɛɛ lɛ ana akɛ mla lɛ tsu nii kɛ.
sisa Yɛ nɔkwɛmɔnɔ kome lɛ mli lɛ, /a/ eje wiemɔkuli lɛ shishi.
Miishɛɛnamɔ Mɔ floŋŋ kpeemɔ he sɛɛnamɔi
(Tsɔɔlɔ agbala enɛ mli yɛ amɛ kusum naa) Gbele Henɔi: Nyɔŋmɔjeŋ Gbele Kɛ Kpatu Gbele
Ha nɔkwɛmɔnii ni jeɔ jebɔi nɛɛ akpo Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Nitsumɔ Sanenaataomɔ he Nikasemɔ (Problem-Based Learning) - (ankroankro kɛ kuu)
Tsakpaa baa sanemuji lɛ ateŋ
Gbalamɔ gbɛjianɔtoo ni yaa nɔ yɛ gbalamlibotemɔ mli kɛ esɛɛnamɔi ahe.
Weku ni agbo yɛ amɛnɔ lɛ taa hiɛ.
Wɔkɛ kpokpoi kɛ palm nut soup yeɔ afahyɛ lɛ be mli.
Yitso: Mlijaramɔi yɛ Mligbalamɔi amli.
Week 4 Nikasemɔ lɛ Otii: Okɛ nikanemɔ vii kɛ hiɛhiɛ nikanemɔ nikasemɔ ni ona lɛakane nii kɛye abua jeŋshihilɛ mli haomɔi anaamɔmɔ Yitso: Nikanemɔ vii Nikanemɔ Vii Nikanemɔ vii ji nikanemɔ oyayai koni ona adafitswaa ni akɛba lɛ.
Nɛkɛ ŋaagbɛi nɛɛ baanyɛ eha kaselɔi ayiŋkaa aya hiɛ, amɛhe asa yɛ naagbai anaamuu mli, ni amɛna hesalɛ yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Srɔtoi yɛ mlai ni kulaa sanemuu feemɔ kɛ gbɛiwiemɔi mli.
Akɛ tsɛnsi bibioo ko ni maa shi kɛha nikee.
Mligbalalɔi Mligbalalɔi ji wiemɔ ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni afee nɔ ko aha, bɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ eha aloo be ni nɔ ko tee nɔ.
Okɛ nikanemɔ oyayaayai gbɛjianɔtoo lɛ atsu kɛtao adafitswaa gbajaa ni yɔɔ niŋmaa lɛ mli
Yarafeemɔ Kusum Yɛ Ghana Maji Lɛ Ekomɛi Anɔ Note Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ akwɛ yarafeemɔ kusum yɛ Ghana maji lɛ ekomɛi anɔ he niŋmaa lɛ yɛ baafa 56-58 yɛ nikaselɔ gbɛtsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Wiemɔi ni yɔɔ lalai lɛ amli lɛ woɔ nitsulɔi ahewalɛ ni amɛbɔɔ nitsumɔ lɛ he mɔdɛŋ.
Sanemuu oti ji sane ni jieɔ yiŋtoo oti ni yɔɔ mlijaa lɛ mli lɛ kpo.
Adiki kaselɔi aniŋmaai lɛ ni akɛ nɔ ni amɛna lɛ awo SAP lɛ mli.
Okɛ feemɔwiemɔi kɛ mligbalalɔi ni oha lɛ afee wiemɔmuji.
Sanefa Eji wiemɔkulibii enyɔ loo etɛ ni hiɛ feelɔ kɛ helɔ.
Ha shishinumɔ ni jɛ shikwɛɛ wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ ahi shi, ni ekatsake .
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ omashi okadi lɛ muɔ nɛkɛ sanemuu henɔ nɛɛ naa.
Shigbɛlɛmɔi krokomɛi ni atsɔ hiɛ afee yɛ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ
Mumɔi/ Ayɛkpɛmɔ: Ayeɔ aheɔ akɛ wɔji nyɛɔ amɛgbeɔ mɛi kɛji amɛtɔ amɛ nɔ loo kɛji amɛkɛ shade fɔŋ ba maŋ mli kɛji amɛtɔ mla nɔ.
No hewɔ lɛ, ayɛ gugɔjiaŋ gbɛɛmɔ henɔi enyɔ.
Srɔtoi ni yɔɔ Afii ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ: (Biɛ lɛ, esa akɛ kaselɔi akase maji krokomɛi anɔ afii ni amɛtsɔɔ srɔtoi ni yɔɔ amɛteŋ) Nɔkwɛmɔnii nɛ.
Nifeemɔ nɛɛ baanyɛ eha hesalɛ yɛ yiŋkaa, saji anaataomɔ
Bei pii lɛ, akɛ gbɛɛmɔ lɛ hɔlɔɔ wiemɔkulibii amli bɔ ni afee ni ehɔlɔ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔi ni tsara nɔ lɛ koni anyɛ atsɛ.
Okɛ nilee ni ona lɛ akwɛ mfoniri lɛ koni okɛ mɔ ni ta omasɛi lɛ agba nɔ ni ona lɛ he sane
Yɛ nakai gbɛ nɔŋŋ nɔ lɛ, wɔbaanyɛ wɔna wiemɔkulibii pɔtɛɛ ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ mlawoohe, adafitswaa, sukuu, kɛ nitsumɔhei krokomɛi hu amli.
Bei pii lɛ yɛ nanemɛi, wekumɛi, intanɛti lɛ mɛi anaanyobɔɔ mli.
Mlijaa lɛ hu haa kaselɔi anilee ni mli kwɔ kɛ shishinumɔ yɛ amɛ diɛŋtsɛ amɛkusum kɛ ŋaa naa.
Ebɔɔɔ mɔdɛŋ kwraa yɛ nitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli ni tsɔɔ akɛ ehe esako kwraa yɛ wiemɔ mli.
Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ afee ekome kɛgbala sanekuku kɛ sanefa kɛ amɛhenɔi lɛ amli.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa
Kɛji eji hiɛdɔɔ nɔ, tipɛŋfoi loo nɔ ni haa mɔ ko tsakeɔ ejwɛŋmɔ.
Kaselɔi babaoo kɛ intanɛti lɛ tsuɔ nii daa gbi.
Enɛ he hia yɛ lalawiemɔ mli ejaakɛ ehaa liamɔi lɛ tsaa nɔ ni lalawiemɔ lɛ hu yaa lɛ oyaoya.
Bɔ ni akɛbaatsu nii kɛ esɛɛ saji II.
Daamɔ nikasemɔ ni ona lɛ nɔ ko ni okane Afoata sane ko ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii ohai enumɔ.
Ekome enyɛɔ edamɔɔ shi kɛjieɔ shishinumɔ ko kpo.
LI.2 Gbalamɔ fɔnɔlɔji gbɛjianɔtooi ni yɔɔ Ga mli.
Mɔ sɛɛgbɛtsɔɔlɔi, yifalɛ kɛ kadilɔi) kɛtsɔɔ Yitso: Sutsɔɔlɔi Henɔi Sutsɔɔlɔi Sutsɔɔlɔ ji wiemɔ ni tsɔɔ bɔ ni mɔ ko, nɔ ko loo he ko yɔɔ ha loo eji wiemɔ ni gbalaa gbɛiwiemɔ loo najiaŋgbɛi ko najiaŋ.
Kɔ yitso ni osumɔɔ ni odamɔ gbaa saneŋmaa jebɔi lɛ nɔ kɛŋma.
Ehe hiaa akɛ obaana nɔ hewɔ ni obaakane nii hiɛhiɛ.
Srɔtoi yɛ nibii ni akɛtsuɔ kusumii nɛɛ amli
Gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ waa kɛgbalaa gbɛjianɔtooi ni kudɔɔ wiemɔ, kɛ tsakemɔi ni eba wiemɔi amli yɛ bei pɔtɛɛ ko mli.
Kadimɔ kaselɔi awoji oya ni otswa mii lɛ owo SAP lɛ nɔ.
Jaramɔ feemɔwiemɔi ni ona loo oyɔse lɛ owo feemɔwiemɔ ni ŋɔɔ nɔnalɔ kɛ nɔ ni eŋɔɔɔ nɔnalɔ mli.
Gbalamɔ henɔi ni ona lɛ mli otsɔɔ.
(nk., hiŋmɛi loo wao ni okɛbaanyiɛ sane ko nɔ oyayai, kɛtao wiemɔkulibii loo sanekuku, kn.)
Wɔkɛ nilee ni wɔna lɛ tsu nii yɛ kukuji lɛ amli kɛna sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ.
Aakpa tsɔɔlɔi agbɛ akɛ amɛkɛ nikasemɔ henɔ ni haa kaselɔi taoɔ saji anaa, kɛ klasi muu fɛɛ nitsumɔ atsɔɔ yitso nɛɛ.
Gbalamɔ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli gbɛjianɔtooi etɛ ni yɔɔ Ga mli ni oha nɔkwɛmɔnii etɛtɛɛtɛ yɛ eko fɛɛ eko shishi.
Daamɔ nɔ ni okase lɛ nɔ, ni okɛtsɔɔ osusumɔi kɛ yiŋshwiemɔi yɛ yitsei ni yɔɔ shishi lɛ he “Eji gbekɛbii yei/yei agbɛnaa akɛ amɛhoo nii be fɛɛ be amɛha weku lɛ ” Ŋɛlɛ 3 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Jwɛŋmɔ krɛdɛɛ
Akɛ mɔ ko nii miikwɛ kadaaŋgbamɔ yɛ osukuu mli lɛ.
Afoata he nikasemɔ lɛ nɔdoomɔ: Shwɛlɔi, oti he ni awieɔ, naatoo, he ni sane lɛ ba yɛ, mɔ susumɔ/ jwɛŋmɔ, henumɔ.
Okɛ susumɔ oti he nilee lɛ aŋma nii kɛtsa nitsumɔ ni ho lɛ nɔ.
Lalai yɛ lalawiemɔ sui lɛ eko.
Atsɔɔ akɛ amɛfata ojotswalɔi akuu ni yɔɔ kpokpaa lɛ nɔ lɛ he ni amɛjuɔ mɛi daa jenamɔ mli.
Yɛ kaselɔi ateŋ lɛ, mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli haa amɛtoiboo yaa hiɛ kɛtsɔ kaselɔi
Tsɔɔmɔ shikwɛɛ saneyli jebɔ lɛ he (tsɛ, weku nukpa, weku tɔkpaa yitso)
Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ aŋmaa Nɔkofeemɔ sane Akɔ kɛjɛ he ko loo adoo nɔ kɛjɛ sanebimɔ ko mli Nɔkofeemɔ:Saneŋmaa lɛ hiɛgbelemɔ Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ, subaŋ kpakpai( cultural values) (mɔbulɛ, mlijɔlɛ, shishinumɔ , ŋmɛ nɔ ko he gbɛ kɛ ekrokomɛi), tsɔsemɔ, gbekɛbii anyɔŋyeli, wɔhewɔŋ, nibii ahiɛtsakemɔ kɛ feemɔ yɛ Ghana, su mli jwɛtri, kɛ GESI kn.
Kɔ emli etɛ yɛ fɛɛ eko shishi koni okɛfee wiemɔmuu nta.
Kaselɔi baakase oti nɛɛ kɛjɛ Ghana shikwɛɛ kusum srɔtoi lɛ amli ni amɛkɛ nilee ni amɛbaana lɛ baawie bɔ ni kui srɔtoi lɛ yeɔ amɛfii lɛ amɛhaa.
Mlijaa 9: Lalai OTSI 20 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie shwɛmɔ lalai ahe OTII AHE NI ASUSUƆ LƐ: SHWƐMƆ LALAI Shwɛmɔ Lalai Shwɛmɔ lalai lɛ alaa yɛ shwɛmɔ be mli.
Nɛkɛ mlijaa nɛɛ hɛe yɛ sɛɛnamɔi kɛha kaselɔi jeee yɛ Ghana wiemɔ kasemɔ mli pɔtɛɛ shi ekɛ tsakpaa hu baa lala kɛ jeŋnilee kasemɔ.
Nɔ hewɔ ji akɛ, akutsoŋ nifeemɔi ni kwɛɔ be, bɔ ni wiemɔ lɛ yaa lɛ yɛ lɛ kudɔɔ sanegbaa lɛ.
Kɛjɛ gbekɛbiashi kɛyaboteɔ amɛnukpatsɔmɔ mli yɛ gbɛiwoo kusum sɛɛ.
Mumɔŋ naagbai: Kpatu gbele haa mɛi jeɔ amɛhemɔkɛyelii loo Nyɔŋmɔ he ŋwane.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli Nikasemɔ Kɛ Nitsɔɔmɔ He Ŋai
Kusum nifeemɔ nɛɛ, esoro bɔ ni maŋ fɛɛ maŋ feɔ enɔ ehaa kɛ mlai ni yɔɔ he hu.
Nyɛsusua nilee ni yɔɔ wiemɔ naajiemɔ mli.
Kɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ he mlai lɛ atsu nii ni otsɔɔ niŋmaa nɛɛ shishi kɛba owiemɔ lɛ mli.
Mligbalalɔi: Mligbalalɔi ji wiemɔkulibii ni wieɔ feelɔ, feemɔwiemɔ loo helɔ lɛ he.
Nya shwɛmɔ ko he.
Esa akɛ eje kpo yɛ kuku lɛ mli klɛŋklɛŋ wiemɔ lɛ mli loo ebɛŋkɛ.
Ajieɔ shishi maŋkɛ, akaiɔ hiimeji (Nananom) kɛ maŋbii fɛɛ ni amɛgboi yɛ afi ni eho lɛ mli yɛ shia fɛɛ shia ni yɔɔ Akropɔŋ lɛ mli.
Tsɔɔlɔ lɛ baanyɛ kaselɔi lɛ ni ekadi bɔ ni wiemɔkulibii lɛ yaa lɛ lɛ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Ehaa ankroankro nilee loo jwɛŋmɔ ni he hiaa kɛha gbɛhe hee mli ni eyabote lɛ kuramɔ.
Shikwɛɛ lalai kɛ joo: Ashwɛɔ joi srɔtoi kɛjieɔ Ga kusum lɛ kpo kɛtsɔɔ je lɛ.
Fosu mukpo lɛ nɔkwɛlɔ (Emissafo) ni yɔɔ Oguaa lɛ yeɔ ŋkpai yɛ kpaakpo lɛ naa ni ebaatsɛ mumɔi lɛ koni amɛshwie mumɔi fɔji kɛ oshrai ni baanina gbɔi ni baa afi lɛ kɛya sɛɛ flɛŋŋ.
No hewɔ lɛ, wɔkɛɔ akɛ /b/ nyɛɔ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ.
Nikasemɔ mli Nitsumɔ
Akrowaŋbii lɛ tsɔ enɛ nɔ kɛwo lɛ maŋtsɛ kɛtsɔɔ amɛhiɛsɔlɛ.
Vaoli gbɛɛmɔi: Eji vaoli gbɛɛmɔ henɔ kome ni akɛje wiemɔkulibii ashishi ni tsara amɛhe nɔ yɛ liamɔ.
Wiemɔkulibii ni ama: Eji gbɛ nɔ ni amaa wiemɔkulibii kɛjɛɔ wiemɔ krokomɛi amli kɛbaa Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli.
Lilɛi lɛ nɔwomɔ: Enɛ tsɔɔ he ni lilɛi lɛ kaa yɛ vaoli gbɛɛmɔfeemɔ mli.
Afeɔ yitsɔi srɔtoi ahaa mɛi nɛɛ
Titri lɛ, najiaŋgbɛi damɔɔ shi akɛ: Najiaŋgbɛi ni yeɔ gbɛiwiemɔ najiaŋ, no hewɔ atsɛɔ lɛ najiaŋ gbɛi lɛ
Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ kɛ amɛ agba he sane koni amɛkadi mlijaramɔ otii ni yɔɔ mli Nɔkwɛlɔ lɛ akpa nibii ni baa nɛɛ agbɛ:
Eji be mli ni Africa Anai maŋ nɛɛ miibɔ ehe mɔdɛŋ kɛje shika haomɔi ni eyɔɔ mli ni asusu akɛ asraafoi amaŋkuramɔ kɛba, ni egu shika he ŋaatsɔɔ nitsumɔi fɛɛ lɛ.
Afamɔɔ mɛi ejwɛ (hii sɔŋŋ) kɛjɛɔ tsɛsɛɛ weku mli.
Wiemɔi ni yɔɔ lalai amli hiɛ shishinumɔi ni mli kwɔ ni amaa amɛ nɔ mi.
Mɛi ni na hegbɛ lɛ na tsɔsemɔ yɛ bɔ ni atsɔɔ ŋoo nu kɛyaa nuŋma mli, nitsumɔi srɔtoi yɛ hei ni nu yɔɔ, nidumɔ sui kɛ nidumɔi ŋaa srɔtoi, Biogas, Integrated biorefinery (mlɛ namɔ, mu kpakpa namɔ, black soldier fly technology kɛ nii kɛ nii) SLECI irrigation system kɛ Biochar
Jarayeli: Bianɛ lɛ mɛi tsɔɔ yarafeemɔ nɔ kɛfeɔ shika.
Gbee mli gbeleɔ
Wiemɔi ni akɛtsɔɔ jwɛŋmɔ
Kɛha mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) atsu nii lɛ, feemɔ nibii nɛɛ:
Ha ohiɛ aka Nifeemɔ lɛ nɔ Wiemɔ nibii nɛɛ amli:
Tamɔ gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ lɛ, gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔmɔ yɛ hei srɔtoi yɛ wiemɔkulibii amli.
Mlijaa 4 Mlikwɛmɔ Mlijaa nɛɛ kwɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi amli.
Nifeemɔi: Kusumi, mii ayii, joo, lalai kɛ hiimeji kɛ wowɛi lɛ anihamɔ
Kɛ nifeemɔ nɛɛ awo shwɛmɔ mli.
Ayeɔ ŋkpai kɛtsɔɔ nɔ kɛbiɔ sisa lɛ gbɛ bɔ ni afee ni eŋmɛ gbɛ kɛha enibii amlikwɛmɔ.
Esa akɛ atoɔ nifeemɔ bei lɛ anaa taakɛ eba lɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ, ni sane lɛ amɔ toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ be ni eyaa hiɛ lɛ.Wielɔ lɛ wiemɔi lɛ asha jwɛŋmɔŋ mfoniri yɛ toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ mli, ni enyɛ ekɛ fɔŋwiemɔi atsu nii kɛgbala toibolɔi ajwɛŋmɔ kɛba sane lɛ nɔ Gbaa saneŋmaa nɔkwɛmɔnɔi:
Klasi muu lɛ
Enyɛɔ ebaa lɛ hu akɛ, shitiimɔ bɛ ekomɛi amli.
Ehaa niŋmaa lɛ feɔ faŋŋ
Gbɛhegbɛi sanekuku: Gbɛhegbɛi ji wiemɔ oti ni yɔɔ mli ni egbalaa gbɛiwiemɔ loo feemɔwiemɔ mli.
Wiemɔi lɛ bɛ faŋŋ tsɔ ni wiemɔi lɛ ekomɛi ahe ehiaa yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli.
Ŋa shinaa lɛ.
Kwaku hiɛ ka shi fe enyɛminuu lɛ.
Akɛwoɔ lɛ tsofa hu.
Nitsumɔ Oti Ŋɛlɛ 1 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshwiemɔ
Wiemɔ nɔ ni mɛi krokomɛi wie lɛ he.
Tsɔɔmɔ afiyeli gbɛnaa yɛ maŋ nɔyaa kɛ akɔntaabuu mli.
Ehaa anyaa mɛi awalashihilɛ saji ahe titri lɛ nibii ni mɔ ni etsɔ esɛɛ lɛ bafee.
Nɔ ni kɔɔ falefale he, maŋ nɛɛ kɛ maŋsɛɛ nɔ, kn.).
Eyɛ mli akɛ saneshishitsɔɔmɔ bɛ kpɛŋŋ moŋ nɛ, shi kɛji akwɛɛɛ ni ahi ni akɛ henɔ nɛɛ tsu nii fe nine lɛ, shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ eyafeŋ faŋŋ loo oti komɛi baalaje yɛ shishitsɔɔmɔ lɛ mli.
Ewaa kɛbuaa gbɛiwoo kusum nifeemɔ lɛ naa ni elaajeee ejaakɛ akɛmiitsu nii be fɛɛ be.
Tsɔnesaalɔ kɛ mɔ ni tsɔne efite lɛ sanegbaa mli

Mlijaa 8: Gbele Kɛ Yarafeemɔ Gbonyo Hejuu Ayeɔ ŋkpai kɛjeɔ gbonyo lɛ hejuu shishi.
Akwɛ ni abo shwɛmɔ lɛ toi ni ahara wiemɔkulibii ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli lɛ.

Saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni damɔ shishinumɔ nɔ
Gbalamɔ oti wuji ni yɔɔ sanegbaa mli.
Mɔ ni yeɔ ŋkpai lɛ etsi emama lɛ kɛba shi.
Ahaa ŋɛlɛ etɛ yiŋkaa he sanebimɔi (level 3 strategic reasoning) kɛtsɔ niŋmaa loo saji amli nikasemɔ kɛwkɛ kaselɔi lɛ asusumɔi kɛ amɛniiashishinumɔ mlikwɔlɛ yɛ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa lɛ mli (turn taking) kɛ bɔ ni amɛkɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa gbaa ŋmɛnɛŋmɛnɛ kɛ jeŋ maji saji ahe sane lɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔ nibii ni kɔɔ adesa he (nk.
Ewie lalawiemɔ mli otii kɛ ehesɛɛnamɔi ahe hu, saneshishitsɔɔmɔ kɛ ehe saji krokomɛi ahe.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi leɔ kaselɔi ni yɔɔ nitsɛmɔ kɛ wiemɔi amli naagbai koni amɛtsɔɔ ŋaagbɛi anɔ kɛku susumɔi ni ejaaa fɛɛ anaa yɛ klasi lɛ mli.
Esa akɛ tsɔɔlɔi kɛ kaa ŋaagbɛi srɔtoi atsu nii koni amɛkɛna kaselɔi amɔdɛŋbɔɔ, amɛmii kɛ hetoo kɛha amɛnikasemɔ muu lɛ nɔyaa.
Jaramɔ gbɛiwiemɔi amli owo amɛhenɔi lɛ ashishi (kwamaŋgbɛi, kɔsuiaŋgbɛi, kɔɔyɔɔŋ gbɛi, gbɛi kushao kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he.
Yɛ efeemɔ mli lɛ sɛŋ kpaa lɛ kpokpoɔ.
Ekwɛɔ ekpokpa lɛ nɔ
Sanemuji nɛɛ eko fɛɛ eko wieɔ sanemuu oti lɛ he ni ehaa kanelɔi lɛ nɔ fɛɛ nɔ ni baaye ebua amɛ kɛnu shɛɛwiemɔ lɛ shishi.
Shigbɛlɛlmɔ trilii hehiaa jogbaŋŋ yɛ otii kɛ saji ni ahe hiaa lɛ akpojiemɔ mli.
Na hemɔkɛyeli akɛ anɔkwale ni awieɔ lɛ
Ŋɛlɛ 2 Asɛsimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Bei pii lɛ, gbekɛ ni eye afii ejwɛ naa tseɔ yɛ emaŋwiemɔ mli ni enuɔ shishi wiemɔ lɛ shishi ni ekɛtsuɔ nii.
Akɛ nitsumɔ lalai jieɔ nitsulɔi ayi koni egbala mɛi krokomɛi hu.
Kɛji eŋma otii lɛ mli enyɔ lɛ - mii 2
Namɔ ji maŋtsɛ?
Tsɛrɛmɔ wiemɔkulibii ni abaanyɛ ayɔse vaoli kome ni jeɔ shishi, baa yɛ teŋ ni asaŋ ebaa yɛ wiemɔkulibii lɛ naagbee.
Hulu lɛ nyɔɔ shi yɛ anai.
Anaa gbɛjianɔtoo nɛɛ kɛji hiɛ vaoli /i, e, ɛ/ nyiɛ kɔnsonanti gbɛɛmɔ ko sɛɛ kpaakpa.
Tsɔɔmɔ sanemuu henɔ (kɛɛmɔ, sanebimɔ kɛ famɔ) ni akɛtsu nii yɛ sanemuji nɛɛ amli lɛ:
Yifalɛ ji sutsɔɔlɔi loo kadilɔi ni tsɔɔ gbɛiwiemɔ yifalɛ.
Kaa yeɔ ebuaa ni anaa kaselɔi anishishinumɔ shidaamɔ yɛ shikwɛɛ afiyeli yɛ Ghana nikasemɔ lɛ mli.
Gbɛjianɔtoo: Gbɛɛmɔ mlihɔlɔ kɛ eko fataa he shi gbɛɛmɔ laajemɔ jeɔ eko yɛ mli.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni sanemɔuu oti walɔi lɛ yeɔ buaa susumɔ oti ni yɔɔ niŋmaa ko mli lɛ.
Kaselɔi baakase nɔ ni gbele ji, nɔ ni kɛbaa, ehenɔi, nɔnyɛɛ kɛ gbɛi anɔ ni abaanyɛ atsɔ kɛku gbele naa.
Oti: Koni ajura maŋ lɛ kɛ akutsei lɛ, ni anya ekomefeemɔ kɛ hewalɛ ni maŋ lɛ yɔɔ lɛ he.
Gbalamɔ sanemuu he sui lɛ amli.
Esa akɛ kaselɔi agba wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtoo henɔi lɛ ahe sane ni amɛha nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ Ga mli.
Ayaa ŋshɔnaa ni atsuɔ nɛkɛ kusum nɛɛ.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli OTSI 16 Bɔ ni abaadiki sane ko mlikwɛmɔ: Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli I.
Ahara lalawiemɔ mli otii ni yɔɔ lalawiemɔ ni akane lɛ mli
Kpaa niŋmaa lɛ mli ni otao (i) mɔ ni to nitsumɔ lɛ shishi (ii) maji krokomɛi ni naa nitsumɔ lɛ he sɛɛ (ii) okwaafoi abɔ ni na he sɛɛ.
Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ etɛ - mii 3 Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ enyɔ - mii 2 Nibii ni yɔɔ ŋwɛi lɛ ateŋ ekome - mi 1
Wiemɔkulibii ni atsɛrɛɛɛ lɛ jogbaŋŋ lɛ ekomɛi ji kɔshikɔshi/ shikɔshikɔ, tokota/kotoka, lajeko/lakoje.
Nikanemɔ vii ji nifeemɔ ni akɛkaneɔ nii oyayaayai kɛkadi susumɔ otii ni yɔɔ sane lɛ mli.
Etsɔɔ nɔ ni feelɔ lɛ feɔ: Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Okpɔŋɔ fioo lɛ joɔ foi fe ekrokomɛi lɛ fɛɛ.
Baawie ohetoo lɛ he.
Ehaa mɛi nyaa amɛŋaa ahe yɛ mumɔi kɛ kusum gbɛfaŋ.
Naatoo lɛ tsuɔ naagbai srɔtoi lɛ ahe nii, awuŋayeli kɛ mataŋi srɔtoi.
Etsɔɔ tsakpaa ni kaa mɔ ni egbo lɛ wala beiaŋ kɛ be ni egbo nɛɛ.
Wiemɔ otii ni yɔɔ vidio lɛ mli.
Kanemɔ oniŋmaa lɛ mli ekoŋŋ ni osaa emli tɔmɔi fɛɛ.
Agbɛna ogbɛnaa ji owa ni asaa shia lɛ tamɔ tsu mli bɛɛmɔ, nibii ahetsuumɔ kɛ agbo naa saamɔ.
Apɛi wiemɔkulibii ni aha amɛ lɛ mli ni amɛtsɔɔ gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli henɔ ni akɛtsu nii lɛ (tsɔɔlɔ aha nɔkwɛmɔnii komɛi yɛ Ga mli)
Efɔɔ kaa akɛ amɛhiɛ subaŋ kome, yiŋtoo kome kɛ gbɛkpamɔ kome ni buaa amɛnaa.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Tsɔɔmɔ nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he.
Susumɔ vaolii gbɛɛmɔi ni baa nɛɛ otsɔɔ a.
He ni Ŋ malɔ lɛ jɛ: Enɛ tsɔɔ nibii ni ŋmalɔ lɛ etsɔ mli, enitsumɔ kɛ esubaŋ.
Adaawe ji yei ashwɛmɔi lɛ eko ni shwɛmɔ nɛɛ mli lamɔ fa naakpa.
Eji wekui ni hiɛ tsakpaa kome, amɛjɛ blematsɛ kome mli, ni amɛhiɛ hiɛnyiɛlɔ kome ni amɛkɛ woo haa.
Kuku nɛɛ baatsɔɔ otii komɛi yɛ afii komɛi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ.
Kusum nifeemɔi eyi Odwira afiyeli lɛ mli tɔ, ŋaa, lalai, hesaamɔ kɛ niyenii.
Sanenaataomɔ Nikasemɔ: yɛ ŋaagbɛ nɛɛ mli lɛ, akɛ jeŋshihilɛ mli saji toɔ nikasemɔ lɛ he ni no haa kaselɔi nuɔ nibii ashishi faŋŋ fe nitsɔɔmɔ ŋaagbɛ ni awieɔ nibii ahe tamɔ bɔ ni amɛji lɛ.
Enɔ jetseremɔ Jufɔ hu lɛ, afeɔ nibii pii.
Mlijaa 7: AfiyeliMlijaa 7: Afiyeli Odwira gbi Shɔ Gbi nɛɛ nɔ lɛ, Akropɔŋ maŋ muu lɛ fɛɛ yeɔ awerɛho.
Mlijaa 4: Wiemɔkulibii Asɔɔ Gbɛjianɔtooi OTSI 8 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Kɛ wiemɔkulibii asɔɔ gbɛjianɔtooi nɛɛ asɔ wiemɔkulibii hee Kaimɔ Afi 2 Otsi 5: Akwɛ afisi nitsumɔ yɛ Ga mli lɛ amli.
Lalawiemɔ mli otii
Ayeɔ afii kɛkadiɔ nifeemɔi, loo akɛgbaa kusum he otii komɛi kɛtsɔ agwaseiaŋ wiemɔi, joi kɛ lalai amli.
Shigbɛlɛmɔ he ŋaagbɛi lɛ nɔmimaa (kɛyashi 5)
Gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli shishinumɔ yɛ Ga wiemɔ lɛ mli lɛ he sɛɛnamɔ
Afeɔ enɛ dani gbi klɛŋklɛŋ ni yɔɔ Gbo lɛ shɛɔ.
Nibii komɛi ni akɛyeɔ ŋkpai (Source: jumia.com.gh/general) Kaa oti Kaselɔi akwɛ bɔ ni ayeɔ ŋkpai ahaa kɛŋmala nibii nɛɛ agbɛi lɛ.
Ejaakɛ ebɛ hewalɛ (ehiaa sanefa oti dani ebaaye emuu) Sanefafai ahe nɔkwɛmɔnii yɛ Sanemuu mli
Nɔkofeemɔ saneŋmaa mli lɛ akɛ nɔdoomɔ feɔ naamuu.
Aha mi wolo ni miyajɔɔ mihe, No hewɔ mifoɔ mihe fioo nɛɛ;
Nikasemɔ mli sanegbaa: Klasi muu lɛ fɛɛ nifeemɔ:
Ŋmaa sane yɛ oblonya gbɛhamɔ ni ho nɔŋŋ lɛ he.
Adae Kese Afiyeli: Ashantemɛi ji mɛi ni yeɔ nɛkɛ afi nɛɛ kɛkaiɔ tsɛmɛi lɛ ni amɛtsɔɔ nɔ kɛbiɔ jɔɔmɔ kɛjɛɔ amɛdɛŋ kɛtsɔ gwabɔɔ, miyii kɛ jooshwɛmɔi amli.
Enɛ baa kɛji vaoli kokoloo tamɔ /u, o, ɔ/ loo glaadi /w/, nyiɛ kɔnsonati ko sɛɛ kpaakpa yɛ tsɛrɛmɔ mli.
Ŋaa kpojiemɔ: Yarafeemɔ ebatsɔɔ he ni mɛi jieɔ amɛŋaa kpo yɛ, mɛi ni joɔ shikwɛɛ joo, ni amɛyiɔ mii kɛ lalai srɔtoi afolɔi, mɛi ni kɛɔ lalawiemɔ kɛ mɛi ni tɛŋɔ nii tete baa shishi.
Sanemuu oti kɛ sanemuu oti walɔ yoomɔ yɛ niŋmaa mli: Daamɔ sanemuji lɛ asui anɔ kɛtsɔɔ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti kɛ nɔ ni ji sanemuu oti walɔ yɛ niŋmaa loo kuku ko mli.
Wu haa hɛlɛnii kɛ kuumɔ kpaa, tɛklɛ, adeka kɛ atade.
Maŋtsɛ lɛ tsɛmɛi ni ye bua akrowa lɛ mli bii fɛɛ kɛ niyenii babaoo.
Kuku fɛɛ kuku hiɛ sanemuu oti ko ni ewieɔ he ni no tsɛɔ kanelɔ lɛ toi sɛɛ kɛha nibii ni amɛkpaa gbɛ lɛ.
Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ Nitsumɔ: Yiŋshiewmɔ
Naatoo kɛ tsakpaa (mii 5)
/g/, /d/, /m/, /b/ ni nɔ ni haaa gbeekpaa lɛ akpokpo lɛ ekomɛi ji;
Ni mitsui shweɔ o daadaa, Bo okeme mitaoɔ yɛ teemɔ mli, Mikɛ o ato ajina nɛɛ.
Nɔkwɛmɔnɔ: Nuu lɛ joɔ foi fe mɛi ni eshwɛ lɛ.
Gbaa sane lɛ otsɔɔ.
(Nk., afi lɛ gbɛi, mɛi ni yeɔ, bɔ ni ayeɔ lɛ, kusum nifeemɔi komɛi kɛ afi lɛ he sɛɛnamɔ kn.kn.
Kɛji vaoli ko je wiemɔ ko shishi lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ shishijee Kɛji eba yɛ wiemɔkuli ko teŋ lɛ, atsɛɔ lɛ teŋ.
Nɔ ni sɛɛ asrasu lɛ…
Taomɔ vaolii ni yɔɔ wiemɔkulibii ni nɛɛ amli ni otsɔɔ amɛgbɛhe.
Ŋmaa sanemuu oti kɛha nikanemɔ ni baa nɛɛ he.
Ha sɛɛnamɔi ni yɔɔ afii srɔtoi ni ayeɔ yɛ Ghana lɛ.
Gbaa yarafeemɔ henɔi enyɔ ni afeɔ yɛ okutsoŋ lɛ he sane ni otsɔɔ sɛɛnamɔ ni yɔɔ yarafeemɔ kusumi nɛɛ ahe.

Ŋɛlɛ 3 Asɛsisimɛnti: Jwɛŋmɔ kɛya shɔŋŋ
Akwambɔ Afiyeli: Ajumako, Breman Esiam, Enyan Abaasa kɛ ekrokomɛi ni fata he lɛ ji mɛi yeɔ afi nɛɛ kɛgbaa gbɛ ni amɛkɛtsɔɔ woo ni amɛyɔɔ kɛha tsɛmɛi lɛ.
Susumɔ mɛi ayiŋtoo yɛ saji ni sa komɛi ahe kɛtsɔ sanegbaa mli.
Bei komɛi lɛ akɛ wiemɔkulibii ni sa tsuuu nii.
Mlijaa 5: SanemuuMlijaa 5: Sanemuu nɔnalɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ ninaaa lɛ tuuntu.
Abayaŋ Mama bibioo ni yaratsɛmɛi kɛfiɔ amɛnineshi (abɛku gbɛ).
Aheɔ ayeɔ akɛ kɛji afee nɛkɛ yara nɛɛ jogbaŋŋ lɛ, gbele mumɔ lɛ mii baashɛ ehe ni ebaabaŋɔ bii krokomɛi ni eshwɛ lɛ hu kɛya, Afooo pii, ayeee nii pii ni anyaaa gbonyo lɛ he hu.
Be komɛi lɛ akɛ nibii komɛi haa mɛi ni ba yara lɛ.
Amɛkase ŋaagbɛi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ sanegbaa mli kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli he hiaa yɛ sanegbaa mli.
Wɔ dekanamɔ be lɛ haa wɔjɔɔ wɔjwɛŋmɔ he ejaakɛ wɔjoɔ tɔlɛ nitsumɔ naa foi.
Nikasemɔ Mli Nitsumɔ
Nɛkɛ be nɛɛ amli lɛ, amɛkɛhereɔ gbɔi ni jɛɔ je lɛŋ he fɛɛ he kɛbaa amɛmaŋ kɛ amɛmaŋbii ni amɛfafai gbɛ kɛbaa Oguaamaŋ ekoŋŋ.
Tsɔɔmɔ susumɔ oti lɛ, sanemuu oti lɛ kɛ sanemuu oti walɔi lɛ.
Adadzewa nitsumɔi lɛ yɛ miishɛɛ waa ni ekɛ nifeemɔ babaoo fata shwɛmɔ lɛ he.
Mɛni haa shishinumɔ eyabaaa saneshishitsɔɔmɔ ni ejaaa he lɛ?
Mlijaa nɛɛ mli lɛ, kaselɔi baakase mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) ni amɛkɛtsu nii yɛ klasi lɛ mli kɛ amɛwalashihilɛi amli.
Ehaa miyeɔ hejaramɔ akɛ Akan shwɛmɔ lɛ he ehiaaa dɔŋŋ, shi eji TV nɔ nifeemɔi ni Ghanabii babaoo sumɔɔ waa yɛ nakai beiaŋ lɛ mli.
Tsofagbuu : Esa ni wɔgbulɔ ni wɔkɔlɔ tsofa ni sa, no hu yeɔ ebuaa
Kuŋbii lɛ eko fɛɛ eko wie ni eje kpo faŋŋ akɛ amɛsaa amɛhe.
OTI: KUSUM NIFEEMƆI KƐ MAŊKURAMƆ Oti Mlijaa: Kusum Nifeemɔi Nikasemɔ Gbɛkpamɔ: Akɛ nilee ni ayɔɔ yɛ gbele kɛ yarafeemɔ he lɛ atsɔɔ srɔto kɛ nibii ni haa yarafeemɔ kusumi yɛ maji srɔtoi anɔ lɛ yeɔ hegbɔ.
Ekɛ jaomɔ ni je shishi, ni ekɛ shɛɛwiemɔ ni tsa nɔ ni ekɛ wiemɔkulibiii komɛi mu naa.
Gbekɛ fɛɛ gbekɛ yɛ nitsumɔi komɛi yɛ shia, akutso mli ni eyɔɔ kɛ sukuu.
Efaa jemawɔji agbɛi ni fata gbɛi henɔi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ gbɛiwoo mli lɛ ahe ni elaajeee.
Gbalamɔ wowɔi agbɛii amli ni otsɔɔ bɔ ni amɛbabɔ ade kɛ nɔ hewɔ ni amɛba lɛ.
Enɛ haa akpaa gbɛ akɛ, nikasemɔ ni amɛyana kɛjɛ sukuu kɛtsɔ nikanemɔ kɛ ŋma mli baawa okwaa yɛ gbɛjianɔ strɔtoi ni amɛyakase ni akɛ gbɛi hei srɔtoi baatsu nii.
Yitso: Susumɔ oti taomɔ yɛ sane mli he Susumɔ oti Susumɔ oti ji hiamɔ nɔ ko ni sanetsomuu lɛ fɛɛ damɔ nɔ.
Ghana maŋ lɛ mli yɛ fɛo naakpa.
Kɛ sanegbaa asusu toiboo kɛ saneŋmaa he, kn.
Afɔɔ kɛshwieɔ gbonyo lɛ hiɛ.
Mlijaa 7: Afiyeli OTSI 15 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Awie shikwɛɛ afiyeli kɛ amɛhe sɛɛnamɔi lɛ ahe YITSO OTI NI AKWƐƆ: AFIYELI
Wɔkɛ wɔhiɛ ma lalai, nitsumɔ lalai amligbalamɔ he.
Nɔ ni kaselɔi baanyɛ amɛfee: Ajie nilee kɛ niiashishinumɔ kpo yɛ nɔdoomɔ/ kukufoo ŋmaa he.
Kɛ kaselɔi atsɔmɔ ŋaagbɛi lɛ amli.
Tsɛrɛmɔ sanemuji ni baa nɛɛ amli.
Bi nɔ hewɔ ni nifeemɔ lɛ tee nɔ: Ani onanɔ hewɔ loo agbala nɔ ko ni tee nɔ mli atsɔɔ?
Abaaha saneŋmaa he sanebimɔi ejwɛ ni esa akɛ kaselɔi aŋma sanŋmaa ni hiɛ wiemɔkulibii aaafee 300.
Abaaha kaselɔi sanebimɔi enyɔ ni amɛha hetoo.
Nilee ni amɛbaana yɛ mli lɛ baaye ebua amɛ yɛ wiemɔkulibii atsɛmɔ, nikanemɔ, kɛ wiemɔkulibii ashishinumɔ mli jogbaŋŋ.
Jebɔi nɛɛ ekomɛi ji:
Ayeɔ afi nɛɛ yɛ nikpamɔ beiaŋ be mli ni niyenii ebu ni maŋbii lɛ tsɔɔ no nɔ kɛdaa hiimeji ni etsɔmɔ amɛ sɛɛ lɛ ashi.
Ekomɛi ji wiemɔkulibii srɔtoi ni akɛtsuɔ nii yɛ nitsumɔhei srɔtoi amli lɛ.
Mɛni woɔ bo hewalɛ kɛ feɔ nakai nɔ lɛ?
Mlijaa 5: Sanemuu OTSI 10 Nikasemɔ gbɛkpamɔ: Gbalamɔ sanemuu henɔi lɛ amli Kaimɔ Afi 1 Otsi 10: Daamɔ mlai ni akɛŋmaa gbɛiwiemɔi kɛ najiaŋgbɛi lɛ anɔ kɛha sanemuji.
Wɔkase gbɛɛmɔ nɔyeli kɛ blawa shikamɔ gbɛjianɔtooi lɛ amli.
Akɛ sɛɛ afisi tsaa wiemɔkulitso lɛ sɛɛ gbɛ.
Tsɔɔmɔ gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ kɔnsonanti lɛ (nk; gbɛɛmɔi afeemɔ, hei ni gbɛɛmɔi jeɔ kpo yɛ kɛ bɔ ni afeɔ gbɛɛmɔi lɛ Yitso: Gbɛi anɔ ni atsɔɔ kɛfeɔ vaoli kɛ kɔnsonanti Voali Eji wiemɔ gbɛɛmɔi ni hoɔ yɔɔ kɛtsɔɔ daaŋ ni nɔ ko etsiii enaa.
Mɛni ji afiyeli?
Mligbalamɔ sanekuku: Egbalaa feemɔwiemɔ, sutsɔɔlɔ loo mligbalamɔ kroko mli.
Nitsumɔi Oti Ŋɛlɛ1 nɔ nitsumɔ: Ŋaalee Tswaa Ha wiemɔkulibii enyɔnyɔɔnyɔ enyɔ ni abaana vaoli gbɛɛmɔi ni jeɔ wiemɔ shihsi, baa yɛ teŋ kɛ nɔ ni gbeɔ wiemɔ naa yɛ mli.
Bɔɔlu lɛ ka okpɔlɔ lɛ shishi
Ekɛ mɛi ni esumɔɔ yeɔ be lɛ, ni eyeɔ ebe lɛ bɔ ni lɛ diɛŋtsɛ esumɔɔ.
Yɛ mɛɛ gbɛ nɔ hiɛdɔɔ sane lɛ wo oti lɛ obɔ?
Kusum tsɔɔ akɛ afii babaoo ni eho lɛ, maŋtsɛ lɛ tsɛmɛi asisai lɛ ye bua akrowa lɛ jogbaŋŋ.
Mɛi fe 3,000 ni ji okwaafoi bibii kɛ amralofoi ni yɔɔ ku ni kwɛɔ okwaayeli nɔyaa lɛ ena shika ni ana kɛjɛ Diversification Pathways yɛ Afrika kɛtsɔ Circular Agriculture Innovation (DVIA-GRI) nitsumɔ lɛ he sɛɛ.
Kaa kpitiokpitioo haa anaa kaselɔi lɛ ashidaamɔ kɛ amɛnɔyaa.
Etsɔɔ nɔ kɛnaa saji kɛtsɔ sanebimɔ nɔ.
Eyeɔ ebuaa kaselɔi hu kɛ ŋai kɛha saneshishitsɔɔmɔ emuuyeli.

Ŋɛlɛ 2 nɔ nitsumɔ: Yiŋshiewmɔ
Etamɔɔɔ nɔ ni atsɔɔ mɔ nii lɛ traa lɛ.
Afeɔ mumɔŋ kɛ heloŋ hebuamɔi.
Ekome enyɛɛɛ edamɔ shi yɛ kɛfee sanemuu.
Tsɔɔmɔ bɔ ni wiemɔkulibii lɛ aaanyɛ atsake kɛji oŋmɛ gbɛ kɛha sanebimɔi.
Afeɔ be ni mɔ lɛ ji mɔ floŋŋ
Bei pii lɛ, lalawiemɔi kɛ jebɔi srɔtoi kɛjeɔ henumɔi kɛ susumɔi akpo yɛ gbɛ srɔto ko.
Mligbalalɔi ni tsɔɔ nɔ hewɔ ni nɔ ko tee nɔ eha yɛ sanemuu mli.
Sanemuu oti walɔ mligbalamɔ: Sanemuu oti walɔ ni yɔɔ kuku ko mli lɛ maa oti ni sanemuu oti lɛ wieɔ he lɛ nɔ mi.
Nitsɔɔmɔ Kɛ Nikasemɔ Ŋaagbɛ He Kaa
Niahefɛlemɔ Ha kaselɔi nitsumɔ ni amɛfee yɛ kuui amli yɛ Otsi 3 lɛ nɔ ni oha amɛ be ni sa ni amɛkɛfee.
Ŋaagbɛi ni yɔɔ mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (turn taking) he: Mɛɛ yɛ sanegbaa mli (Turn taking) he nifeemɔ lɛ yɛ srɔto yɛ kusumii kɛ nifeemɔi amli.
